*Marco Virgolin,Harry Desmond,Ester Bergantin,Giorgio Morales,John W. Sheppard,Luigi Romano,Alessandro Lucantonio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向通用模型发现的智能体技能，并通过多个科学与工程领域的案例展示其发现新模型和改进模型的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型发现正从人工设计转向数据驱动自动化，但现有方法往往依赖特定算法或领域专用流程。论文探讨能否将模型发现方法整理成可复用的操作指南，交由通用编程智能体执行，从而降低自动发现的使用门槛并扩大适用范围。

Method: 作者开发了一个通用模型发现智能体技能，将模型发现方法封装为可供商业编程智能体使用的流程。研究通过天体物理学、心血管动力学、精准农业、交通流和车辆轮胎动力学等案例，评估该技能辅助模型发现的表现。

Result: 案例研究表明，配备该技能的商业智能体能够识别具有新颖性和科学相关性的模型。摘要未提供定量指标、基线比较或各领域的具体模型细节。

Conclusion: 将模型发现方法封装为可移植的智能体技能，可能成为一种简单、交互性强且适用范围广的替代方案，补充或替代领域专用的自动发现流程。其实际优势仍需结合更系统的定量评估加以验证。

Abstract: The search for better models, whether expressed as analytical equations or neural architectures, has increasingly shifted from manual design toward data-driven automation. While a multitude of algorithms exist, ranging from exhaustive search to AlphaEvolve, recent advances in general-purpose coding agents such as Claude Code and OpenAI Codex raise the question: could providing such systems with a "model discovery recipe" be enough to discover or improve models? We present automodel, an agent skill for general model discovery. With case studies in astrophysics, cardiovascular dynamics, precision agriculture, traffic flow, and vehicle tire dynamics, we show how commercial agents equipped with automodel can successfully identify novel and scientifically-relevant models. Our findings suggest that encoding model-discovery methodology as a portable agent skill can provide a simple, interactive, powerful and broadly applicable alternative to domain-specific automated discovery pipelines. The skill is available at: https://github.com/unlayer-ai/automodel.

</details>


### [105] [When Figures Disagree with Text: Evaluating Autonomous Visual Evidence Verification in Scientific Papers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24353)
*Guijia Zhang,Hongbo Qiu,Jiajun Zha,Guohao Cai,Hao Zheng,Huihao Jing,Kexu Cheng,Shipeng Luo,JinYang Huang,Shing-Chi Cheung,Changsheng Lu,Haoxuan Che,Harry Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究模型能否在完整论文中自主发现值得核查的图表，并判断图表与正文主张是否矛盾。结果显示，模型常能提到有问题的图，却很少准确指出矛盾；直接告知图号可显著改善诊断，但仅靠模型自行选择核查对象效果有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学图表承载论文主张的证据，因此审稿不仅要描述图表内容，还要确认图表是否支持引用它的正文主张。以往评测常直接提供图表、主张或矛盾存在的信息，未能检验模型能否在完整论文中自主找到核查对象、关联视觉元素与相关主张并识别冲突。

Method: 研究使用三十一篇完整论文，每篇均含有基准数据集中记录、但对模型隐藏的图文矛盾。六种多模态模型在中性提示下审阅论文，并通过逐项增加输入的方式比较不同条件。研究共分析一千四百四十五份开放式审阅意见，另设受控实验，分别考察告知图号、提供一致性核查指令、提供粗略视觉线索，以及仅阅读文本或阅读包含图表的页面等因素；还评估了按图表中心性排序、由模型选择核查对象的基线方法。研究者对自动评分标准进行了盲审，并将其认可率视为比较条件的指标，而非绝对准确率。

Result: 在开放式审阅中，百分之六十点六的意见提到了有缺陷的图表，但评分标准仅认可百分之三点三准确诊断出矛盾；盲审还发现该标准偏宽松。受控实验中，只告知图号、但不说明图表有问题，使认可率从百分之四点三升至百分之二十点四；一致性核查指令和粗略视觉线索则没有带来类似改善。当矛盾仅存在于渲染后的图表中时，纯文本审阅没有产生获认可的诊断；展示渲染页面并告知图号后，认可率为百分之十五点八。按中心性排序、由模型选择图表核查的基线认可率为百分之四点八。

Conclusion: 图文核查的关键难点不仅是判断给定图表与主张是否一致，更在于自主决定应核查什么。告知图号能明显提升矛盾诊断表现，但测试的图表选择规则未能有效解决对象发现问题。因此，多模态文档理解评测应检验模型能否主动提出核查对象，而不能只考查模型回答关于指定图表的问题。

Abstract: Scientific figures carry evidence for the claims that cite them, so reviewing a paper means checking that a figure agrees with the text, and not only describing what the figure shows. Benchmarks of figure-text verification typically hand the model the figure, the claim, or the knowledge that an inconsistency exists, which leaves untested whether a model can find the figure worth checking in a complete paper, relate a visual element to the claim it conflicts with, and recognize the conflict. We study this autonomous setting on 31 full manuscripts, each carrying a reviewer-documented figure-text inconsistency from PRISMM-Bench that is hidden from the model. Six multimodal models review each PDF under a neutral prompt, and we then add one input at a time to the same manuscripts. Of 1,445 open-ended reviews, 60.6% mention the flawed figure, yet a grading rubric accepts only 3.3% as diagnosing its contradiction; a blind audit finds the rubric lenient, so we read these as rubric-accepted rates and compare conditions rather than absolute values. In a separate controlled run, naming the figure, without stating that it is flawed, raises rubric-accepted diagnosis from 4.3% to 20.4%, while a consistency instruction and coarse visual cues do not. When the contradiction is visible only in the rendered figure, text-only review yields no accepted diagnosis, while review of the rendered pages with the figure named reaches 15.8%. A baseline that lets the model choose which figures to check, ranked by centrality, stays at 4.8%. Deciding what to verify is thus a large part of the difficulty, and the selection rule we tested does not resolve it. Multimodal document understanding should be evaluated on whether a model can propose what to verify, not only answer questions about a figure it is handed.

</details>


### [106] [Semantic Context Handoff with On-Demand Evidence for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24357)
*Guolong Zheng,Chen Hou,Xu Yang,Jianshan Zhang,Tao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该方法将紧凑的语义状态与可按需重新引入的交互证据分开保存，在减少上下文开销的同时提升长程智能体表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统长程大语言模型智能体通常在每一步都携带完整交互历史，导致上下文持续膨胀、推理成本上升，并可能降低准确性。现有压缩方式虽能保留简洁结论，却可能丢弃之后才变得重要的观测证据。

Method: 提出语义上下文交接方法，核心是将状态与证据分离：用紧凑的语义状态表达智能体当前的理解，同时将支撑该理解的行动与观测证据独立保存。在后续执行需要时，相关证据可重新进入上下文，无需额外调用摘要模型或检索模型。

Result: 在一项包含一百五十个任务的软件工程基准实验中，证据恢复使首次尝试通过率从百分之八十提升至百分之八十六点九，而令牌用量和执行时间基本不变。完整系统比较中，该方法达到百分之八十六点九的首次尝试通过率，相较任务表现最强的基线减少百分之四十三的令牌用量和百分之四十一的运行时间。跨模型与基准的实验也显示，该方法相较完整历史提示持续降低令牌消耗，同时保持竞争力或取得更好表现。轨迹分析表明，它能恢复交互历史较早阶段的证据，而非仅保留近期上下文。

Conclusion: 将智能体当前状态与支持性证据分开管理，有助于兼顾长程任务中的信息可恢复性与上下文效率。实验结果显示，该方法能以显著更低的计算与时间成本提升或维持任务表现。

Abstract: Traditional long-horizon LLM agents often hand off context through full-history prompting, retaining all previous action--observation interactions. As execution proceeds, this history keeps growing, increasing inference cost and potentially degrading accuracy. Existing methods control this growth by preserving compact conclusions while removing earlier observations, but doing so can discard evidence that later becomes relevant. We introduce Semantic Context Handoff (SCH), whose central abstraction is state--evidence separation: a compact semantic state carries the agent's current interpretation, while supporting action--observation evidence remains separately addressable and can re-enter context when later execution makes it relevant, without separate summarization or retrieval-model calls.
  Empirically, SCH improves long-horizon agent performance while substantially reducing context cost. In a controlled 150-task SWE-bench study, SCH's evidence recovery improves Pass@1 from $80.0\%$ to $86.9\%$ at essentially unchanged token use and execution time. In the complete-system comparison, SCH achieves 86.9% Pass@1 while using 43% fewer tokens and 41% less wall-clock time than the strongest baseline by task performance. Across models and benchmarks, SCH consistently reduces token use relative to full-history prompting while remaining competitive with or improving upon the evaluated baselines. Trajectory analysis confirms that SCH recovers evidence from substantially earlier parts of the interaction history rather than merely preserving a recent context window.

</details>


### [107] [CaDO: Formal Verification-Guided Multi-Agent Framework for Causal Dataset Repair and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24406)
*Songli Yu,Qi Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出因果引擎引导的多智能体数据优化框架，用于诊断并修复因果推理数据集中的标注错误与结构失衡，实验显示其能提升下游模型准确率且较少误改正确标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量因果推理数据集是训练具备可靠因果理解能力模型的基础，尤其在专业领域中更为重要。然而，现有数据集常含错误标注和类别、查询类型分布失衡，可能造成模型预测偏差并削弱泛化能力，因此需要自动化、可靠的数据诊断与优化方法。

Method: 提出因果引擎引导的多智能体数据优化框架，分为三个阶段：一是由形式化智能体将自然语言问题转化为形式化因果查询，减少语义偏差并便于检索和验证；二是通过相互独立的批评智能体进行双层诊断，既识别单个样本的错误，也分析查询类型覆盖率和标签分布，以发现数据集层面的结构失衡；三是利用基于形式因果语义、可复用的修复操作纠正错误样本，并由探索智能体和扩展智能体协作生成多样且语义一致的新样本。

Result: 在十八种实验设置中的十六种设置下，该框架优于五种数据清理基线中表现最强者，下游模型准确率最高提升百分之九点一。被其标记的样本中至少百分之九十五确有缺陷；在“因果阶梯”数据集上，与基于大语言模型的判别器相比，该框架误改正确标签的比例降低百分之九十二点二。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，该框架能够较准确地识别数据缺陷，并通过语义约束下的修复与生成改善数据质量，进而提升下游因果推理性能。其较低的正确标签误改率也支持其作为因果推理数据集自动优化工具的可靠性。

Abstract: High-quality causal reasoning datasets are essential for developing models with reliable causal understanding, particularly in specialized domains. However, existing datasets frequently suffer from incorrect annotations and structural imbalances, which can lead to biased predictions and limited generalization. To address this issue, we propose CaDO (Causal-Engine-Guided Multi-Agent Data Optimization), a multi-agent framework for automated diagnosis and repair of causal reasoning datasets. Our framework consists of three key stages: formal causal reasoning, multi-level diagnosis, and automated data repair and generation. First, a Formalizer Agent translates natural language questions into formal causal queries to minimize semantic deviations and make retrieval and verification more explicit and tractable. Second, a dual-level diagnosis mechanism employs independent Critic Agents to identify sample-level errors while analyzing query-type coverage and label distributions to detect dataset-level structural imbalances. Finally, the framework employs reusable repair operators grounded in formal causal semantics to correct erroneous samples, while Explorer and Expander Agent collaboratively produce diverse and semantically consistent new samples. Experimental results show that CaDO significantly outperforms the strongest of five data cleaning baselines in 16 out of 18 experimental settings, improving downstream model accuracy by up to 9.1%. Moreover, at least 95% of the samples flagged by CaDO are defective, and on CLadder, CaDO incorrectly modifies 92.2% fewer correct labels than an LLM-based judge, demonstrating its effectiveness and reliability. Our code and data are publicly available at https://github.com/thisissyu/cado.

</details>


### [108] [Risk-Triggered Search: Behavior-Preserving Safe Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24409)
*Xinyu Deng,Bohao Lan,Kunpeng Li,Mingkai Yuan,Weihang Pan,Binbin Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出风险触发搜索方法：利用冻结的视觉世界模型预测候选动作序列的过程风险，仅在检测到风险时进行局部规划修正，从而在提升安全性的同时减少计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉世界模型通常根据当前状态与目标状态生成多步动作计划，但仅优化终点接近程度，无法保证路径安全，可能穿越禁区或发生刚体碰撞。纯视觉模型也可能缺少显式动力学、结构化状态和碰撞几何，因此需要从动作条件下的未来预测中评估过程风险。此外，静态状态特征难以区分起点相同但后续动作不同的计划。

Method: 该方法冻结联合嵌入预测架构世界模型，并展开动作条件下的未来潜在状态序列，利用过程违规监督训练轻量风险预测器。风险预测同时用于搜索触发门槛和硬可行性约束：安全计划直接执行；出现风险时，则通过交叉熵方法进行局部搜索，在可行计划中尽量减少对原计划的偏离。为避免欧氏距离度量将朝向障碍物的动作压缩成停滞动作，作者设计了保持动作幅度的距离度量，使其保留切向推进，同时确保原计划是唯一零距离点。

Result: 在涵盖五项任务的过程安全基准测试中，该方法相较直接执行，在导航和关节控制任务上的安全到达率提升了百分之十二点六七至百分之四十一点六七；在立方体操作和推块任务的边界样本组中分别提升百分之二点零八和百分之四点一七。与每一步都搜索的同核方法相比，该方法在闭环轨迹上保持百分之百的动作一致性，并在一千九百六十八条配对轨迹中将控制器耗时降低百分之十七点七一至百分之七十点四一。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将动作条件下的过程风险预测用于选择性搜索，可以在维持安全名义行为的同时，有针对性地修正危险计划。统一风险门控与保持唯一零点的距离度量，使其既能复现逐步搜索的闭环行为，也能减少不必要的搜索开销。

Abstract: Pre-trained visual world models generate multi-step action plans from current and goal observations, yet terminal-proximity objectives fail to prevent forbidden-zone traversal or rigid-body collision sliding. Purely visual interfaces may lack analytical dynamics, structured states, or explicit collision geometry, requiring internal prediction of action-conditioned process risk. We propose Risk-Triggered Search (RTS) for frozen Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) world models. Rather than relying on static state features that cannot distinguish candidate plans sharing an observation, RTS unrolls action-conditioned future latent prefixes in the frozen world model and trains a lightweight risk head with process-violation supervision. To intervene selectively while preserving safe nominal behavior, RTS uses the same predicted prefix-risk criterion as both the search trigger gate and hard feasibility gate: safe nominal plans execute directly, whereas alarms trigger local Cross-Entropy Method (CEM) search to minimize nominal deviation within the feasible set. To prevent isotropic Euclidean minimal modification from shrinking obstacle-directed actions into zero-amplitude stalling, we introduce an amplitude-preserving metric $d_{\mathrm{AP}}$ that retains tangential progression while maintaining a unique zero at the nominal plan. Under unified gating and this unique-zero condition, bypassing safe-state search yields closed-loop trajectories strictly identical to every-step same-kernel search. Evaluated under a process-safety benchmark and Safe Arrival Rate (SAR) metric across five tasks, RTS improves SAR over Direct by 12.67--41.67 percentage points on navigation and articulated control and +2.08/+4.17 pp on Cube/PushT boundary cohorts, maintaining 100% action identity with every-step search while cutting controller time by 17.71%--70.41% across 1,968 paired trajectories.

</details>


### [109] [Resolution as Attention for Efficient Agent Memory Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24422)
*Siqi Xiang,Zhipeng Xu,Shengrui Liu,Zulong Chen,Chunyan Miao,Shijian Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种按需调整交互历史视觉分辨率的记忆感知机制，在不损害任务表现的前提下，将词元消耗降低一半以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程复杂任务会使智能体积累大量交互历史，造成记忆处理成本上升。现有方法多关注保留哪些信息或检索哪些信息，却较少考虑每段历史应以多细致的程度被感知。论文认为，记忆效率不仅取决于保存内容，也取决于分配给不同历史记录的感知粒度。

Method: 论文提出“分辨率即注意力”机制：将过去的交互记录渲染为图像，并根据其与当前决策的相关性，动态调整每段记录的图像分辨率。重要记录保留较高细节，相关性较低的记录则降低分辨率，以减少视觉词元消耗。研究还利用教师模型指导训练轻量级相关性估计器，并进一步优化该估计器，使智能体在降低视觉词元成本的同时保持决策一致性。该机制可作为插件式感知层，与现有记忆管理方法结合。

Result: 在多个任务和不同模型骨干上的实验表明，该机制可将词元消耗降低百分之五十以上，同时不损害任务表现。与现有记忆管理方法结合后，还能进一步节省词元。

Conclusion: 研究表明，智能体记忆管理除了决定保存和检索什么，还应决定以何种感知精度处理每段历史。自适应分辨率能够在保留关键细节与降低计算成本之间取得平衡，并可补充现有记忆管理策略。

Abstract: LLM-based agents accumulate extensive interaction histories when solving complex, long-horizon tasks, making efficient memory management critical to their performance and scalability. While existing memory management methods primarily focus on what to retain or retrieve, they often overlook how much perceptual detail should be allocated to individual past interactions. We posit that effective agent memory depends not only on the information preserved, but also on the granularity at which each past interaction is perceived. Motivated by this insight, we propose Resolution as Attention (RAA), a memory perception mechanism that uses visual resolution as a means of attention allocation across interaction histories. RAA renders past interactions as images and dynamically adjusts their resolution at every step according to their relevance to the agent's upcoming decision, preserving fine-grained information for critical interactions while reducing visual-token consumption for less relevant ones. To adaptively determine the perceptual fidelity of each interaction, we train a lightweight relevance estimator with teacher-model guidance and further optimize it to preserve the agent's decision consistency at a reduced visual-token cost. Experiments across multiple tasks and backbones show that RAA reduces token consumption by over 50% without compromising task performance. Moreover, RAA can be integrated as a plug-in perception layer with existing memory management methods, yielding further token savings and revealing adaptive perception as a complementary dimension of agent memory.

</details>


### [110] [From real to synthetic: PSG-GAN learns sleep apnea signal dynamics for AI training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24436)
*Riku Huttunen,Samu Kainulainen,Matias Rusanen,Sami Myllymaa,Juha Töyräs,Timo Leppänen,Henri Korkalainen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究提出一种生成对抗网络，可合成具有阻塞性睡眠呼吸暂停特征的多通道、较长时段睡眠信号，并显示其可用于辅助睡眠诊断算法开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实多导睡眠监测数据涉及隐私且采集困难。既有合成研究多集中于单一信号的短片段，未充分呈现睡眠信号之间的动态关联，而这些关联对识别睡眠障碍很重要。

Method: 研究开发生成对抗网络，联合合成较长时段的多种睡眠监测信号，并使其体现阻塞性睡眠呼吸暂停的复杂特征。研究评估了合成信号的真实性、不同睡眠阶段脑电与眼电频带功率的分布，以及信号间的生理关联；此外，仅使用自动标注的合成信号训练自动评分模型，并测试睡眠分期和呼吸事件分割能力。

Result: 合成信号整体较为逼真，脑电和眼电频带功率在不同睡眠阶段的分布与真实数据相近。模型还学到了呼吸阻塞期间及之后，光电容积脉搏波所反映的心率变化。在由健康志愿者和阻塞性睡眠呼吸暂停患者构成的两组测试集中，仅用合成数据训练的模型在睡眠分期上的一致性系数分别为零点七一和零点六三，在呼吸事件分割上的一致性系数分别为零点五七和零点六零。

Conclusion: 该方法能够合成具有多信号交互特征的较长时段睡眠数据，并支持仅以合成数据训练的模型完成睡眠分期和呼吸事件识别，显示出合成数据辅助睡眠诊断算法开发的潜力。

Abstract: Synthetic polysomnography (PSG) data has great potential in healthcare applications, for example in substituting real data that is sensitive and challenging to collect. Previous research on synthetic PSG data has been largely focused on short segments of individual signals, overlooking the inter-signal dynamics related to sleep disorders. In this study, we developed a generative adversarial network (PSG-GAN) for co-synthesizing longer segments of multiple PSG signals with diverse and complex characteristics typical of obstructive sleep apnea (OSA). The synthetic signals appeared realistic, and the electroencephalography and electrooculography band powers were similarly distributed across sleep stages. Moreover, the PSG-GAN learned to mirror signal interactions such as changes in photoplethysmography-based heart rate during and after breathing obstructions. An automatic scoring model trained solely with automatically scored synthetic signals reached good performance on sleep staging (\k{appa}=0.71 and 0.63) and respiratory event segmentation (\k{appa}=0.57 and 0.60), on two test sets consisting of healthy volunteers and OSA patients, respectively. This demonstrates the potential of synthetic data in supporting algorithm development for sleep diagnostics. The source code is available at https://github.com/rikuhuttunen/psg-gan.

</details>


### [111] [Study and Improvement of Search Algorithms in Stochastic Perfect-Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24486)
*Quentin Cohen-Solal,Tristan Cazenave*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文将无界极小化搜索扩展到随机、零和、双人且信息完全的博弈，并通过实验表明其表现优于主要随机博弈搜索算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无界极小化是确定性、零和、双人且信息完全博弈中的先进搜索算法，但其原有框架不适用于包含随机性的博弈。论文旨在弥补这一适用范围上的不足，并提升此类博弈的搜索效果。

Method: 将无界极小化算法推广至随机、零和、双人且信息完全的博弈，并通过实验与主要随机博弈搜索算法进行比较。

Result: 实验结果显示，推广后的算法在性能上优于主要的随机博弈搜索算法。摘要未提供具体实验设置或性能提升幅度。

Conclusion: 无界极小化不仅适用于确定性博弈，也可推广至随机博弈；实验结果支持其作为随机零和双人信息完全博弈搜索方法的有效性。

Abstract: In this article, we generalize Unbounded Minimax, the state- of-the-art search algorithm for deterministic zero-sum two- player games with perfect information to the framework of stochastic zero-sum two-player games with perfect informa- tion. We experimentally show that this generalized algorithm achieves better performance than the main stochastic search algorithms.

</details>


### [112] [The Largest Tractable Subclass of Multi-Scale Allen Temporal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24498)
*Quentin Cohen-Solal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种用于多尺度时间区间推理的新模型，并分析其推理完备性及最大可处理子代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间推理模型难以同时处理多种尺度上的时间区间关系。论文旨在建立适用于多尺度区间的推理框架，并进一步刻画其可有效处理的关系范围。

Method: 提出多尺度时间区间推理模型，研究其完备性和其他性质；同时识别该模型的最大可处理子代数，并通过从新模型迁移结果，分析定性尺度情境下另一种完备模型的最大可处理子代数。

Result: 摘要指出，新模型构成一个完备的推理框架，并具有若干有意义的性质。论文还确定了新模型及定性尺度情境下另一种完备模型的最大可处理子代数；后者的性质较弱，其相关结果可由新模型的结果推导。

Conclusion: 该研究为多尺度时间区间推理提供了新的完备模型，并通过刻画最大可处理子代数，明确了模型能够有效推理的范围。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a novel model for temporal reasoning over multi-scale intervals. We show that this model yields a complete reasoning framework and exhibits other particularly interesting properties. Furthermore, we identify the maximal tractable subalgebra for this model, as well as for the other complete model, in the qualitative scales context, which exhibits weaker properties but whose results can be derived by transfer from our new model.

</details>


### [113] [ABSClaw: From Invented Worlds to Reality-Grounded Social Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24517)
*Shaobin He,Yan Gao,Quan Zheng,Zhenyang Lin,Zhen Liu,Jiyan He,Shuxin Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种由自然语言驱动的社会模拟系统：它以真实人口、地理环境和社会网络为基础，并用可组合的原子操作描述模拟过程；实验显示该系统具有较强的现实贴合度、扩展性和规模化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的人工社会模拟常依赖假设的人物与环境、合成互动网络，以及自由生成的模拟代码，因此难以对应真实人群，结果也容易受底层模型影响。论文旨在让模拟世界有真实数据依据，并使模拟逻辑更加标准化、可复用。

Method: 作者提出零代码智能体，将自然语言需求编译为可执行的社会模拟。系统利用调查与人口普查数据、地理信息及真实社交网络确定参与者、活动场所和互动对象；通过二十六种原子操作描述状态变化；还支持以限定生命周期的技能扩展功能，而无需修改核心系统。作者同时构建了包含一百九十三项真实社会模拟任务的基准测试。

Result: 在基准测试中，该系统在受评估的社会模拟器中总体表现最佳。三项现实案例研究表明，依据真实数据设定人物、环境和互动网络，均能提升模拟结果与观测结果的一致性。借助一项运行时技能，系统完成了十三项人口规模模拟，累计涉及二千五百万个智能体。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将真实世界数据与标准化、可组合的模拟操作结合，有助于提升社会模拟的可信度，并兼顾功能扩展和大规模运行。

Abstract: LLM-based artificial societies exhibit rich behavior but often lack scientific grounding: personas and environments are assumed, interaction networks are synthetic, and simulators are generated as free-form code, making results difficult to tie to real populations and sensitive to the backbone model. We argue that simulated worlds should be sampled from observed data, while simulation logic should be implemented by composing operations from a shared, task-independent vocabulary. In this paper, we introduce ABSClaw, a zero-code agent that compiles natural-language requests into executable, reality-grounded social simulations. ABSClaw grounds who acts, where they act, and with whom they interact in survey and census data, geospatial sources, and real networks. It expresses state transitions through 26 atomic operations. To support an open and extensible ecosystem, ABSClaw further allows capabilities to be added as lifecycle-scoped skills without modifying its core. Furthermore, we propose ABSBench, a benchmark of 193 real-world social-simulation tasks. On ABSBench, ABSClaw achieves the best overall performance among the evaluated social simulators. Three real-world case studies show that grounding personas, environments, and interaction networks each improves agreement with observed outcomes. With one runtime skill, ABSClaw executes 13 population-scale simulations, collectively involving 25 million agents. Together, these results demonstrate ABSClaw's real-world fidelity, extensibility, and scalability.

</details>


### [114] [NaviGATE: Noise-Aware Dual-View Generation for Multimodal Knowledge Graph Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24552)
*Jie Peng,Yongxue Shan,Yongfu Zha,Cui Miao,Xiaodong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种噪声感知的多模态知识图谱推理框架，通过结构知识激活、双视角指令调优和受约束解码，提高缺失事实推断的准确性与抗噪能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态知识图谱推理需要联合利用图结构、文本和视觉信息。现有嵌入方法虽能建模图拓扑，却难以针对具体查询进行细粒度语义推理；生成式方法则容易受检索噪声影响，还可能生成图谱中不存在的实体。本文旨在兼顾语义推理能力、检索鲁棒性和答案有效性。

Method: 提出噪声感知生成框架。首先对齐图结构表示与文本语义；随后利用多模态实体摘要和注入的结构标记微调多模态语言模型，以激活结构相关的内部知识。在双视角指令调优阶段，联合训练无检索视角与有检索视角：通过对比式逻辑值锐化强化可靠候选，并通过参数一致性蒸馏抑制误导性候选。推理时采用结构约束解码，将生成结果限制在目标知识图谱的有效实体范围内。

Result: 在标准基准上的实验表明，该方法持续优于有竞争力的单模态、多模态及生成式基线；在检索结果含噪的场景下也表现出较强鲁棒性。摘要未提供具体数据集、指标或提升幅度。

Conclusion: 该方法将结构信息注入生成模型，并通过双视角训练与有效实体约束，针对性缓解检索噪声和无效实体生成问题。实验结果支持其在多模态知识图谱推理中的有效性与稳健性。

Abstract: Multimodal knowledge graph reasoning (MKGR) aims to infer missing facts by jointly exploiting relational structures, textual descriptions, and visual evidence. Existing embedding-based methods effectively capture graph topology, but their fixed multimodal representations are less effective for query-specific fine-grained semantic reasoning, while generation-based methods are vulnerable to noisy retrieval and may produce invalid entities. To address these challenges, we propose NaviGATE, a noise-aware generative framework for MKGR. NaviGATE first aligns graph-derived structural representations with textual semantics, then performs structure-aware internal knowledge activation by fine-tuning the multimodal language model with multimodal entity summaries and injected structural tokens. In the noise-aware dual-view instruction tuning stage, NaviGATE jointly optimizes retrieval-free and retrieval-aware views: reliable retrieved candidates are reinforced through contrastive logit sharpening, while misleading candidates are suppressed through parametric consistency distillation. In inference, structure-constrained decoding restricts generation to valid entities in the target knowledge graph. Experiments on standard benchmarks show that NaviGATE consistently outperforms strong unimodal, multimodal, and generation-based baselines, and remains robust under noisy retrieval scenarios.

</details>


### [115] [AGAMOTTO: An AI-Enabled Framework for Time-Critical Tactical Course-of-Action Evaluation and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24568)
*Matthew L. Corbett*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍一项仍在进行的研究，旨在为战术指挥与控制系统开发人工智能辅助仿真能力，让指挥员能在实战中快速探索行动方案。初步结果显示该系统具备可行性，同时也揭示了部署限制及培训需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指挥与控制平台缺少集成式仿真工具，指挥员难以在行动进行期间动态比较不同方案。随着战术环境日益受时间限制，研究需要探索如何借助人工智能支持快速、有效的决策，并推动指挥控制系统现代化。

Method: 研究围绕“阿加莫托”系统展开，该系统隶属于新一代指挥与控制项目，面向军事行动、战术威胁及结果分析。论文介绍其当前设计、初步实验与阶段性发现，并讨论剩余研究任务、人工智能生成行动方案的实际应用条件，以及使其适用于战术场景的培训策略。

Result: 初步开发与实验表明，系统的实现具有可行性，并显示人工智能工具有望帮助指挥员生成和评估行动方案。实验同时指出，实际部署存在操作层面的收益与约束；要使人工智能生成的方案能够用于战术环境，还需要适当的培训和配套安排。摘要未提供量化性能指标或完整系统评估。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了将仿真与人工智能能力融入战术指挥系统的潜力，但系统仍在开发中，现有证据属于初步结果。未来工作需继续完成系统研发并验证其在时间紧迫的作战环境中的效用，同时妥善处理部署限制与人员培训问题。

Abstract: Current command and control (C2) platforms lack integrated simulation capabilities that would allow commanders to dynamically explore courses of action (COAs) during live operations. This paper presents ongoing research and contributions to the development of AGAMOTTO (Advanced Guidance and Analytics for Military Operations, Tactical Threats, and Outcomes) as part of the overarching NGC2 program, which advances the Army's efforts to modernize C2 systems, such as the Tactical Assault Kit (TAK). The paper outlines the current design, preliminary findings, and the remaining research tasks, with an emphasis on how the completed work will support tactical decision-making in future time-constrained environments. While the full system is still under development, initial development and results demonstrate the feasibility of AGAMOTTO. Early experiments highlight both the potential benefits and practical constraints of deploying AI-enabled tools in operational settings, and introduce lessons learned and a viable training strategy to make AI-generated COAs feasible in tactical scenarios.

</details>


### [116] [Think Offline, Act in Real Time: Self-Improving Visual Game Agents through Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24590)
*Zipeng Qiu,Shusheng Xu,Jiale Li,Yukun Zhou,Chenyue Li,Wenshuang Li,Tongkai Yang,Binhang Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究比较两种让智能体从少量游戏尝试中自我改进的方法：由模型实时选动作，或让模型在尝试间编写并迭代程序。后者在170项任务中表现更好，平均进度达到47.80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练模型虽能理解屏幕并推理游戏，但面对陌生游戏仍需学习。挑战在于智能体只能观察画面，且游戏在智能体思考时不会暂停，因此改进方法必须适应实时交互，并能利用少量自主尝试。

Method: 研究两种策略形式。第一种是“模型即策略”：模型在游戏过程中选择动作，并采用实时动作分块机制，使已提交的动作在模型继续推理时仍能执行；研究者还为该交互方式训练了一个20亿参数的控制器。第二种是“代码即策略”：模型在两次游戏尝试之间编写、测试和修改程序，再由程序在游戏过程中执行。实验涵盖170项游戏任务，每次运行最多进行八轮、每轮一分钟的游戏执行。

Result: “代码即策略”的平均进度为47.80%，高于训练控制器的30.90%，并在五类游戏中均占优。相比之下，针对单个游戏调整控制器权重仅带来有限提升。

Conclusion: 结果表明，对于需要实时操作的陌生视觉游戏，让模型在游戏间隙离线思考，并把学到的经验写入可实时运行的程序，比单纯改进模型权重或依赖模型实时决策更有优势。

Abstract: Pre-trained models can read screens and reason about games, yet on an unfamiliar game, an agent still has to learn to play. We ask how an agent can self-improve on such a game from a few of its own attempts, in real-time visual games where the agent sees only the screen and the game keeps running while the agent thinks. We study two kinds of policy that can carry this improvement. In model as policy, a model selects actions during play; we adapt real-time chunking so that committed actions keep executing while the model reasons, and train a 2B controller for this interface. In code as policy, a model writes, tests, and revises a program between attempts, and the program acts during play. Across 170 GameWorld tasks, with at most eight one-minute executions per run, code as policy reaches 47.80% mean progress, against 30.90% for our trained controller, and leads in all five game categories. Improving the controller's weights on a single game, by contrast, yields only modest gains. These results favor letting a model think offline and storing what it learns in a program that acts in real time. Project repository: https://github.com/TabibitoQZP/SIGame

</details>


### [117] [GateWatch: Low-Overhead LLM Monitoring with Boolean Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24619)
*Firas Spanioli,Yaniv David*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种轻量、可解释的行为监测分类器，以少量稀疏自编码器特征构成布尔逻辑电路；在大幅降低内存开销的同时，准确率接近使用全部特征的探针，部署时也基本保持基线吞吐量与缓存容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型及其智能体被广泛部署，及时识别不安全或非预期行为变得重要。现有激活探针存在准确率、可解释性与计算开销之间的权衡：直接使用原始激活难以解释，而基于稀疏自编码器的探针通常依赖加性分类器和庞大编码器，导致决策过程不够直观且内存占用较高。

Method: 论文提出“门控监视器”，将分类决策显式表示为少量二值化稀疏自编码器特征上的布尔逻辑，并在给定逻辑门预算下探索三种电路构造方式。为支持高效部署，配套推理系统只计算选定特征，在推理过程中跨层流式传递其二值结果，并通过紧凑查找表并行评估多个分类器。

Result: 在九十亿参数的格玛二模型上，对二十四项任务的测试准确率平均为百分之八十点四九；每个探针平均仅使用三点八个稀疏自编码器特征和三点五个逻辑门，比使用全部一万六千个特征的探针低一点一七个百分点。该方法在十项任务上达到或超过完整特征探针，并将探针内存减少百分之九十九点九八。在高效推理引擎中并发运行一千个跨层、多词元分类器时，吞吐量保持基线的百分之九十七至九十八，键值缓存容量保留百分之九十九点八。

Conclusion: 研究表明，少量可解释特征构成的布尔电路能够在保持较高检测准确率的同时显著压缩探针，并支持高并发、低开销部署。这为大语言模型行为监测提供了一种更紧凑且决策规则更透明的方案。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) and LLM-based agents are increasingly deployed, monitoring their behavior becomes important for detecting unsafe or unintended actions. Activation probes offer an efficient approach, but existing methods face trade-offs between accuracy, interpretability, and computational overhead. Probes on raw activations lack interpretable inputs, whereas sparse autoencoder (SAE) probes use interpretable features but often rely on additive classifiers and large SAE encoders, limiting interpretability and increasing memory overhead. We introduce GateWatch, a compact, interpretable classifier that expresses its entire decision rule as an explicit Boolean logic over a small number of binarized SAE features. We explore three approaches to constructing these circuits under a specified gate budget. For efficient deployment, GateWatchServe computes only selected SAE features, enables streaming of their binary values across layers during inference, and evaluates multiple GateWatch classifiers in parallel using compact lookup tables. Across 24 tasks on Gemma-2-9B, GateWatch achieves 80.49% mean test accuracy using only 3.8 SAE features and 3.5 logic gates per probe on average, just 1.17 percentage points below full 16K-feature SAE probes, while matching or outperforming them on 10 tasks and reducing probe memory by 99.98%. Inside vLLM, 1,000 concurrent multi-layer, multi-token GateWatch classifiers retain 97-98% of baseline throughput and 99.8% of KV-cache capacity.

</details>


### [118] [Movie2Game-Bench: Holistic Evaluation of Film-to-Game Adaptation Beyond Playability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24669)
*Jialing Xie,Yifan Zhou,Yiwen Zhang,Junjun He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一套评估电影改编游戏的基准，发现游戏对电影可玩情节的保留普遍不足，且需要更紧密地把原作内容转化为玩家行动及其后果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电影改编游戏不仅要保留原作元素，还要将其转化为玩家能够执行的行动、游戏规则与反馈。仅评估游戏是否可玩，无法判断改编是否忠于原作、是否实现了有效互动，因此需要更全面的评估方法。

Method: 研究提出电影改编游戏评测基准，结合通用评估、电影专属评估，以及由三名图形界面模型玩家进行的试玩。评估流程将原作内容与游戏步骤及实现证据对应起来，以衡量内容保留和互动实现。基准涵盖二十二部电影及六百五十八项原作相关参考条目，并使用各自选定的主要开发框架，对十四个模型在同一批电影上的表现进行评估。

Result: 十四个模型在游戏质量、改编效果和玩家体验方面各有优势。所有模型在“可玩情节”类别上的内容呈现均值最低，平均为三十七点九分，低于标志性元素的五十四点八分。在保留情感发展内容的判断中，叙事选择占百分之六十，场景操作仅占百分之七点二。

Conclusion: 电影改编游戏的关键改进方向有二：保留能够支撑玩法的原作情境，并将保留下来的内容与玩家行动及其后果相连接。该基准为超越单纯可玩性、系统评估电影到游戏改编提供了方法。

Abstract: Adapting a film into a game requires selecting source elements and translating them into player actions, rules, and feedback. We introduce Movie2Game-Bench (M2G-bench), a benchmark for evaluating film-to-game adaptation beyond playability. M2G-bench combines general evaluation, movie-specific evaluation, and playtesting by three GUI model players. The protocol connects source content to gameplay steps and implementation evidence, capturing both content preservation and interactive realization. The benchmark comprises 22 films and 658 source-specific reference items. Evaluating 14 models on the same films with their selected primary harnesses reveals complementary strengths in game quality, adaptation, and player experience. A shared weakness concerns playable sequences: this category has the lowest content-presence mean in all 14 models, averaging 37.9 out of 100 compared with 54.8 for iconic elements. Among judgments of retained emotionaldevelopment content, narrative choices account for 60.0% and scenario operation for 7.2%. These findings identify two linked priorities for film-to-game adaptation: preserving source situations that can support gameplay and connecting retained content to player actions and consequences.

</details>


### [119] [Depth Before Breadth: Reverse-to-Forward Curriculum with Advantage Decomposition for LLM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24697)
*Siyuan Du,Yuxin Liu,Meng Tang,Haoran Li,Yechao Bai,Feiyang Pan,Jia He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向长程任务的强化学习方法，通过成对优势信号与渐进式课程学习，改善稀疏奖励下的探索和任务完成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程任务通常只有终止时才提供奖励，导致智能体难以判断哪些行动有助于成功，也难以探索有效路径。论文指出，当前策略存在一个“能力区域”：其中的状态可被策略可靠地完成任务。即使目标仍很遥远，靠近该区域边界的状态也可能只需少量行动即可到达，因此可将长程学习拆解为先抵达更易完成任务的状态，再从该状态完成任务。

Method: 方法由两部分构成：一是成对优势信号，分别衡量行动是否使智能体到达更易解决的状态，以及智能体能否从该状态完成任务；二是渐进式“由后向前”课程，先沿成功参考轨迹向前扩展能力区域，再通过策略自身的探索，将能力扩展至参考轨迹之外的状态。

Result: 在两个交互式任务基准上，该方法在两种模型规模下均优于具有竞争力的智能体强化学习方法。较小模型的成功率分别为百分之九十点一和百分之八十点二；较大模型分别达到百分之九十六点一和百分之八十二点六。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将长程任务分解为抵达更可解状态和从该状态完成任务，并分别提供学习信号、逐步扩展策略能力区域，有助于缓解稀疏终止奖励带来的信用分配与探索困难。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a common approach for training large language model (LLM) agents on long-horizon tasks. However, learning from sparse terminal rewards makes credit assignment and exploration difficult. We approach these challenges through a policy-induced capability region, the set of states from which the current policy can reliably complete a task. Near its boundary, an agent may need only a few actions to reach a more solvable state, even when the goal remains distant. This reduces long-horizon learning to two coupled subproblems of reaching that state and completing the task from there. We address them with paired advantages and a progressive reverse-to-forward curriculum. The advantages provide separate signals for reaching more solvable states and completing the task from them, while the curriculum expands the capability region backward along successful reference trajectories and then into off-reference states through on-policy exploration. On ALFWorld and WebShop, our method outperforms competitive agentic RL methods at both model scales, achieving success rates of 90.1%/96.1% and 80.2%/82.6% with Qwen2.5-1.5B/7B-Instruct, respectively.

</details>


### [120] [F$^3$: What Makes Good Experience in World-Action Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24708)
*Zihe Huang,Sirui Zhou,Haozhi Han,Ruge Zhang,Teng Ma,Tao Ma,Zheng Liu,Ce Zhang,Ting Cao,Yunxin Liu,Yunquan Zhang,Yifeng Chen,Kun Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种让世界动作模型有效利用自身经验的方法：将成功与失败轨迹整理成经过核验的操作记录，训练模型判断记录何时有用，并将记录融入策略输入。仅用两百个样例训练一次，部署时配合不断扩充的经验库，即可提升任务成功率、减少完成步数并降低性能回退。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已部署的世界动作模型需要善用自身经历才能持续改进，但已有方法通常把检索到的轨迹或示范作为上下文提供给策略，反而可能使原本已解决的任务失败。论文指出，经验是否有价值，不取决于来源轨迹是否成功，而取决于经验被记录成什么形式、何时使用，以及如何融入策略。

Method: 论文提出由三个环节构成的方法。其一，形式化整理：将成功和失败轨迹都压缩为简短且经过核验的操作记录。其二，适配训练：教会策略读取记录，并预测记录是否会对当前任务有所帮助。其三，策略融合：把记录中的图像、文本和动作作为前缀及动作先验注入策略。模型只需用两百个样例训练一次，部署后则可配合持续增长的经验库运行。

Result: 在莫尔莫空间和机器臂实验室基准上，该方法取得当前最佳结果；在机器臂实验室上没有进行训练。相较于原生的宇宙三纳米模型，该方法在两个基准上的成功率分别提高十二点零和十点八个百分点，成功所需步数分别减少百分之十二点二和百分之十一点三，性能回退减少百分之五十七。

Conclusion: 有效经验的关键不只是收集成功示范，而是合理地整理经验、判断其适用时机，并选择恰当方式融入策略。该方法表明，少量训练结合可增长的经验库，也能提升模型在新环境中的任务表现，同时减少经验使用带来的负面影响。

Abstract: Deployed world-action models (WAMs) can keep improving only if they make good use of their own experience. Existing methods supply retrieved rollouts or demonstrations to the policy as context, yet such experience often turns previously solved tasks into failures. What, then, makes good experience for a WAM? We show that experience is valuable only in use: its value depends not on whether the source rollout succeeded but on what is stored, when it is used, and where it enters the policy. We therefore propose F$^3$, which makes these three decisions with Form, Fit, and Fusion. Form compresses successful and failed rollouts alike into short, verified operation records; Fit teaches the policy to read a record and to predict when it helps; Fusion injects the record's images, text and actions into the policy as a prefix and an action prior. Trained once on only 200 examples and deployed with a growing experience library, the model achieves state-of-the-art results on MolmoSpaces and RoboLab without any training on RoboLab: over native Cosmos 3 Nano, it raises success by 12.0 pp and 10.8 pp, reduces steps to success by 12.2% and 11.3%, and cuts regressions by 57%.

</details>


### [121] [Choosing What to Observe: Budget-Paired Counterfactual Audits for Credible Evaluation of Visual Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24867)
*Ruiqi Liu,Jinming Li,Mo Wang,Yang Lu,Yanlan Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种反事实审计方法，将视觉智能体的准确率损失拆分为观察过程中的信息获取损失与停止观察后的决策损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 处理密集文档的视觉智能体必须在有限观察预算下选择查看哪些区域。仅看最终答题准确率，无法判断错误源于选错观察区域、未能根据剩余预算调整策略，还是观察结束后的答案判断失误，因此难以准确评估其规划能力。

Method: 提出预算配对反事实审计：固定公开证据与区域绑定关系，只改变剩余观察预算或预先声明的任务参数；并为每段已获得的观察历史计算精确的后续价值。由此把预期准确率损失分解为逐步信息获取损失和终止决策损失，从而区分规划问题与固定角色启发式策略造成的问题。

Result: 摘要提及在四十组配对路由锚点及五个开放权重模型上开展评估，但提供的文本在此处截断，未给出实验结果、模型名称或定量比较，因此无法判断审计发现了哪些具体差异。

Conclusion: 该工作将视觉智能体的评估从单一最终准确率扩展为对观察规划和终端决策的分项审计，有助于定位错误来源。由于摘要内容不完整，尚不能据此概括其主要实证结论或方法效果。

Abstract: Visual agents that read dense documents must ration a finite observation budget across candidate regions, yet answer accuracy conflates where an agent looks, how it adapts to the reads that remain, and how it an?swers once observation stops. We introduce budget-paired counterfactual audits, an evaluation protocol that holds public evidence and region bindings fixed while perturbing only the remaining budget or de?clared task parameters, and credits every acquired history with exact continuation values. The resulting accounting decomposes lost expected accuracy into stepwise acquisition loss and terminal decision loss, separating planning from fixed-role heuristics. Across forty paired routing anchors, five open-weight

</details>


### [122] [WorldRuler: Interactive World Modeling under Configurable Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24869)
*Mingliang Zhai,Chuanhao Li,Jiaming Tan,Zhen Li,Yuwei Wu,Yunde Jia,Zian Meng,Yuanyang Yin,Kaipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出世界规则器，通过可执行三维规则图和生成闭环，使生成式世界模型能够持续保存用户规则与世界状态，并准确呈现规则对指定对象的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相似场景和动作空间可因规则不同而产生迥异的玩法体验，因此可定制的生成式世界模型不能只遵循现实世界行为，还需执行用户定义的规则。现有方法面临两项关键挑战：保存与加载时保持规则、依赖关系和世界状态一致；以及在受影响对象尚未出现时，仍准确生成规则导致的对象属性变化，同时避免干扰无关内容。

Method: 提出世界规则器，将规则定义、依赖关系和对象状态组织为可执行的三维规则图，并闭环连接规则执行、视频生成与感知反馈。规则图支持规则和世界状态的联合保存与恢复；动态对象绑定将规则指定的实体对应到视觉实例，并利用已存状态约束对象首次出现时的生成。方法还引入对象属性状态替换和反事实重建训练，以控制目标属性，并协调相容的外观与运动变化。作者利用《侠盗猎车手五》和栖居环境模拟器构建自动化数据采集与标注流程，形成包含详细状态、事件标注及对象显现前规则生效案例的数据集规则宇宙。

Result: 实验表明，该方法能够在保存与加载过程中保持规则和世界状态的一致性，并较准确地呈现规则对指定对象及属性造成的结果；同时可减少对无关内容的干扰。

Conclusion: 研究表明，把规则执行、状态持久化与视觉生成纳入统一闭环，是实现可定制生成式世界的重要路径。该方法尤其针对对象尚未可见时的状态约束，提升了规则驱动交互的连续性与视觉可控性。

Abstract: Rules govern how player actions change interactive worlds. Different rules create distinct gameplay experiences within similar scenes and action spaces. For customizable gameplay, generative world models must follow user-defined rules beyond real-world behaviors. However, realizing user-defined rules presents two challenges: Rule-State Persistence and Rule-to-Visual Realization. The former requires preserving rule definitions, dependencies, and world states across saving and loading, so that restored worlds follow the established rules. The latter requires accurately realizing rule-induced changes on designated objects and attributes, particularly when affected objects are unseen. In this paper, we propose WorldRuler, which organizes rule definitions, dependencies, and object states in an executable 3D rule graph and connects rule execution, video generation, and perceptual feedback in a closed loop. The graph supports joint saving and restoration of rules and world states, while dynamic object binding grounds rule-specified entities in visual instances and uses stored states to constrain generation from their first appearance. We further introduce object attribute state replacement and counterfactual reconstruction training, enabling accurate attribute control and compatible appearance and motion changes while limiting interference with unrelated content. We develop an automated data collection and annotation pipeline using GTA-V and Habitat to construct RuleVerse, a dataset of rule-driven interactions with detailed state and event annotations, including cases where rules take effect before affected objects become visible. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of WorldRuler in preserving rule-state consistency across saving and loading and accurately realizing rule outcomes on designated objects and attributes.

</details>


### [123] [Local AI Agents in Power Electronics Simulation: Deployment, Measurements and Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24880)
*Yuzhuo Li,Pasan Gunawardena,Yunwei Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一套评估框架，衡量本地部署的智能体人工智能在电力电子仿真任务中的功率、能量、词元和耗时成本，并分析其能力边界及任务成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体人工智能正逐步接管电力电子设计中的仿真迭代流程，但现有研究主要关注云端模型能完成什么，对本地部署在真实任务中的资源消耗及不同选择间的权衡缺乏可比、定量的研究。对于设计资料、客户数据或受出口管制材料不能离开现场，且仿真器仅在本地获许可的机构而言，这一问题尤其重要，因为本地智能体会消耗组织自身设备的电力、能源和时间。

Method: 构建系统化评估框架，在本地运行模型，让其处理从变换器级到系统级的电力电子任务；在可比较的条件下，测量各项任务的功率、能量、词元数量和耗时，并据此分析智能体的任务能力边界及每项已解决任务的成本。

Result: 摘要未提供具体数值或实验结论。论文说明将通过测量刻画本地智能体在相关任务上的能力边界，并评估每项解决任务的资源成本与各项选择之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 该研究旨在补足本地部署智能体人工智能的定量评估空白，为电力电子及相关电力系统领域判断其实际可用性和部署成本提供依据。

Abstract: Agentic artificial intelligence (AI) is emerging in power electronics, where it drives the simulation loop that engineers used to close largely by hand. In this loop, a converter design, or a study of the network it connects to, is built, run, measured and revised until it satisfies its specification. Such an agent can be served from a cloud API or from an inference device on the premises. For organizations whose designs, customer data or export-controlled material cannot leave the site, and whose simulators are licensed there, local deployment is the natural choice. In such a deployment, the agent becomes an electrical load on the organization's own equipment, i.e., every run consumes power and energy on a local device, generates tokens and takes time. However, existing work focuses mainly on what the agent can achieve with cloud-hosted models. What a local deployment costs in power, energy, tokens and time on real simulation tasks, and what its choices trade against one another, has received little quantitative investigation under comparable conditions. To address this gap, a systematic evaluation framework is presented in this paper. It applies locally served models to agentic power electronics tasks, from the converter level to the system level, and measures their power, energy, token and time costs. Based on these measurements, the capability boundary of a local agent on such tasks and the cost per solved task are characterized. The results are expected to provide practical insight for the use of agentic AI in power electronics and the power systems it serves.

</details>


### [124] [Knowing What You Know: A Trait Theory of Reviewer Value in Human-AI Cognitive Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24932)
*Priyanka Sinha,Sugam Pandey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出，人工审核者在人工智能协作决策中的价值，核心取决于其元认知校准程度，即主观信心与实际判断正确性的一致程度。研究发现，仅凭行为特征评估审核者可能得出相反结论；依据校准程度分配人机审核，可将百分之九十二点五的人力集中用于真实错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当能力较强的模型参与决策时，并非所有人工审核都能增加价值。常见的活动量、处理速度、与模型意见的一致程度或从业年限，未必能判断审核者是否可靠。论文旨在明确识别哪些审核者真正有助于提升决策质量，并解释如何在人与模型之间合理分配审核任务。

Method: 论文构建了审核者价值的特质理论，核心特质是元认知校准，并提出五项主张：价值由校准而非表面行为决定；校准会在行为记录中留下迹象，但这些迹象单独不足以判定能力，必须结合结果；人与模型的自我判断能力可用同一校准概念描述，系统应逐项交由校准更好的主体处理；个体校准的因果价值不能仅凭单次判断识别。研究使用一套人工智能辅助会计系统连续六个月的生产数据，涵盖五十四万一千六百二十一条审核事件和四百八十三名审核者。

Result: 只依据行为特征构建的专业度画像颠倒了真实表现：它将过度自信群体排在真正专家之前，而两者的更正后存续率分别为百分之五点二和百分之九十九点二。审核质量与从业年限基本无关，说明这一特质并非通过在岗时间自然习得。将校准逻辑同时用于人和模型后，系统能够把百分之九十二点五的人力集中到真实错误上。

Conclusion: 判断人工审核者是否有价值，应关注其信心是否与实际正确率相匹配，而不能把忙碌、快速或资历当作可靠性的替代指标。人机协作应按具体任务比较双方的校准程度，再决定由谁处理。不过，在采用多数投票汇总判断时，个体的因果贡献无法被识别，因此理论的适用范围受到这一限制。

Abstract: When a capable model and a human reviewer cooperate on the same decision, which human still adds value? We argue this is a question about a single cognitive trait, metacognitive calibration: the alignment between a reviewer's behavioral confidence and the actual correctness of their judgments. We develop a trait theory of reviewer value to make it precise. The theory makes five claims: value is governed by calibration rather than by activity, speed, agreement, or tenure; calibration leaves observable traces in behavioral telemetry; those traces are non-diagnostic on their own, because the fast, low-revision signature of a calibrated expert is indistinguishable from that of a confident novice until it is gated by outcomes; human calibration and a model's self-knowledge are the same construct, so the joint system should defer to whichever agent is better calibrated on each item; and individual calibration is causally identifiable only above the level of the single judgment. We ground each claim in a six-month production window of an AI-assisted accounting system (541621 reviewer events, 483 reviewers), and find that a purely behavioral expertise profile inverts the ground truth, scoring an overconfident cohort (5.2 percent correction survival) higher than genuine experts (99.2 percent); that reviewer quality is flat across tenure, so the trait is not learned on the job; and that the same calibration logic that separates good from bad reviewers, applied to the model, concentrates 92.5 percent of human effort on genuine errors. We close with the boundary the theory must respect: under majority-vote aggregation, individual causal value is unidentifiable.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [125] [Neural Network-Based Modeling of Hysteretic Systems: From Prandtl and Preisach Neural Networks to Neuro-Madelung and Neuroplastic Continuous Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23240)
*Mehrdad Shafiei Dizaji,Farzad Shafiei Dizaji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述多类神经网络滞回模型的发展，重点比较其记忆表达、退化与速率效应建模能力及工程应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 滞回现象具有记忆依赖和路径依赖，在许多物理系统中还伴随退化、非一致性、非对称性及速率依赖。传统模型难以统一刻画这些复杂特征，因此有必要系统梳理神经网络滞回模型的演进与能力。

Method: 文章回顾并比较了普朗特神经网络、普雷萨赫神经网络、广义普朗特神经网络、神经马德隆模型、扩展普雷萨赫神经网络及动态塑性连续神经网络，分析其记忆机制、网络结构、激活函数、训练算法，以及对退化和速率依赖的处理方式，并讨论其在非线性结构与材料系统中的应用。

Result: 模型能力逐步扩展：普朗特神经网络通过停止算子将滞回记忆嵌入神经元激活，并用于结构非线性动力分析；普雷萨赫神经网络增加非线性隐藏层以表示非马辛滞回；广义普朗特神经网络借助退化型停止神经元刻画刚度、强度退化和非一致滞回；神经马德隆模型结合马德隆滞回规则与多层前馈网络，表示多种速率无关及速率相关行为；扩展普雷萨赫神经网络进一步融合退化型滞回算子、非线性映射和输入速率信息。

Conclusion: 神经网络滞回模型从基础记忆表达逐渐发展到能够综合处理多种复杂滞回特征。综述指出，扩展普雷萨赫神经网络的适用范围较广，可兼顾马辛与非马辛、对称与非对称、一致与非一致，以及速率无关与速率相关滞回。

Abstract: Hysteresis is a complex nonlinear phenomenon characterized by memory dependence, path dependence, and, in many physical systems, degradation, non-congruency, asymmetry, and rate dependence. This review examines the development and capabilities of Prandtl neural networks (PNNs), Preisach neural networks (PreisachNNs), generalized Prandtl neural networks (GPNNs), the neuro-Madelung model (NMM), extended Preisach neural networks (EPNNs), and dynamic plastic continuous neural networks (DPCNNs). Particular attention is given to their mechanisms for representing memory, network architectures, activation functions, training algorithms, treatment of deterioration and rate dependence, and applications to nonlinear structural and material systems. The PNN introduced hysteretic memory directly into neural activation through stop operators and demonstrated accurate nonlinear dynamic analysis of structures. Preisach-NNs subsequently extended this concept through an additional nonlinear hidden layer to represent non-Masing hysteresis. GPNNs introduced deteriorating-stop neurons to capture stiffness and strength degradation and non-congruent hysteresis. A different generalization was provided by the NMM, which combined Madelung's hysteresis rules with multilayer feedforward neural networks to represent diverse rate-independent and rate-dependent behaviors. These developments culminated in the EPNN, which combines deteriorating hysteretic operators, nonlinear neural mappings, and input-rate information to accommodate Masing and nonMasing, symmetric and asymmetric, congruent and noncongruent, and rate-independent and rate-dependent hysteresis.

</details>


### [126] [GridForge: An Open Physics-Informed Digital Twin and Benchmark Suite for UK 11 kV Underground Distribution Cables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23241)
*Lilliane Linnet Musoke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出开放式物理信息数字孪生与基准套件，填补英国配电电缆监测领域缺乏公开数据、参考模型和统一评测协议的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 英国配电网运营商积累了大量电缆遥测数据，但缺少可公开复用的基准数据集、共享参考模型及统一评测方法，因而难以比较数据密集型模型在故障诊断、预测和维护决策中的实际价值。

Method: 研究构建了一套开放框架，包含依据相关国际电工委员会标准验证的稳态与瞬态热模型；覆盖四类典型英国十一千伏热特征和四种状态模式的合成数据集，共六十四条电缆、三百二十电缆年；满足标准代数热平衡约束的物理信息神经网络代理模型；以及包含五项任务和三种基线模型的基准套件。数值参数取自公开标准，代码采用麻省理工学院许可证发布。

Result: 神经网络代理模型在独立留出集上相对于解析参考模型的均方根误差为零点一四八摄氏度，训练约需两分钟。在当前测试划分中，只有虚拟传感器任务能够区分不同基线模型，其余任务结果相同或无法定义。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了可公开验证、可复现的配电电缆热建模与模型评测基础，有助于推动维护规划和优先级排序等运营决策研究。不过，当前基准任务对模型的区分能力有限，后续需要针对各任务完善基线模型及相应逻辑。

Abstract: The UK distribution-cable monitoring sector is data-rich but framework-poor: each Distribution Network Operator (DNO) collects megabytes of telemetry per asset per day, but there is no public benchmark dataset, no shared reference model, and no commonly agreed evaluation protocol for benchmarking sectoral, data-intensive models for operational decision support; notably for maintenance planning and prioritisation. We address this gap with GridForge, an open-source physics-informed digital twin and benchmark suite for fault diagnosis and prediction, integrating four building blocks: (i) a verified steady state and transient thermal model based on IEC 60287 and IEC 60853; (ii) a calibrated synthetic dataset of 64 cables (covering 320 cable-years) representing four canonical UK 11 kV thermal archetypes and four condition modes; (iii) a Physics-Informed Neural Network (PINN) surrogate that satisfies the algebraic balance defined by IEC 60287 during training and reaches an RMSE of 0.148 °C on an independent held out set against the reference analytical oracle; and (iv) a five-task benchmark suite that includes three reference baseline models (pure physics, gradient-boosted trees, and the PINN). All numerical parameters are sourced from public standards, the code is licensed under the MIT licence, and the trained PINN converges in only two minutes (as run on the computing platform in Section 10.1), enabling independent verification by DNO engineering and research practitioners. At present only the virtual-sensor task (T4) discriminates between the reference baselines; the other tasks tie or are undefined on this test split, so extending the baselines with task-specific logic is a priority future enhancement. For open validation, the dataset, test sets and models are made available at https://github.com/mlily2024/Gridforge

</details>


### [127] [Biologically-Inspired Spike Encoding and Optimization for Spiking Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23242)
*Ayaan Qayyum,Bruce Yi Bu,Zicong Li,Jingfeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究通过生物启发式脉冲编码、可学习阈值和二阶优化，提升脉冲神经网络的准确率与脉冲稀疏性，且无需增加模型规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲神经网络理论上具有能效优势，但视觉任务中的朴素频率编码往往产生不够稀疏的脉冲表示，限制了其实际效益与识别性能。研究旨在借助更合适的编码和训练方法，缩小其与非脉冲网络之间的准确率差距。

Method: 比较频率编码、潜伏期编码、动态视觉传感器式差分调制及高斯差分滤波编码，并结合鲁瑟自适应阈值替代梯度和克罗内克因子近似曲率二阶优化。在手写数字和时尚物品图像数据集上评估这些方法。

Result: 生物启发式编码在模型容量受限时带来最大的准确率提升，并提高参数效率。高斯差分与差分调制产生的脉冲序列明显比频率编码稀疏。克罗内克因子近似曲率优化相较于自适应矩估计优化带来幅度不大但稳定的准确率提升；可学习的逐神经元阈值将时尚物品数据集准确率从百分之八十七点三七提升至百分之八十八点一三。

Conclusion: 生物学启发的输入预处理与改进训练策略能够在不增加模型规模的前提下，改善脉冲神经网络的准确率、稀疏性或参数效率，为提升其视觉任务表现提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) offer theoretical energy-efficiency advantages over conventional deep networks, but realizing these advantages on vision tasks requires sparse spike representations that naive rate-coding methods fail to provide. We combine biologically motivated spike-encoding schemes with two training improvements: Rouser adaptive-threshold surrogate gradients and Kronecker-Factored Approximate Curvature (K-FAC) second-order optimization. The spike encoding schemes experimented with are rate coding, latency coding, DVS-style delta modulation, and Difference-of-Gaussians (DoG) filtering. Experiments on MNIST and Fashion-MNIST demonstrate that biologically inspired encoders yield the largest accuracy gains in capacity-constrained regimes and serve as an effective parameter-efficiency lever. DoG and delta modulation encoders produce significantly sparser spike trains than rate coding. K-FAC yields modest but consistent accuracy improvements over Adam-trained baselines, and Rouser's learnable per-neuron thresholds improve Fashion-MNIST accuracy from 87.37% to 88.13% by enabling homeostatic firing-rate regulation. Biologically grounded pre-processing methods can help close the accuracy gap between SNNs and non-spiking baselines without increasing model size.

</details>


### [128] [Goemans's Unsplittable-Flow Conjecture: The Anatomy of AI-Assisted Counterexample Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23244)
*Jingwei Ge,Na Xie,Li Li,Jiayang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一套有理论依据的反例构造流程，解释并推广了戈曼斯猜想的七顶点反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 戈曼斯猜想认为，任意单源分数流都可转化为不可分流，在不增加总成本的同时，使任一弧的负载增量不超过最大需求。近期发现的七顶点反例推翻了该猜想，但其拓扑与需求、分数流及成本为何共同导致失败，反例是否存在更广泛的结构规律，以及人工智能辅助搜索为何有效，都尚未得到解释。

Method: 从一种必然不会产生失败的有根树形结构出发，逐步加入进入终端的捷径弧。由此得到的负载约束确定有限种可行路径选择；对这些选择取凸包，构造冲突多面体，并据此精确刻画需求、分数流和成本产生反例的条件。

Result: 该流程从简单树形结构出发，重新得到瑞宾的拓扑，并刻画该拓扑支持的全部反例。实验表明，改变起始树形结构和捷径弧位置后，聊天机器人能够构造出与原例拓扑显著不同的反例；从另外九十三种有根树形结构出发，也找到了有效构造。

Conclusion: 研究将孤立的反例转化为可系统分析和推广的构造理论，揭示了反例出现所依赖的结构条件。理论还说明，盲目枚举难以发现原例，而瑞宾与聊天机器人的互动处于盲目搜索与完整理论指导构造之间。

Abstract: Goemans conjectured that any fractional single-source flow could be converted into an unsplittable flow without increasing total cost and while increasing any arc load by at most the largest demand. Recently, Dmitry Rybin announced a seven-vertex counterexample found through an interaction with ChatGPT, which disproved this long-standing conjecture. However, the interaction did not reveal why this particular topology and its demand, fractional-flow, and cost choices work together, whether the example belongs to a broader family, or which structural features determine where counterexamples can occur. It also left unclear why the AI-assisted search succeeded where earlier human efforts had not. In this paper, we develop a forward counterexample construction pipeline. It starts from an arborescence on which failure is impossible and adds terminal-entering shortcut arcs. The resulting load constraints define a finite set of admissible route choices; their convex hull, the conflict polytope, yields exact conditions for demands, fractional flows, and costs. Starting from a simple arborescence, the pipeline selects Rybin's topology and characterizes all counterexamples it supports. Experiments further demonstrate the pipeline's transferability: when instructed to follow it while varying the initial arborescence and shortcut placement, ChatGPT produced counterexamples on topologies genuinely different from Rybin's and found valid constructions starting from 93 other rooted arborescence shapes. The theory also explains why the original example was difficult to discover through blind enumeration and suggests that Rybin's interaction lies between blind enumeration and a complete theory-guided construction.

</details>


### [129] [Response-Relevant Information Is Not Sufficient for Neural Training-State Equivalence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23245)
*Truong Xuan Khanh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究以训练受扰后的响应来定义训练状态等价。若干模型描述指标能预测后续响应，但尚未证明它们可可靠地用于判断两个检查点是否处于相同训练状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅凭模型当前的输出或其他响应相关信息，不能证明两个神经网络处于相同训练状态。作者希望建立一种可操作的等价标准：在特定干预及后续训练时段内，两个检查点受到扰动后的响应相似，才视为等价。

Method: 作者在从头训练的一层变换器上开展模加法学习实验，并向训练过程施加权重衰减脉冲，以检验响应是否具有可重复性。随后考察十五种涉及权重谱、表征几何和优化器几何的描述指标，评估它们在权重范数与损失之外，对后续响应偏差的预测能力；同时使用条件安慰剂和校准后的正向对照检验匹配效果及实验灵敏度。

Result: 在五百步时，权重衰减脉冲引发的响应具有较高可重复性，状态间相关系数为零点九三四，说明目标可测量。十五种描述指标在开发数据集上包含超出权重范数和损失的响应相关信息，并能跨随机种子区块预测留出数据中的响应偏差。按未来响应匹配检查点时，表面优势为百分之十六点一，但与保留各指标对常规观测量拟合依赖关系的条件安慰剂相比，并无显著区分；正向对照则表明，这不太可能只是实验不够灵敏所致。

Conclusion: 描述指标可以携带并预测与训练响应有关的信息，但这并不意味着这些信息在成对比较所需的几何结构中得到组织。因此，预测单个检查点的响应偏差，不足以证明这些指标能够判断两个检查点是否处于等价训练状态。

Abstract: Response-relevant information is not sufficient evidence that two neural networks are in the same training state. We define training-state equivalence operationally: two checkpoints are equivalent, relative to an intervention family and horizon, if they respond alike when training is perturbed and continues. On fresh runs of a one-layer transformer learning modular addition, response to a weight-decay pulse is highly reproducible relative to between-checkpoint differences (rho_state = 0.934 at 500 steps), establishing the target as measurable. Fifteen descriptors of weight spectra, representation geometry and optimiser geometry carry within-run response-relevant information beyond weight norm and loss on a development dataset, predicting held-out response deviation across seed blocks. But their apparent 16.1% advantage at matching checkpoints by future response does not separate from a conditional placebo preserving each descriptor's fitted dependence on the conventional observables, and a calibrated positive control makes simple insensitivity an unlikely explanation. Information a decoder can extract need not be organised in the pairwise geometry that similarity judgments require.

</details>


### [130] [Chebyshev Polynomial Zonotope Abstractions for Certified Reachability of Learning-Enabled Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23246)
*Peng Xie,Amr Alanwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文比较了泰勒展开与切比雪夫多项式在非线性系统可达集计算中的表现：前者受复平面奇点限制，能处理的集合宽度存在上限；后者没有这一宽度上限，并能随多项式次数增加而几何级提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非线性可达集方法多以截断泰勒展开及严格余项传播集合。但一个泰勒多项式必须在整个输入集合上保持精度，其适用范围受收敛圆盘限制。对控制器中的有理函数和门控函数而言，复平面上的极点或奇点可能靠近实轴；门控越陡，奇点越近，因此可认证的集合宽度越小，尤其难以覆盖控制器切换边界附近的区域。

Method: 论文证明了泰勒方法与切比雪夫方法之间的能力差异：在给定次数下，泰勒展开无法认证宽于其收敛性上限的集合；而将切比雪夫近似嵌入同一多项式区间胞计算流程后，不存在相同的宽度上限，且可覆盖宽度随次数增加呈几何增长。作者还给出严格的闭式余项界，包括针对软最大函数的多项式近似余项，并提出预先判断何时更换基函数可能带来收益的适用性图谱。

Result: 实验测得的宽度边界与理论公式吻合度为百分之八十九至百分之九十四。闭环系统中，切比雪夫方法在泰勒方法无法收敛的情况下仍能给出证书，并使部分规格从无法判定变为已证明。对经训练的分类器，包括真实手写数字样本，切比雪夫方法还能认证泰勒基底无法覆盖的输入区域。所有已认证结论均通过大规模蒙特卡洛测试，未观察到违反情况。

Conclusion: 研究表明，泰勒方法的局限并非单纯由计算资源不足导致，而是受到复平面奇点所造成的集合宽度上限约束。对于具有有理项或陡峭门控的系统，切比雪夫抽象可显著扩展可认证范围；适用性图谱则有助于事先判断这种基函数替换是否值得采用。

Abstract: Nearly all current methods for computing reachable sets of nonlinear dynamics propagate them with truncated Taylor expansions plus rigorous remainders. The expansion is pointwise: once its argument is a whole set rather than a point, one polynomial must stay accurate across that set, and it reaches only as far as its disc of convergence. The limit binds hardest on the rational terms and sigmoid gates of modern controllers: smooth as these look on the real line, in the complex plane they blow up at points just off the real axis, closer as a gate sharpens, and those points cap the set width a Taylor expansion can cover, most tightly on the switching surface where the controller decides. We prove a dichotomy: no Taylor degree certifies sets wider than that cap, while a Chebyshev abstraction of equal degree, anchored to the whole set in the same polynomial-zonotope pipeline, has no cap and gains width at a geometric rate in degree. Measured walls match the formulas to between 89 and 94 percent. We give closed-form rigorous remainders, among them the first polynomial softmax transformer; existing sound treatments are linear relaxations. On closed loops, certificates survive where the Taylor ones diverge, and specifications flip from unknown to proven; on trained classifiers up to real MNIST digits, the Chebyshev frontier certifies input territory the Taylor basis surrenders. Every certified claim passed large Monte-Carlo harnesses with zero violations, and an applicability map states in advance where the basis provably cannot matter.

</details>


### [131] [Budgeted Proper Loss Design for Uncertain Decision Thresholds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23247)
*Ankit Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文在给定权重下限与总量预算的条件下，设计一种适当损失，使代理损失后悔到阈值平均决策后悔的最坏情形转换系数尽可能小。最优权重由阈值密度族的上包络经缩放并按下限截断得到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同一个二元概率估计可能供具有不同错误成本的决策者使用，因此决策者关注的概率阈值各不相同。虽然适当评分规则可强调特定阈值，但仅依据阈值分布的平均情况设计损失，未必能保证面对指定分布族中的任一情况时都具有较小的最坏后悔。论文旨在预算受限时，为这类不确定的阈值需求设计有稳健保证的损失。

Method: 将适当损失表示为可积的权重函数，并要求其逐点不低于指定下限、总质量固定。针对有限个阈值密度，推导使分布无关转换系数最小的权重：对这些密度的上包络进行缩放，并在预设下限处截断；再通过一维求根确定归一化参数。论文还证明该系数的尖锐性与解的最优性，分析阈值密度设定有误时的稳定性，并讨论原子型阈值分布带来的限制。

Result: 理论分析给出了预算约束下的最优权重及其保证。精确直方图实现和跨二十个随机种子的两组模拟实验显示，优化后的权重在后悔转换系数上优于均匀权重和平均密度权重。不过，使用拟合模型时的表现并非在所有情况下都更好。

Conclusion: 该研究提供的是针对明确阈值分布族和权重预算的损失设计保证：它优化最坏情形下代理后悔与决策后悔之间的转换，而不是保证预测模型的实际表现普遍提升。

Abstract: A binary probability estimate may be used by several decision makers with different error costs. Proper scoring rules can emphasize selected probability thresholds, but a single average threshold distribution need not give a good worst-case regret guarantee. We study the choice of a proper loss when the threshold distribution belongs to a finite specified family. Among integrable loss weights with a positive pointwise floor and a fixed total mass, we derive the weight that minimizes the sharp, distribution-free multiplier from surrogate regret to threshold-averaged decision regret. The solution clips a rescaled upper envelope of the threshold densities at the prescribed floor; its normalization is found by a one-dimensional root search. We give proofs of sharpness, optimality, and stability under density misspecification, as well as a limitation for atomic threshold laws. An exact histogram implementation and two synthetic experiments across 20 seeds verify the construction. The optimized multiplier improves over uniform and average-density weights, while fitted-model performance does not improve uniformly. The result is a loss-design guarantee under an explicit budget, rather than a general claim of predictive superiority.

</details>


### [132] [Exploring Parkinson's Diagnosis Using Machine Learning Techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23248)
*Farhaan Qazi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究比较多种机器学习方法对帕金森病进行分类；决策树和逻辑回归准确率最高，支持向量机对患者的检出敏感度最高，但整体区分患者与健康对照的能力有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帕金森病是一种目前无法治愈的神经退行性疾病，及时、准确的诊断有助于疾病管理。研究旨在探索机器学习能否辅助帕金森病诊断，并评估不同算法在患者分类中的表现。

Method: 研究使用英国利兹教学医院国民保健体系信托机构提供的数据集，数据涉及五十八人。研究比较支持向量机、决策树、多层感知机、近邻算法、逻辑回归和随机森林，并评估采用与未采用粒子群优化的随机森林模型；主要依据分类准确率及敏感度等指标进行评价。

Result: 决策树和逻辑回归表现最佳，准确率约为百分之七十二至七十六；多层感知机表现最弱。各模型对帕金森病患者的识别敏感度较高，最高达百分之九十七点五六，其中支持向量机最高。然而，各模型区分帕金森病患者与健康对照的能力整体较弱。

Conclusion: 在该数据集中，多种机器学习方法具备一定的帕金森病患者识别能力，但结果尚不足以证明其能可靠地区分患者与健康人群。由于样本规模较小，仍需扩大数据量、开展进一步研究并改进模型，才能提升诊断表现并为帕金森病研究提供更有力的支持。

Abstract: In recent years, machine learning (ML) techniques have emerged as promising tools for accurate and timely diagnosis of Parkinson's disease (PD) which is an incurable neurodegenerative disorder. This study explores the integration of ML algorithms and PD diagnosis, highlighting their potential applications in various aspects of the disease. ML methods such as support vector machines, decision trees, multilayer perceptron, k-nearest neighbours, logistic regression and random forest (with and without particle swarm optimisation) were applied on a PD dataset obtained from Leeds Teaching Hospitals NHS Trust containing data from 58 people. In terms of accuracy, the Decision Tree and Logistic Regression performed the best with scores of 72-76% while multilayer perceptron was least effective. All the models were remarkable in classifying positive PD cases with sensitivity scores going up to 97.56%, with SVM classifier at the pinnacle. However, ability to classify healthy controls from PD cases was insignificant throughout the board. Although, all the employed models delivered reasonable results in categorising PD patients in the presented dataset; access to more data, further study and advancement of these ML approaches could lead to enhanced diagnostic outcomes, supplementing to an improved understanding of Parkinson's disease.

</details>


### [133] [Optimize the Cob, Then the Kernel: Optimizing Beam Search for Generative Recommendation Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23339)
*Shing Wai Pun,Kai Wu,Pengfei Xia,Hui Zang,Wei Guo,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文针对生成式推荐推理中的共享前缀重复计算与解码调度开销，提出一套无需自定义算子的整体优化方案，在昇腾处理器上将端到端延迟最多降低至原来的二十五分之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐与常规大语言模型服务不同，输入序列较长、束搜索宽度较大，而输出仅包含少量固定词元。现有服务引擎将各束作为独立序列批量处理，导致共享提示前缀的键值缓存被重复访问；此外，每一步束搜索都单独调用引擎，还带来主机端处理开销及分页注意力中的键值缓存块管理成本。

Method: 采用树注意力，将多个束沿序列维度拼接，以避免对共享前缀的重复键值缓存访问。在此基础上，结合预生成的局部注意力掩码、静态键值缓存分配、循环展开式生成调度、计算图编译、自动算子融合，以及受词表约束的解码，优化整个生成流程，且不依赖自定义算子。

Result: 在昇腾处理器上，该方案相较于一种主流大语言模型服务引擎，端到端延迟最多降低二十五倍；其生成延迟也达到与采用自定义算子的先进方案相当的水平。

Conclusion: 研究表明，生成式推荐推理的性能瓶颈不仅在算子本身，还涉及缓存访问、调度及运行时管理。应先对生成流水线进行整体优化，再考虑开发自定义算子。

Abstract: Generative recommendation (GR) inference differs substantially from conventional LLM serving, featuring long input sequences, wide beam search, and only a few fixed output tokens. Existing LLM serving engines such as vLLM process beams as batched independent sequences, causing redundant KV cache accesses over the shared prompt prefix. Furthermore, vLLM executes each beam search generation step as a separate engine invocation, incurring host-side beam processing overhead between decoding steps and substantial runtime KV block management in PagedAttention. Using Tree Attention, we concatenate beams along the sequence dimension to eliminate redundant KV accesses, and further optimize execution through pre-generated partial-attention masks, static KV cache allocation, loop-unrolled generation dispatch, TorchAir graph compilation, automatic operator fusion, and vocabulary-constrained decoding. Without the use of custom kernels, our approach reduces end-to-end latency by up to 25x over vLLM on Ascend NPUs, and achieves generation latency comparable to a SOTA solution using custom kernels, showing that the generation pipeline should be optimized holistically before resorting to custom kernel development.

</details>


### [134] [Variance-Optimal Counterfactual Policy Design for Sample-Efficient Online A/B Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24757)
*Shashank Gupta,Subhadeep Maji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种联合优化动作采样与效果估计的方法，以更少的线上流量、更准确地比较两种策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准线上对照实验的均值差估计虽无偏，但方差可能较高：当两种策略对相同动作赋予相近概率时，相关样本会增加噪声，却很少提供区分策略的信息。既有方法通常只在数据收集方案固定后降低估计方差；已有考虑策略重叠的设计也主要优化两种策略之间的整体流量比例，无法将流量重点分配到策略意见不一致的动作上。

Method: 研究联合设计动作级日志采样策略和处理效应估计方式。首先，基于成对离线策略评估，推导出在控制变量固定时、用于估计两种策略平均处理效应的方差最优动作级采样策略，并给出闭式解。其次，利用历史日志联合优化采样策略与控制变量，以进一步降低估计方差。

Result: 在模拟的策略对照实验中，该方法在所有评估设置下均比现有方差缩减方法更准确地估计平均处理效应；总体上还提高了统计功效，并降低选中较差策略的频率。摘要未提供具体数值或真实线上实验结果。

Conclusion: 通过把流量分配到更有助于区分策略的动作，并配合优化后的估计方法，可以提升策略对照实验的效率与决策可靠性，从而以显著更少的线上流量支持部署决策。

Abstract: Online A/B testing is the standard approach for evaluating changes to user-facing systems, such as a new recommendation or ranking policy, before deployment. While the difference-in-means estimator of a standard A/B test is unbiased, it often suffers from high variance. When the treatment and production policies choose the same actions with similar probabilities, the resulting observations can add noise while providing little information about the difference between the policies. Consequently, A/B tests can require large amounts of traffic and long durations to detect small treatment effects. Existing variance-reduction methods mainly improve the estimator after the data-collection scheme has been fixed, and recent overlap-aware designs only optimize a single traffic split between the two policies; thereby, they cannot direct traffic to the actions on which the policies disagree.
  In this paper, we jointly optimize how actions are sampled and how the resulting observations are used to estimate the treatment effect. Our contributions are two-fold. First, building on pairwise off-policy evaluation, we derive the variance-optimal action-level logging policy for estimating the average treatment effect between two policies, which has a closed-form solution for any fixed control variate. Second, we use historical logs to jointly optimize the logging policy and a control variate to further reduce variance. Our experimental results in simulated policy A/B tests show that our designs estimate the average treatment effect more accurately than existing variance reduction methods on every evaluated setting, while generally improving statistical power and reducing how often the worse policy is selected. As a result, our designs enable practitioners to reach reliable deployment decisions with considerably less online traffic.

</details>


### [135] [Confidence-Based Materials Data Quality Assessment via Domain Knowledge and Graph-Structured Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23342)
*Sihan Liaing,Ji Zhang,Kehan Wang,Zihao Gao,Wei Ma,Yangang Wang,Zongguo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出融合材料领域知识与图学习的数据质量评估框架，以连续置信度衡量样本可靠性；实验表明，该框架能识别多类数据问题，并提升材料性质预测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料机器学习依赖来源多样的大规模数据，但实验、模拟和数据库整合过程中常出现测量误差、计算失败、缺失或重复记录，以及违反物理化学规律的数据。此类问题可能仍具统计合理性，难以通过传统离群点检测发现，进而损害模型可靠性。

Method: 将数据质量建模为连续置信度估计，而非二元异常判别，并支持逐样本评估及基于历史参照的批次比较。首先依据材料领域规则与统计证据，构建九维特征质量先验，并进行考虑重复记录的样本初始化；随后利用样本—特征二部图传播和弱监督图注意力机制修正置信度；另设记忆图注意力模块，用于基于历史数据进行参照评分。

Result: 在二十三个异常检测基准数据集上，通用模块的平均受试者工作特征曲线下面积为零点八六六。在五次重复的混合污染实验中，规则校准使材料项目数据上的测试决定系数由零点五三八±零点零零八提升至零点六一六±零点零一四；图精炼使平均精确率由零点九四三±零点零零四提升至零点九五三±零点零零四，而规则校准方案在所比较的数据筛选配置中取得最高平均测试决定系数。另在五次重复的材料原子模拟子集实验中，规则筛选使决定系数由零点五九零±零点零五三提升至零点八八零±零点零二七。

Conclusion: 该框架将领域规则、统计证据、图结构学习与历史参照结合，为材料数据提供细粒度、可比较的置信度评估。实验结果显示，规则校准和图精炼均有助于改善数据筛选及下游性质预测，但不同筛选配置的表现存在差异。

Abstract: The rapid development of materials machine learning relies on large-scale, heterogeneous datasets, yet model reliability is often compromised by imperfect data from diverse experiments, simulations, and database integration. Real-world materials databases may contain measurement errors, failed calculations, missing or duplicate records, and hidden violations of physicochemical constraints. Such problems are difficult to identify because corrupted samples may remain statistically plausible rather than appearing as explicit outliers. In this paper, we propose a confidence-based materials data quality assessment framework that integrates domain knowledge with graph-structured learning. Data quality is formulated as a continuous confidence estimation problem rather than a binary anomaly identification task, enabling sample-level assessment and historical-reference-based batch comparison. A nine-dimensional feature-quality prior and duplicate-aware sample initialization are calibrated using materials domain rules and statistical evidence. Confidence is further refined through sample-feature bipartite graph propagation and weakly supervised graph attention, while a Memory-GAT module provides historical reference scoring. Experiments on 23 ODDS benchmark datasets show that the generic module achieves an average AUC-ROC of 0.866. In five repeated mixed-contamination experiments on mp\_20, rule calibration improves HistGradientBoostingRegressor test $R^2$ from $0.538\pm0.008$ to $0.616\pm0.014$. Graph refinement increases average precision from $0.943\pm0.004$ to 0.953\pm0.004$, while the rule-calibrated configuration has the highest mean test $R^2$ among the evaluated screening configurations. Five repeated MACE subset experiments further show an increase in $R^2$ from $0.590\pm0.053$ to $0.880\pm0.027$ after rule-based screening.

</details>


### [136] [BiMa-ECGSR: reconstructing the 12-lead ECG from reduced lead sets with bidirectional state-space models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23345)
*Ugo Lomoio,Pierangelo Veltri,Pietro Hiram Guzzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究提出一种双向状态空间模型，可由少量心电图导联重建12导联信号；其效果优于线性基线，且多导联重建在诊断分类上的表现接近真实心电图，但无法补回输入中不存在的信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴设备和便携监测器通常只记录少数心电图导联，而多数诊断工具依赖完整的12导联心电图。研究旨在将少导联记录转换为可供现有12导联工具使用的信号，同时检验重建对诊断任务是否有实际价值。

Method: 研究使用双向状态空间编码器—解码器，从六种输入导联组合重建12导联心电图，并比较回归式重建与扩散式重建。模型在一个大型公开心电图数据集上训练，在同一数据集的测试集以及佐治亚州、查普曼—绍兴／宁波两个外部队列上评估；推理时仅使用实际测得的导联。评估包括重建信号与真实信号的皮尔逊相关系数，以及仅用真实心电图训练的分类器在重建信号上的诊断表现，并与最小二乘线性滤波基线比较。

Result: 仅输入Ⅱ导联时，模型对其余11个导联的汇总中位相关系数为0.914，高于线性基线的0.784；仅输入Ⅰ导联时分别为0.906和0.789。由于肢体导联可由Ⅰ、Ⅱ导联推导，多导联输入的比较聚焦于胸导联：输入Ⅰ、Ⅱ导联时为0.939，对照为0.849；输入Ⅰ、Ⅱ、胸前第1及第4导联时为0.979，对照为0.962。回归式模型在全部18种配置与队列组合中均优于线性基线，提升幅度为0.012至0.136，但增加胸导联后优势缩小。仅输入Ⅱ导联时，回归式模型优于扩散式模型；后者采用一步生成时相关系数为0.903，采用六步生成时为0.862。分类方面，真实心电图上的宏平均曲线下面积为0.912；仅Ⅱ导联重建信号为0.843，而四导联重建信号为0.908。外部队列中，三至四导联重建的分类表现与真实心电图相差不超过0.004。回归式模型规模约268万参数，在图形处理器上处理一段10秒记录约需45.8毫秒。

Conclusion: 双向状态空间重建能够提升少导联心电图的可用性，使部分重建记录可供既有12导联诊断工具处理；加入胸导联通常能提高重建质量，并使分类表现更接近真实心电图。不过，重建只是依据已测信号估计缺失导联，不能创造输入中不存在的生理信息，因此不能将其等同于实际测得的完整12导联心电图。

Abstract: Wearables and monitors record few electrocardiogram (ECG) leads; most diagnostic tools assume 12 leads. BiMa-ECGSR reconstructs the 12-lead ECG from reduced lead sets with a bidirectional Mamba state-space encoder--decoder, as regression (BiMa-SR) or diffusion (DiBiMa-SR). Models were trained on PTB-XL for six input sets (I; II; I+II; I+II+V1; I+II+V2; I+II+V1+V4), see only the measured leads at inference, and were tested on the PTB-XL test fold (2,198 records) and two external cohorts (Georgia, 10,275 records; Chapman-Shaoxing/Ningbo, 43,559). From lead II alone, the pooled median Pearson correlation (PCC) over the 11 predicted leads was 0.914 on PTB-XL, against 0.784 for a least-squares linear filter (lead I: 0.906 against 0.789). As limb leads are exactly derivable from I and II, multi-lead inputs were compared on predicted chest leads: 0.939 against 0.849 for I+II and 0.979 against 0.962 for I+II+V1+V4. BiMa-SR exceeded the linear comparator in all 18 configuration--cohort pairs (by 0.012--0.136), a margin that shrank as chest leads were added. From lead II, regression was more accurate than diffusion (DiBiMa-SR: 0.903 one-step, 0.862 with six DDIM steps). A 12-lead classifier trained only on real ECGs reached a superclass macro AUROC of 0.912 on real PTB-XL ECGs, 0.843 on reconstructions from lead II and 0.908 from I+II+V1+V4; externally, reconstructions from three or four leads scored within 0.004 of real ECGs. BiMa-SR (2.68\,M parameters) takes 45.8 ms per 10-s record on a GPU. Reconstruction makes reduced-lead recordings usable by 12-lead tools; it cannot add information absent from the measured leads.

</details>


### [137] [Implementing Binary Transformers with Approximate Memories with Permutation Invariance Enabled Selective (PIES) Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23358)
*Zhen Gao,Xiao Yin,Kexin Liang,Pedro Reviriego,Shanshan Liu,Fabrizio Lombardi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文评估了二值化变换器在存储器位错误下的可靠性，并提出低开销的选择性保护方法，可在较高错误率下恢复大部分性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变换器权重量化，尤其是二值化，能够降低存储、计算和功耗需求；采用近似存储器及激进电压缩放还可进一步降低存储功耗，但会引入位错误。现有二值化变换器研究主要关注任务精度，对存储错误下的可靠运行缺乏系统研究，因此有必要评估其可靠性并设计高效保护机制。

Method: 研究首先以双向编码器变换器为案例，评估二值化权重遭受存储位错误时的任务精度变化。随后提出一种利用置换不变性的选择性保护方案：利用变换器对行、列置换的容忍性，将关键权重集中分组，并仅对这些权重进行保护，以降低保护开销。

Result: 评估显示，只有在位错误率较高时（例如百分之五或百分之十），模型精度才会出现显著下降。仿真结果表明，所提出的方案在位错误率高达百分之十时，仍能恢复大部分由错误造成的性能损失，且开销显著低于传统纠错方案。

Conclusion: 通过针对关键权重进行低开销的选择性保护，二值化变换器能够更可靠地运行于近似存储器。研究结果支持在资源受限、低功耗的边缘设备上，采用激进电压缩放运行二值化变换器的可行性。

Abstract: Quantization is an effective technique to reduce memory storage, computation complexity and power for Transformers, and the binarization of the weights is important for meeting these requirements. Approximate memory can further reduce the power for memory, but it also introduces errors. Current research for binary Transformers focuses on task performance, but the model dependable operation against errors on the memory has not been well studied. In this paper, we first evaluate the dependability of binary Transformers with the Bidirectional Encoder Representations from Transformers (BERT) as a case study. A large accuracy loss occurs only at high Bit Error Rates (e.g. BER = 5% or 10%). Then we propose a novel Permutation Invariance Enabled Selective (PIES) scheme to protect the binary weights at very low overhead. This scheme exploits the invariance of Transformers to permutations in the rows and columns to group the critical weights and perform selective protection on those weights. Simulation results show that PIES can recover most of the performance loss caused by bit errors at BERs as high as 10%, at an overhead significantly lower than traditional error correction schemes. These results confirm the feasibility of running binary Transformers using approximate memory with aggressive voltage scaling on resource-limited low power edge devices. The code for PIES is released on github (https://github.com/YangZhutian/binary-transformer-prefix-repair).

</details>


### [138] [TRACE-MAS: Diagnosing and Repairing Collaboration Failures in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23362)
*Zekai Chen,Yihan Sun,Xunkai Li,Xun Wu,Xinmo Jin,Yinlin Zhu,Haodong Lu,Xuaner Chen,Rong-Hua Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种面向多智能体系统的细粒度故障诊断与修复框架，可定位交互层面的故障根源，并以最小干预恢复协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体系统防护主要检测单个智能体的异常。当故障源于智能体之间的消息交互或任务依赖时，这类方法难以充分诊断；而检测到异常也未必能揭示真正的故障来源。

Method: 框架从正常协作轨迹中学习执行模式，并监测智能体状态变化、消息传递及任务依赖演化的偏离。随后通过考虑故障来源的归因方法识别责任执行环节，并选择尽可能小的干预措施，在保留任务关键能力的同时恢复协作。

Result: 在五个多智能体系统基准上的实验表明，该方法相较于强基线防护方案，根因定位准确率提升百分之六十点二，并在任务恢复方面持续取得更好表现。

Conclusion: 多智能体系统的可靠防护需要从单体异常检测拓展到对状态、消息与任务依赖等交互过程的联合诊断。该框架通过来源定位和最小化修复，提升了故障诊断与任务恢复能力。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) rely on not only individual agent behaviors but also the interactions that connect agents, including message exchanges and task dependencies. Existing MAS safeguards mainly perform agent-level anomaly detection, which becomes insufficient when failures originate from agent interactions and detected anomalies do not necessarily reveal their true sources. To address this limitation, we propose \textbf{TRACE-MAS}, a finer-grained framework for diagnosing and repairing MAS failures. TRACE-MAS learns normal collaborative execution patterns from benign trajectories and detects deviations in agent state changes, message transitions, and task dependency evolutions. It further identifies responsible execution elements through source-aware attribution and selects minimal interventions to restore collaboration while preserving task-critical capabilities. Experiments on five MAS benchmarks demonstrate that TRACE-MAS significantly improves root-cause localization accuracy by \textbf{60.2}\% and achieves consistent gains in task recovery over strong MAS safeguards.

</details>


### [139] [QDA: Closed-Loop LLM Quantization Design with Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23388)
*Xingyang Li,Tianlang Zhao,Song Han,Ligeng Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一套自动化量化系统，可为不同模型和精度搜索训练后量化方案，并以更快的评估和统一的方法库降低搜索成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型通常通过训练后量化降低部署成本，但最佳方案会因模型和精度而异，依赖专家逐个调整既耗时，也可能效果不佳。量化推理模型的完整评估需要数十个图形处理器小时，不同代码库中的方法也难以直接移植到新架构。

Method: 系统使用智能代理逐轮构建和改进量化方案，并提供兼容多种架构的公开量化方法库。为加速评估，它在代理不可见的推理轨迹上，比较候选模型与原始模型输出分布的库尔贝克—莱布勒散度，数分钟内筛选方案；再对有潜力的方案进行长文本生成测试，以发现重复循环等失效问题。

Result: 在四个推理模型上，每次搜索耗用六十四个图形处理器小时。相较最强公开方案，该系统在权重四位、激活值十六位，权重四位、激活值八位，以及权重三位、激活值十六位设置下，平均准确率分别提高零点九、一点四和二点二个百分点。在三千二百亿参数的混合专家模型、英伟达四位浮点权重与激活值设置下，准确率达到八位浮点模型的百分之九十八点三，高于最佳厂商检查点的百分之九十六点九。所有量化检查点均可使用现有推理内核运行，且没有额外推理开销。

Conclusion: 该系统将量化方案设计、快速筛选和最终验证整合为自动化流程，在有限搜索预算下取得优于现有公开方案的效果，并降低方法适配新模型架构的成本。作者计划公开方法库、评估器和实验框架。
