Method: 作者提出“主导对齐子空间”，先对海森矩阵进行完整特征分解，再依据特征方向与梯度的对齐程度选择子空间，而不是按特征值大小筛选。研究以抛物型、椭圆型和双曲型微分算子构成的物理信息神经网络为测试平台，涵盖泊松方程、热方程、波动方程和黏性伯格斯方程，并在该子空间中结合线搜索进行训练；此外还分析了该子空间内的高斯—牛顿结构。

Result: 在上述基准中，即使投影后的海森矩阵仍然病态，限制于主导对齐子空间的训练仍能达到与全空间训练相当的效果。结果表明，优化收敛并不需要利用海森矩阵的所有特征方向，而只需一个低维且与梯度对齐的方向集合。

Conclusion: 研究指出，刻画神经网络训练动力学时，梯度对齐程度可能比曲率大小更关键。主导对齐子空间既提供了理解优化过程的工具，也为设计利用训练相关低维结构的优化器提供了依据；其适用范围及实际算法价值仍有待进一步研究。

Abstract: The search for a low-dimensional subspace that governs neural network training has been a persistent goal in optimization. Yet subspaces defined by Hessian eigenvalue magnitude, such as the dominant/bulk decomposition underlying Dom-SGD and Bulk-SGD, have repeatedly failed to capture where optimization takes place, and adaptive Krylov-based alternatives built on energy-fraction thresholds show promise but still struggle at lower eigenmodes. We argue this failure is structural: the directions of highest curvature are not, in general, the directions along which training moves. We introduce the dominant aligned subspace (DAS), constructed from a full eigendecomposition of the Hessian but selected by gradient alignment rather than eigenvalue magnitude, as a tool for understanding optimization dynamics and a foundation for designing methods that exploit them. To expose this structure under the hardest conditions, we use physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) as a testbed: differential operators from the parabolic, elliptic, and hyperbolic classes induce severely ill-conditioned, anisotropic loss landscapes. Across Poisson, heat, wave, and viscous Burgers benchmarks DAS-restricted training with a line search matches full-space training even where the projected Hessian remains ill-conditioned, showing empirically that convergence does not require all Hessian eigendirections but only a low-dimensional, gradient-aligned subset. We extend the analysis to Gauss-Newton within the DAS and discuss what this structure implies for future subspace-aware optimizers.

</details>


### [173] [TabBioMed: A Large-Scale Benchmark for Biomedical Tabular Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23779)
*Pau Mateo-Bernadó,Saivenkata Nagavyjayanthi Polapragada,David Bonet,Pol Arbiol Rakuljic,Laia Mogas Pladevall,Marçal Comajoan Cara,Jesus Gonzalez-Ferrer,Jordi Abante,Daniel Mas Montserrat,David Haussler,Alexander G. Ioannidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出覆盖九十四个公开生物医学表格数据集的大规模基准，系统比较二十三类模型，指出最佳模型取决于数据特征、任务类型与计算预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习正在推动生物医学预测，但结构化生物医学数据的评估仍较为零散。数据往往具有维度高、样本量大、缺失值多和类别不平衡等特点，且缺乏统一、可复现的比较框架，因而难以可靠地选择模型或评估新方法。

Method: 研究构建了生物医学表格学习基准，整理九十四个公开数据集，涵盖电子健康记录、药物发现、基因组学、转录组学、蛋白质组学、代谢组学、单细胞组学和系统生物学。基准采用标准化且适配数据集特征的预处理与评估流程，支持分类、回归和多目标任务，并比较二十三类模型，包括经典基线、梯度提升树、表格神经网络架构和表格基础模型。

Result: 总体而言，表格基础模型这一类模型的预测表现最强，但优势取决于具体数据与任务条件。树模型和经过调优的多层感知机仍具有较强竞争力；当推理成本或训练成本受到限制时，它们往往更占优势。研究结果表明，不同数据情境下的模型表现差异显著。

Conclusion: 生物医学表格学习不存在适用于所有场景的单一最优模型。模型选择应综合考虑数据特征、任务类型与计算预算。该基准通过公开整理后的数据集、数据划分、预处理代码、基线模型和评估结果，为可复现的实际模型选择及后续方法研究提供了基础。

Abstract: Recent advances in machine learning have accelerated biomedical prediction, but evaluation on structured biomedical data remains fragmented, especially for datasets with high dimensionality, large sample size, missingness, and class imbalance. We introduce TabBioMed, a large-scale benchmark of 94 curated public biomedical tabular datasets spanning electronic health records, drug discovery, genomics, transcriptomics, proteomics, metabolomics, single-cell omics, and systems biology. TabBioMed unifies these datasets through a standardized, dataset-aware preprocessing and evaluation framework, enabling reproducible comparison across classification, regression, and multi-target tasks. We benchmark 23 models across classical baselines, gradient-boosted trees, neural tabular architectures, and tabular foundation models. Foundation models deliver the strongest family-level predictive performance. However, their gains are regime-dependent: tree-based models and tuned multilayer perceptrons remain highly competitive, often dominating under inference-time or training-cost constraints. Overall, TabBioMed shows that biomedical tabular learning is strongly context-dependent, with optimal model choice depending on data regime, task type, and computational budget. We release TabBioMed with curated datasets and splits, preprocessing code, baselines, and results, providing a reproducible benchmark for practical model selection and future methods development in biomedical machine learning, available at https://github.com/AI-sandbox/TabBioMed.

</details>


### [174] [CAFE-AQI: Coverage-Aware Day-Ahead AQI Forecasting with Early Warning and Conformal Uncertainty in Bangladesh](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23815)
*Md Rafid Islam,Rafsan Jany,Rafid Bin Zaman,Mohammad Fahim Islam,Hafiz Abdur Rahman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出覆盖感知的次日空气质量预测框架，在处理重复轨迹与数据覆盖变化后，提升了空气质量指数预测、健康风险预警及区间估计表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有次日空气质量预测常假设各城市数据完整且相互独立。对孟加拉国大型数据集的审计发现，二〇二二年八月后数据覆盖显著扩大，且一百零三个城市标签实际仅对应六十一条独特空气质量指数轨迹。若忽略这些问题，重复记录可能不成比例地影响训练与评估，也会削弱结果的可信度。

Method: 提出覆盖感知框架，用于预测次日最高空气质量指数，并估计空气质量指数达到一百五十一及以上、即不健康或更差水平的概率。数据按时间划分为训练集（二〇二二至二〇二三年）、校准集（二〇二四年）和测试集（二〇二五年）；相同轨迹采用加权处理，以避免重复记录主导结果。模型结合时间特征、污染物特征和五邻居空间信息，并设置类别平衡的预警模块及按风险条件进行的保形校准，以支持预警和预测区间估计。

Result: 在对重复轨迹进行平衡处理的二〇二五年测试数据上，模型的平均绝对误差为九点七六，均方根误差为十五点零四，决定系数为零点九一零。相比持续性基线，平均绝对误差降低三点六三，百分之九十五置信区间为三点四九至三点七六；在全部六十一组轨迹中误差均较低。不健康空气预警的调和平均值为零点九零八，受试者工作特征曲线下面积为零点九八二；名义覆盖率为百分之九十的预测区间，实际覆盖率为百分之九十二点零九。排除一个完整空间区块进行训练后，平均绝对误差为十点六六。

Conclusion: 结果表明，显式处理数据覆盖变化和重复轨迹，有助于获得更可靠的预测评估。该轻量级框架兼具较好的次日数值预测、健康风险识别和区间覆盖表现；在未见空间位置上的误差仍保持较低，显示出一定的空间迁移能力。

Abstract: Next-day air quality forecasting often assumes city records are equally complete and independent. However, an audit of a large Bangladesh dataset revealed a sharp coverage expansion after August 2022 and showed that 103 city labels correspond to only 61 unique AQI trajectories. To address these issues, this paper presents CAFE-AQI, a coverage-aware framework for forecasting next-day maximum AQI. It also estimates the probability of Unhealthy-or-worse conditions (AQI >= 151). Data were divided chronologically into training (2022-2023), calibration (2024), and testing (2025). To prevent duplicate records from dominating training and evaluation, identical trajectories were weighted. The framework combines temporal and pollutant features with a five-neighbor spatial context. It also includes a class-balanced warning head and risk-conditional conformal calibration aimed at providing early warnings and prediction intervals. Evaluation on the duplicate-balanced 2025 data gave an MAE of 9.76, an RMSE of 15.04, and an R-squared value of 0.910 for CAFE-AQI. Its MAE was 3.63 AQI lower than persistence (95% CI: 3.49-3.76), with lower errors in each of the 61 trajectory groups. For unhealthy-air warnings, the F1-score was 0.908 and the AUROC was 0.982. The nominal 90% intervals covered 92.09% of observations. When an entire spatial block was excluded from training, the MAE remained 10.66, showing that the lightweight model transferred reasonably well to unseen locations.

</details>


### [175] [Online State-Representation Adaptation under Synthetic Interaction-Cue Remapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23836)
*Bin Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在预测关系发生变化的任务中，持续利用状态标签更新预测器，能显著提高后期行动准确率；但与其他决策方法的比较表明，提升不能全部归因于在线适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当线索与其所预测状态之间的关系改变时，智能体需要调整已学得的映射，才能选择合适行动。研究旨在检验持续更新预测器是否优于迁移时冻结参数，并厘清行动阈值和反馈信息对效果解释的影响。

Method: 研究在单步合成任务中比较持续利用状态标签学习的逻辑回归状态预测器与迁移时冻结系数的同一预测器，并设置直接策略和情境赌博机作为比较方法。实验涵盖十种条件、六个留出环境；每种条件进行一千二百九十六组配对运行，共产生一百二十九万条迁移记录。另检验依据开发阶段历史设置权重的效果。

Result: 后期行动准确率方面，在线更新预测器达到百分之九十三点三九，冻结预测器为百分之六十四点六三，配对差值为二十八点七六个百分点，百分之九十五环境自助法区间为八点七三至五十三点一九；在线更新在六个测试环境中均更优。直接策略达到百分之九十五点六五。情境赌博机准确率为百分之九十四点二三，且累计效用高于自适应预测器；其所选行动的奖励与行动结合后可揭示真实状态。依据开发历史设置的权重反而降低累计效用，差值为负三点四五，区间为负四点四八至负二点四三。

Conclusion: 持续更新状态预测器相较冻结参数，能在各测试环境中提升后期行动准确率。不过，直接策略通过改变行动阈值也取得更高准确率，而赌博机利用了能够反推出真实状态的奖励反馈，并获得更高累计效用。因此，在线适应确有价值，但其作用应与决策阈值设计及反馈所包含的信息区分开来。

Abstract: When cues change what they predict, continuing to update a learned mapping may help an agent choose the appropriate action. We tested this in a one-step synthetic task by comparing a logistic state predictor that continued to learn from state labels with the same predictor frozen at transfer. We evaluated ten conditions in six held-out environments, with 1,296 paired runs per condition and 1,296,000 transfer records in total. Late action accuracy was 93.39% with online updates and 64.63% with frozen coefficients, a paired difference of 28.76 [8.73, 53.19] percentage points (95% environment-bootstrap interval). A direct policy reached 95.65% using the same predictions with a different action threshold. A contextual bandit reached 94.23% and earned more cumulative utility than the adaptive predictor. The bandit used the reward of its chosen action, which in this task reveals the true state when combined with that action. Weights based on development history lowered cumulative utility relative to neutral weights (difference -3.45 [-4.48, -2.43]). The adaptive predictor outperformed its frozen counterpart in late accuracy in each of the six test environments. The other comparisons show how the choice of action threshold and the information available in feedback affect what can be attributed to online adaptation.

</details>


### [176] [Beyond Cross-Modal Reconstruction: Participant-Level Evaluation of sEMG-Kinematic Coupling in Post-Stroke Gait](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23856)
*Rafsan Jany,Md Rafid Islam,Mohammad Fahim Islam,Hafiz Abdur Rahman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，跨模态重建主要利用参与者共有的步态时相模式，未能证明存在明确的个体特异性神经肌肉耦合，也未提升临床评分预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨模态信号重建准确常被视为不同生理信号之间存在个体化耦合的证据，但模型可能只是捕捉了不同参与者共有的时间结构。研究旨在检验肌电活动与步态运动学之间的重建，是否真正反映个体特异性耦合，并评估学习到的不对称特征能否改善临床预测。

Method: 研究使用双向冻结模型，在八十二名健康参与者和四十三名脑卒中后参与者组成的留出样本中，进行肌电与步态运动学的双向跨模态重建。通过完全移除源模态、以其他参与者的源信号替换，以及基于步态周期位置、年龄和性别的模板基线，检验重建是否依赖个体信息。此外，将模型学习到的不对称特征与波形及导数不对称指标进行比较，以重复嵌套交叉验证预测行动能力评估量表得分。

Result: 完全移除源模态使重建均方根误差分别增加百分之六点九零和百分之十一点三八；但换用其他参与者的源信号仅使误差增加百分之一点六八和百分之一点六七，模板基线甚至令误差分别降低百分之二点八九和百分之零点五五。仅使用步速、年龄和性别时，平均绝对误差为三点六九三；加入绝对或有符号的学习不对称特征后，误差分别升至四点二二九和四点四二七。二十次重复评估中，所有不对称特征均未优于协变量基线；经协变量调整和错误发现率校正后，也没有局部学习特征保持显著。

Conclusion: 该模型的重建主要依赖参与者共享的步态周期模式，而非清晰的个体特异性神经肌肉耦合；学习到的不对称特征也没有带来临床预测价值。因此，不能仅凭重建准确度推断个体化生理联系，研究此类模型时应设置参与者层面的替换与对照检验。

Abstract: Accurate cross-modal reconstruction is often treated as evidence of physiological coupling, although it may simply reflect temporal patterns shared across participants. We tested whether frozen bidirectional models captured subject-specific neuromuscular coupling between electromyographic activity and gait kinematics in 82 held-out able-bodied and 43 post-stroke participants. Complete source-modality removal and cross-participant source substitution, along with a cycle-position-age-sex template baseline, were applied. Learned bilateral asymmetry was also compared with transparent waveform and derivative asymmetry for predicting Performance-Oriented Mobility Assessment (POMA) scores in the case of repeated nested cross-validation. Complete source removal increased reconstruction RMSE by 6.90% for electromyography-to-kinematics and 11.38% in the reverse direction. In contrast, cross-participant substitution increased RMSE by only 1.68% and 1.67%, respectively, while the template reduced RMSE by 2.89% and 0.55%. For POMA prediction, walking speed, age, and sex achieved an MAE of 3.693, compared with 4.229 and 4.427 after adding absolute and signed learned asymmetry, respectively. In all of the 20 repeated evaluations, all asymmetry representations performed worse than the covariate baseline. No localized learned feature remained significant after covariate adjustment and false-discovery-rate correction. The model mainly relied on shared gait-phase patterns, with no clear subject-specific coupling or added clinical value. Participant-level controls are therefore needed before reconstruction accuracy is treated as evidence of individualized physiology.

</details>


### [177] [A Rank-One Bypass of Fixed Linear Erasure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23880)
*Jai Kumar Sharma,Vanshaj Khattar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究表明，线性概念擦除只能保证当前内部表示对特定线性分类器不可读；若开放权重模型允许修改擦除层上游，攻击者可能重建目标信息。攻击能否成功，取决于擦除层是否保留了下游分类器可见的方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性概念擦除通常被视为降低模型敏感信息可读性的手段，但这种保证往往假设模型内部特征保持不变。开放权重模型允许攻击者修改产生特征的上游层，因此需要判断固定擦除层能否抵御此类篡改，以及攻击所需的条件和代价。

Method: 作者分析一个固定的仿射擦除层，并允许攻击者修改其上游模型。研究推导出攻击成功的充要条件：擦除层至少传递一个能被允许的下游分类器观察到的方向；对于非恒定的线性目标分数，攻击者只需进行秩一仿射更新，即可将目标分数写入该方向并通过擦除层传递。作者还评估了特征修改幅度、少量目标标签和无标签校准样本对攻击效果的影响。

Result: 在六组十类图像数据集的类别配对任务中，该攻击几乎恢复了擦除前的目标性能，同时可将已部署的良性线性分类头分数保持在数值误差范围内。使用八个目标标签和两千个无标签校准样本，并允许特征平均相对改动为百分之十时，测试准确率达到百分之七十九点八，相当于拟合目标分数相对随机猜测所获提升的百分之九十一点二；更严格的改动限制会显著降低攻击成功率。

Conclusion: 线性防护对于固定的当前表示仍是有效保证，但不能单独构成对可编辑开放权重模型的防篡改保证。评估概念擦除的安全性时，必须考虑上游参数是否可被修改，以及攻击者能够承受多大的特征改动代价。

Abstract: Linear concept erasure can make a target unreadable to a specified family of linear classifiers applied to a model's current internal features. In an open-weight release, however, an attacker can modify the layers that produce those features. We study a fixed affine erasure layer whose upstream model is allowed to change. If the layer transmits even one direction visible to an allowed downstream classifier, the attacker can write a target score into that direction and carry it through the layer. We show that this condition is also necessary, and that for a nonconstant linear target score the required change is a rank-one affine update. Across six CIFAR-10 class-pair tasks, the route restores nearly all pre-erasure target performance and can leave a deployed benign linear head's scores unchanged to numerical tolerance. We then price the attack: with eight target labels and 2,000 unlabelled calibration examples, a 10% mean relative feature edit yields 79.8% test accuracy, which is 91.2% of the fitted score's gain over chance; tighter edit limits sharply reduce success. Linear guardedness therefore remains a valid guarantee about the current representation, but it is not by itself a tamper-resistance guarantee for an editable open-weight model.

</details>


### [178] [Kernel Ridge Attention: Nonlinear Memory with Selective Forgetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23896)
*Haowei Chen,Yichen Wang,Congfu Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 核岭注意力是一种结合非线性特征与协方差引导记忆更新的递归序列模型；它以固定大小的推理状态实现自适应记忆，并在受控语言建模实验中展现出较强的预测与联想回忆能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列模型在记忆长程关联、根据输入灵活调整记忆，以及控制推理状态规模等方面存在局限。本文旨在构建一种兼具非线性表达能力、统计学原理支撑和高效递归推理的序列记忆机制。

Method: 模型通过递归岭回归存储键与值之间的关联，并利用历史键之间的相关性决定新观测如何更新记忆。内容依赖的奥恩斯坦—乌伦贝克转移机制控制记忆保留，并为后续写入恢复不确定性。该构造具有精确的时间核解释，推理状态大小不随上下文长度增长；同时，模型借助关联式分块映射与显式伴随运算支持并行训练，并保持递归更新形式。

Result: 在受控语言建模实验中，核岭注意力的预测质量优于所比较的注意力模型和递归基线；在自然联想回忆方面，它也强于评测中的递归模型。此外，实验表明该方法能够实现实用的分布式训练吞吐量。

Conclusion: 核岭注意力为非线性、自适应序列记忆提供了一种有原则的设计：它将递归岭回归、协方差引导更新与可并行训练相结合，并兼顾固定规模推理状态和有竞争力的实验表现。

Abstract: Kernel Ridge Attention (KRA) is a recurrent sequence mixer that combines learned nonlinear features with covariance-guided memory updates. It stores associations through recursive ridge regression, using correlations among past keys to determine how new observations modify memory. A content-dependent Ornstein--Uhlenbeck transition controls retention and restores uncertainty for future writes. This construction admits an exact temporal-kernel interpretation and maintains an inference state whose size is independent of context length. Associative block maps and explicit adjoints support parallel training while preserving the recursive update. In controlled language-modeling experiments, KRA improves prediction quality over attention and recurrent baselines, strengthens natural associative recall over the evaluated recurrent models, and achieves practical distributed training throughput. These properties provide a principled foundation for nonlinear, adaptive sequence memory.

</details>


### [179] [PGU-Decoder: Prior-Guided Unified Decoding for Industrial Road-Network Map Updating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23936)
*Yuguang Ning,Bingbing Kang,Nan Zhao,Zhixuan Li,Ze Zhao,Dongdong Liu,Wenjian Zhu,Zekai Liu,Junhui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出统一的道路网络地图更新解码框架，整合三类预测任务；在大规模部署中提升了预测质量，并显著降低人工核验成本、加快更新频率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 道路网络地图需要及时更新，涉及路口内部道路网络生成、合流与分流检测、可行驶区域检测，分别对应拓扑、几何和属性信息。传统做法为各任务建立独立流程，造成规则维护、质量保障和人工核验重复，推高运营成本，因此需要一种统一且适用于生产环境的方案。

Method: 提出生产级统一解码框架。先从道路网络几何中采样位置，由先验查询模块构造点级先验查询，并通过固定参考点在解码过程中保留位置信息，使模型能够聚焦周边上下文；再将点级表示聚合为实例级表示，利用关系图注意力网络建模实例间依赖，最后由任务专属预测头输出各项任务结果。

Result: 在中国超过360个城市的大规模自动化道路网络地图更新中，该框架持续优于七种基线方法。部署于高德地图后，三项任务的质量评分均超过98%，人工核验成本降低95%，地图更新频率从每周一次提升至每小时一次。

Conclusion: 该框架将道路网络地图更新中的多类语义预测任务统一到同一解码流程，在大规模真实业务中兼顾预测质量、更新效率与运营成本，验证了其作为工业级解决方案的实用性。

Abstract: Timely road-network map updates are essential to navigation and autonomous driving. Three key tasks are intra-intersection road network generation, merging/diverging detection, and drivable-area detection, covering topology, geometry, and attributes, respectively. Separate pipelines duplicate rule maintenance, quality assurance, and manual verification, increasing operational costs. We propose PGU-Decoder, a production-grade unified decoding framework for semantic prediction across these tasks. A Prior Query Module (PQM) constructs point-level prior queries from sampled locations on road-network geometries. Fixed reference points preserve positional priors throughout decoding, allowing the model to focus on surrounding context. Point-level representations are aggregated into instance-level representations, and a Relational Graph Attention Network (RGAT) captures inter-instance dependencies. Task-specific heads then generate predictions. We validate PGU-Decoder in large-scale automated road-network map updating across more than 360 cities in China, where it consistently outperforms seven baselines. Following deployment at AutoNavi Map, it achieves quality scores above 98% on all three tasks, reduces manual verification costs by 95%, and increases update frequency from weekly to hourly. These results demonstrate a practical and cost-effective industrial solution for road-network map updating.

</details>


### [180] [OMNIS+: Semantic RAN Slicing and Quantization for Dynamic Split Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23977)
*Langtian Qin,Avishkar Dnyaneshwar Kawadgave,Ian Andrew Harshbarger,Leïla Nasraoui,Carla Fabiana Chiasserini,Marco Levorato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出面向多小区边缘计算的语义无线接入网切片框架欧米尼斯增强版，通过动态神经网络切分、压缩与量化，以及分布式资源优化，提升推理准确率并降低服务质量违约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无线接入网切片方案通常采用静态计算模型，难以利用现代神经网络所支持的动态语义表示，因而无法灵活协调任务卸载中的通信负载与计算需求。与此同时，系统部分可观测，移动设备与边缘服务器的优化目标也各不相同。

Method: 提出欧米尼斯增强版框架，结合动态神经压缩、自适应量化和动态切分深度神经网络，并采用一种增强传输比特错误鲁棒性的“盒式”量化方案。移动设备侧通过考虑队列积压的李雅普诺夫控制与因果上下文多臂老虎机，联合选择网络分支和服务小区，在服务质量及队列稳定约束下提升推理准确率；边缘服务器侧通过分块坐标下降，联合分配带宽、图形处理器频率和调制编码方案，以提升关联设备中的最低推理准确率，并满足小区容量限制。

Result: 与先进基线方案相比，该框架降低了服务质量违约概率，最高降幅为摘要所述的“1.75”，并实现了更优的准确率与违约率权衡。摘要未明确说明该数值代表倍数还是其他度量。

Conclusion: 动态语义切分与压缩、量化机制能够拓展通信和计算资源的联合决策空间；结合移动设备与边缘服务器的分布式协同优化，可在多小区边缘计算中兼顾推理准确率、服务质量和队列稳定性。

Abstract: Edge computing enables resource-constrained devices to execute machine learning (ML) applications via task offloading, with radio access network (RAN) slicing playing a crucial role in provisioning joint communication and computing resources. However, existing RAN slicing frameworks rely on static computing models, limiting their ability to exploit dynamic semantic data representations enabled by modern neural architectures. To address this limitation, we propose OMNIS+, a semantic RAN slicing framework for multi-cell edge computing built upon a new generation of dynamic split neural models. OMNIS+ incorporates dynamic neural compression paired with adaptive quantization for task offloading, providing a versatile decision space across communication payload and compute dimensions. We leveraged a novel ``box'' quantization scheme that markedly enhances resilience to transmission bit errors across diverse compression rates. Considering partial system observability and distinct node objectives, we formulate two interdependent optimization problems: (i)~mobile devices (MDs) maximize inference accuracy under quality-of-service (QoS) and queue-stability constraints by jointly adapting dynamic split deep neural network (DNN) branches and cell association; and (ii)~each edge server (ES) allocates bandwidth, graphics processing unit (GPU) frequency, and modulation and coding scheme (MCS) to maximize the max--min inference accuracy among associated MDs under cell capacity limits. We solve these problems using a multi-agent distributed optimization framework, where MDs act as causal contextual multi-armed bandit (MAB) agents under backlog-aware Lyapunov control, while each ES executes resource allocation via block coordinate descent (BCD). Compared with state-of-the-art baselines, OMNIS+ reduces the QoS violation probability by up to 1.75 while achieving a better accuracy--violation trade-off.

</details>


### [181] [Algorithmic Market Making Can Lead to Rough Volatility Through the Order Book](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24028)
*Sai Niranjan Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种由不同信息观察周期的做市商共同形成幂律记忆订单流的机制，并以此解释市场中的粗糙波动率；模型预测得到模拟与币安真实订单数据支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究发现，金融市场价格波动路径有时比布朗运动更粗糙；理论上，接近临界不稳定且具有幂律衰减记忆的订单流能够产生这种现象。但这种订单流如何从以算法做市为主的订单簿活动中形成，尚缺乏清晰机制。

Method: 将每个做市商视为一个交易台：交易台依据一阶梯度规则，调整订单相对中间价的距离，使其趋向由未知目标机制给出的目标距离。各交易台不会同步行动，而是在各自目标机制相关的新信息到达时更新，并使用不同的历史观察周期。由此，整体更新可视为受到信息驱动的小批量噪声影响的随机梯度下降。取连续极限后，订单簿成交量满足守恒型随机偏微分方程，即迪恩—川崎方程；不同观察周期进一步造成幂律衰减的订单流记忆。

Result: 模型推导表明，订单簿波动能够驱动具有持久记忆的订单流，并通过标准尺度分析导出粗糙波动率。作者在模拟交易者中验证了关键预测，并使用约六个月、约七亿九千万笔订单的币安数据进行检验；以做市商为主导的高流动性市场订单簿，与模型描述的交易台调节行为相符。

Conclusion: 研究给出了从异步算法做市行为到幂律记忆订单流、再到粗糙波动率的一套一致机制。不同做市商的信息观察周期是产生持久记忆的关键；实证结果支持该机制在币安永续合约等高流动性市场中的适用性。

Abstract: Existing work suggests that markets can show volatility with paths rougher than those of Brownian motion. In theory this can happen when the order flow is close to instability with memory that decays as a power law. However, it is not fully clear how such order flow could arise from the predominantly algorithmic market making that characterizes order book dynamics. We show one such consistent mechanism. We treat each market maker as a desk that uses a first-order gradient rule to move the distances of its orders from the mid price toward target distances provided by an unknown oracle. We assume that distinct desks do not act synchronously in time but act whenever new information relevant to their respective oracles arrives, which each desk samples over its own characteristic lookback horizon. Across all desks the rule therefore takes the form of stochastic gradient descent (SGD) with information-driven (mini-batch) noise. In the continuum limit this update rule makes the volume of the order book follow a conservative stochastic partial differential equation (the Dean--Kawasaki equation) whose fluctuations drive the order flow. The distinct horizons of the desks further induce a persistent memory in the order flow that decays as a power law. Standard scaling arguments then lead to rough volatility. We confirm the key predictions of the model on simulated agents and on six months of Binance data with about 790 million orders. In liquid markets governed mainly by market makers, such as the perpetual futures of Binance, the order books are consistent with how our desks change the book.

</details>


### [182] [From Data to Valves: A Self-Learning, Production-Grade Edge Stack for Sustainable HVAC Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24052)
*Abbas Raza Ali,Muhammad Hamza Rehman,Xu Li,Muhammad Ajmal Siddiqui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一套部署在楼宇边缘网关上的在线学习暖通空调控制系统，包含温度预测、强化学习控制和节能量基线评估。实测支持预测器与基线的表现；强化学习控制器目前仅完成仿真训练并在真实设备上影子运行，尚未证明实际节能效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型暖通空调控制器多停留在仿真环境，或在离线训练后冻结部署，并依赖云端重新训练。论文希望实现一套可在楼宇本地持续学习、具备安全约束且能验证自身效果的生产系统，同时明确区分已验证的能力与尚未证实的效果。

Method: 系统在每栋楼的边缘计算网关上运行三个组件：一是按设备组配置的紧凑型变换器编码器温度预测器，利用设备嵌入预测各末端设备下一控制周期的温度，每五分钟更新一次，并通过五道独立门控限制异常读数的影响；二是跨设备类型共享策略的分布式软演员—评论家强化学习控制器，将测量状态映射为冷冻水阀门开度，并受不可被学习模块放宽的结构性安全边界约束。其第二代版本仅用设备记录的转移数据在夜间本地训练，结合行为锚定和评论家校准项，以四项无模型指标评估后通过原子热切换部署；三是遵循相关行业指南的变点回归基线，用于构建反事实并核证节能量。论文还测量了各服务在共享设备上的延迟与内存占用。

Result: 在一家酒店十八个月的数据上，温度预测器从随机权重出发，一周内收敛，且无须重新训练事件；空气处理机组、新风机组和风机盘管的平均绝对误差分别为零点三七、零点三九和零点一一摄氏度。在另一家酒店，基线对四十六天留出时段的复现误差为百分之零点零七。强化学习控制器已完成仿真训练，并在一百零四台设备上以影子模式运行；摘要未报告其已实际控制设备或实现经核证的节能。系统服务的延迟和内存测量也已报告，但摘要未给出具体数值。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了在楼宇边缘设备上运行并持续学习多种控制相关组件的可行系统架构，并提供了温度预测精度、基线复现能力及资源测量方面的实证。现有证据尚不能证明强化学习控制器在真实闭环运行中的安全性、稳定性或节能收益；其部署效果仍需通过实际控制及严格的反事实评估进一步验证。

Abstract: Learned HVAC controllers rarely leave the simulator, and those that do are typically trained offline, frozen at deployment and retrained from the cloud. We present a production stack in which three learning components run on one NVIDIA Jetson Orin gateway per building, adapt continuously from the building's own data, and verify their own effect. (a) A compact transformer-encoder forecaster, one per equipment group with learned per-unit embeddings, predicts every air-side unit's next-cycle temperature and learns prequentially, one gradient step per five-minute cycle, behind five independent gates that bound the influence of any corrupt reading. (b) A distributional soft actor-critic agent, one shared policy across unit types, converts the measured state into a chilled-water valve position under a structural safety envelope that no learned component can soften; its second generation learns nightly on the device from recorded transitions alone, with a behaviour anchor and critic calibration terms, is scored by four model-free metrics and promoted by atomic hot-swap. (c) A change-point regression baseline held to ASHRAE Guideline 14 supplies the counterfactual against which savings are certified. On eighteen months of data from a hotel the forecaster converges from random weights within a week and reaches 0.37, 0.39 and 0.11 degree C MAE for air-handling, fresh-air and fan-coil units with no retraining event; on a different hotel the baseline reproduces a 46-day held-out period to within 0.07%. The RL agent has completed its in-simulator campaign and runs in shadow on 104 units; we report measured latency and memory of every service on a shared 15 GB device, the systems lessons of the campaign, and precisely what the evidence does and does not yet establish.

</details>


### [183] [Faster to the Wrong Answer: The Price of Local Updates in Federated Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24057)
*Molly Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文刻画了联邦学习中局部计算带来的最优偏差：在给定条件数上界时，最坏情形偏差与客户端异质性之比有精确表达式，并证明局部梯度下降和近端更新可以达到该界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部计算能够改善联邦更新的条件数，却可能引入偏差，使算法的固定点偏离全局最小值。论文旨在回答：当更新的条件数受到约束时，能够达到的最小最坏情形偏差是多少？

Method: 研究客户端目标为强凸二次函数、 Hessian（海森矩阵）特征值位于［μ，L］内的情形，并将局部更新表示为共享的正谱滤波器。以全局最小值处梯度的均方根衡量客户端异质性，再对均匀替代条件数设定上界 χ。通过将任意维度、海森矩阵不必彼此对易的问题归约为标量曲率分布，构造最优性证书及最坏情形实例；这些实例至多需要三个标量客户端。

Result: 当 χ∈［1，L／μ］时，单位异质性对应的精确最坏偏差为（L－μχ）／（2μL√χ）。步长足够小的局部梯度下降以及近端更新均可达到这一界。论文还将近端更新的锐利偏差界推广到一般光滑强凸目标，并量化局部求解不精确造成的误差。

Conclusion: 论文给出了局部计算条件数与联邦固定点偏差之间的精确权衡，并证明该界不仅适用于特定二次问题的上界分析，也能由具体更新方法达到。标量曲率归约使高维、非对易客户端问题获得可验证的最坏情形刻画。

Abstract: Local computation can improve the conditioning of federated updates but may also introduce bias, causing their fixed point to differ from the global minimizer. We characterize the minimum worst-case bias achievable under a prescribed conditioning bound. We consider strongly convex quadratic client objectives with Hessian eigenvalues in $[μ,L]$ and local updates represented by a shared positive spectral filter. Client heterogeneity is measured by the root-mean-square gradient at the global minimizer. For a uniform surrogate condition number bounded by $χ\in[1,L/μ]$, we establish the exact worst-case bias per unit heterogeneity, $(L-μχ)/(2μL\sqrtχ)$, and show that local gradient descent with sufficiently small step sizes and proximal updates attain this bound. Our analysis reduces populations of arbitrary dimension, including noncommuting Hessians, to distributions over scalar curvatures. This reduction provides optimality certificates and worst-case constructions involving at most three scalar clients. We further extend the sharp proximal bias bound to smooth strongly convex objectives beyond quadratics and quantify the error introduced by inexact local solves.

</details>


### [184] [Replay, Don't Resample: Off-Policy Consolidation of Long-Horizon RL Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24059)
*Zhenrui Yue,Gongxiaohui Chen,Huimin Zeng,Honglei Zhuang,Jiashuo Sun,Zhen Han,Dong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出多专家回放策略蒸馏：按专家学习顺序回放强化学习日志，在不重新采样或调用环境的情况下，将多个领域专家合并为一个模型；其效果优于在线强化学习基线及同源专家的多教师在线蒸馏，且成本显著更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常见的专家合并方法需要学生模型在各环境重新生成轨迹、专家逐词评分，计算开销较高；同时，学生还可能继承最终专家策略的偏移。论文认为，专家训练过程已经积累了带有采样概率和奖励的轨迹，且轨迹顺序记录了专家的学习过程，因此可以直接利用这些日志完成合并。

Method: 方法按各专家的学习顺序，将训练日志中的轨迹回放给学生模型。在每一步，基于已评分轨迹重新计算策略梯度，并在日志记录的前缀处蒸馏最终专家的概率分布。由于轨迹顺序贴合专家策略的演进，训练与推理之间的偏差得以减小，学生也较少超出裁剪范围。整个合并过程不生成新样本、不调用环境或奖励模型，仅由训练器处理日志。

Result: 在推理、搜索和结构化查询等三个长时程智能体领域的实验中，该方法得到表现最好的学生模型，明显优于在线强化学习基线，也以远低于多教师在线蒸馏的成本取得更好效果；合并专家的计算量约为训练这些专家所需计算量的七分之一。消融分析显示，回放轨迹贡献了大部分收益，策略梯度和蒸馏项也各自带来增益。

Conclusion: 专家训练日志不只是可混合的数据集合，也包含有价值的学习顺序。实验表明，日志顺序对效果的影响超过日志数量或奖励本身，说明按学习过程组织并回放数据，可能是低成本整合多领域专家的关键。

Abstract: Frontier post-training follows a common recipe: train one expert per domain with reinforcement learning (RL), then consolidate the experts into one model via multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD). Such consolidation is expensive: the student samples in every environment again, an expert scores every sampled token, and the student inherits the drift of the final experts. Yet we argue that the experts' RL runs have already produced what consolidation needs: rollouts with their sampling probabilities and rewards, in the order the experts learned from them. We propose multi-expert replay policy distillation (MRPD), which replays these training logs through the student in the experts' order. At each step, the policy gradient is recomputed on these scored trajectories, and the final experts' distributions are distilled at the logged prefixes. Following the experts' own trajectory reduces the training-inference mismatch and keeps the student within the clip range, so the replay is off-policy in origin but quasi-on-policy throughout consolidation. MRPD samples nothing and calls no environment or reward model: the trainer is the sole process, and consolidating the experts costs only about a seventh of the compute used to train them. On three long-horizon agentic domains (reasoning, search and SQL), MRPD yields the best student model, substantially outperforming online RL baselines, and surpassing MOPD from the same experts at a fraction of MOPD's cost. Our analysis on the same logs shows that the replayed trajectories carry most of the gain, and that both the policy gradient and the distillation term add to it. Most interestingly, the order of the logs matters more than their amount or rewards, suggesting that expert training logs are a curriculum, not a data mixture.

</details>


### [185] [FDM+: A Framework for Constrained Control of the 2D Radiative Transport Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24070)
*Hualing Zhong,Shuai Wang,Hui Xie,Heng Yong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出增强型冻结扩散模型方法，用于二维辐射输运方程的约束控制，改善重建精度并支持多解设计探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将冻结扩散模型用于二维辐射输运方程的约束控制面临三项困难：控制变量与状态函数维度不同、传统数值求解器与深度学习模型的分辨率不匹配，以及问题存在多个可行解。论文旨在解决这些障碍，提升控制结果的重建质量与设计灵活性。

Method: 提出增强型冻结扩散模型，包含三项改进：采用解耦预测架构处理控制变量与状态函数的维度差异；设计高效参数化策略缓解分辨率不匹配，并推导总误差界，分析参数化误差与模型估计误差对性能的共同影响；利用扩散模型学习到的条件分布，在多解问题中探索设计空间。

Result: 一系列数值实验表明，该方法相较现有方法具有更高的重建精度和更强的设计灵活性。摘要未提供具体数值或基准方法信息。

Conclusion: 通过针对维度差异、分辨率不匹配和解的非唯一性进行专门设计，该方法拓展了冻结扩散模型在约束控制中的应用能力，并为多解场景下的方案探索提供了途径。

Abstract: Applying the frozen diffusion model (FDM) [29] to the constrained control of the 2D radiative transport equation presents several challenges. These include the dimensional difference between control variables and state functions, the resolution mismatch between traditional numerical solvers and deep learning models, and solution non-uniqueness. To address these obstacles, we propose a novel approach, FDM+, which includes three key improvements. Firstly, a decoupled prediction architecture is introduced to address the dimensional heterogeneity. Secondly, an efficient parameterization strategy resolves the resolution mismatch. Meanwhile, we derive a total error bound to analyze how the parameterization error and the model estimation error jointly affect the performance. Thirdly, the conditional distribution learned by the diffusion model is leveraged to perform design space exploration for multi-solution problems. A series of numerical experiments demonstrate that FDM+ outperforms existing methods in reconstruction accuracy and design flexibility.

</details>


### [186] [VESPA: Verifiable Symbolic Policy Adaptation for Safe Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24083)
*Paul König,Sascha Marton,Stefan Lüdtke,Christian Bartelt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用形式化验证反例定向微调决策树策略的方法，可更高效地提升强化学习策略的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人、自动驾驶等安全关键领域，强化学习策略必须满足安全约束。但许多策略经验证后会被判定为不安全，传统做法需要从头训练并反复验证，训练和验证成本都较高，因此需要一种能直接改进现有不安全策略的方法。

Method: 提出可验证符号策略适配框架，将形式化验证发现的反例作为训练信号，对现有决策树策略进行定向微调，使策略学习如何避免触发安全约束。微调后再进行验证，并可依据新的反例继续迭代改进。

Result: 在平衡杆任务中，平均可验证安全步数从六十三点八提升至至少九十二点七；使用反例的方案经过两轮迭代达到的效果，相当于不使用反例的方案迭代十轮。在连续山地车任务中，该方法在六十次运行中的五十三次将不安全策略转变为安全策略。

Conclusion: 形式化验证产生的反例能够成为有效的策略训练信号。通过针对这些反例微调现有策略，可以减少反复从头训练的需求，并系统性地推动策略趋于安全；不过，该方法并不保证每次都能完全消除安全违规。

Abstract: Safe reinforcement learning (RL) aims to prevent agents from taking actions that are dangerous or destructive to their environment. This is especially important for safety-critical domains, such as robotics or autonomous driving. Formal verification can be used to prove agent safety through safety constraints. However, in practice, many policies do violate safety constraints, and thus are verified to be unsafe. Since the goal is to obtain a safe policy, we would need to retrain a new policy from scratch and then verify it again until a candidate happens to pass. Both training and verification are usually not cheap, which makes this process inefficient. We introduce VESPA ($\textbf{VE}$rifiable $\textbf{S}$ymbolic $\textbf{P}$olicy $\textbf{A}$daptation), a framework for targeted fine-tuning of decision tree policies. We show that using counterexamples for policy adaptation can make existing unsafe policies safe, or at least make considerable progress towards safety. Targeted fine-tuning of the existing policy on those counterexamples enables it to learn how to avoid violating safety constraints. We thus use the result of a formal verification as a training signal for fine-tuning. On CartPole, VESPA raises the mean number of verified safe steps from 63.8 to at least 92.7, reaching after two iterations what the same loop without counterexamples reaches after ten. On MountainCarContinuous, VESPA turns an unsafe policy into a safe policy in 53 of 60 runs. We conclude that counterexamples from a verifier are an effective training signal, which can be utilized to systematically improve the safety of policies. Our implementation is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/VESPA-Submission/

</details>


### [187] [UCLIR: Unsupervised Continual Learning using Independent Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24093)
*Kamel Aouaidjia,Yanchao Duan,Zihui Zhen,Xin Li,Yongfei Lv,Chongsheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出将输入拆分为多个独立学习的互补表示，并分别进行权重正则化，以减少持续学习中的任务间干扰、提升旧知识保持能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于正则化的持续学习方法会限制对旧任务重要参数的改动，但新任务的更新往往分布于整个高度互联的网络中。随着任务增多，这种广泛更新容易干扰已学知识。论文借鉴神经连接随物理距离增加而减少的规律，认为局部学习有助于降低任务间依赖与干扰。

Method: 将输入分解为多个部分，使用相互独立的自编码器分别学习这些部分，并通过无监督正则化形成多个较小、互补的表示。新任务的参数更新因此局限于特定表示。推理评估时，使用线性分类器对表示进行分类。

Result: 在五类任务序列实验中，包括手写数字拆分、五数据集序列、彩色图像类别拆分和小型图像数据集拆分，该方法取得了有竞争力的结果，可与监督学习方法及回放式方法相比。摘要未提供具体数值。

Conclusion: 相较于对单一大型共享表示进行正则化，分别正则化多个独立学习的互补表示，能够使新任务更新更加局部化，从而减轻任务干扰并改善知识保持。

Abstract: In continual learning, regularization-based methods aim to mimic neuroplasticity by updating network weights to learn new tasks while constraining changes to parameters that are important for previously learned tasks through penalty terms. However, as the number of tasks increases, preserving knowledge from earlier tasks becomes less effective. This is mainly because updates for new tasks are distributed across the entire network, where parameters are highly interdependent due to the dense connectivity between neurons. Inspired by the Exponential Distance Rule (EDR), which states that the number of neural connections decreases with increasing physical distance, learning is naturally locally correlated while inter-area updates remain less task-dependent. Motivated by this principle, in this paper we demonstrate that applying weight regularization to multiple small and independently learned complementary representations is more effective than regularizing a single large shared representation. By decomposing the input into independently learned components, learning new tasks induces only localized parameter updates within specific representation, which limit interference and improve knowledge retention across tasks. We employ independent autoencoders that learn different parts of the input independently through unsupervised regularization. At inference time, a linear classifier is used for evaluation. The experimental results on a sequence of tasks using 5-Splits MNIST, sequence of 5-datasets, 5-Splits CIFAR-10, 10-Splits Tiny ImageNet shows competitive results with supervised and replay-based methods. Code is available.

</details>


### [188] [The 7th International Verification of Neural Networks Competition (VNN-COMP 2026): Summary and Results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24114)
*Tobias Ladner,Konstantin Kaulen,Stanley Bak,Julien Girard-Satabin,Taylor T. Johnson,Edoardo Manino,ThanhVu H Nguyen,Ben Wooding,Haoze Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文总结了二〇二六年神经网络验证竞赛，介绍竞赛规则、基准测试、参赛工具、评测结果及经验教训。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络验证方法不断发展，亟需在统一、公平的条件下比较其性能。竞赛旨在客观评估现有方法的可扩展性与运行速度。

Method: 竞赛采用统一的神经网络与验证规格格式，使用标准硬件环境，由组织方在云平台上运行各工具，并采用工具作者提供的参数。报告整理了竞赛规则、基准测试、参赛工具和评测情况。

Result: 报告总结了竞赛的评测结果与经验教训，但摘要未提供具体的工具排名、数值结果或详细性能差异。

Conclusion: 通过标准化数据格式、硬件环境与评测流程，该竞赛为比较神经网络验证工具提供了较客观的依据，也总结了后续竞赛与方法评估可借鉴的经验。

Abstract: This report summarizes the seventh International Verification of Neural Networks Com- petition (VNN-COMP 2026), held as part of the Symposium on AI Verification (SAIV) co-located with the International Conference on Computer-Aided Verification (CAV) at FLoC 2026 in Lisbon, Portugal. The goal of the competition is to provide an objective comparison of the state-of-the-art methods in neural network verification, in terms of scal- ability and speed. Along this line, we used standard formats (ONNX for neural networks and VNNLIB for specifications), standard hardware (all tools are run by the organizers on AWS), and tool parameters provided by the tool authors. This report summarizes the rules, benchmarks, participating tools, results, and lessons learned from this competition.

</details>


### [189] [QuantAtlas-OM: Taming Neural Spectral Bias in Long-Horizon Spatiotemporal Ocean Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24124)
*Hao Wu,Yuan Gao,Tong Wu,Fan Xu,Fan Zhang,Jinhan Li,Kun Wang,Zhenyu Ma,Bryan Dai,Xiaomeng Huang,Qingsong Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种将大尺度长期演化与小尺度涡旋、锋面细节分开处理的海洋预测框架，显著改善长期预报中的细节保留、稳定性及跨分辨率泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归神经网络在长期滚动预测中存在频谱偏差，高频细节会逐渐衰减，导致预测场模糊并使远期预报不稳定。海洋中尺度涡旋和锋面承载大量动能，对这一问题尤为敏感。粗网格演化容易抹平小尺度结构，细网格演化则可能放大小尺度相位误差，引发滚动失稳。

Method: 提出量图海洋模型，采用尺度—频谱解耦设计：将深层时间演化限制在低波数潜在空间，以维持长期稳定；同时通过外侧旁路把全分辨率的涡旋与锋面残差信息传递至解码器。演化核心结合非局部注意力与分辨率不变的空洞空间门控。

Result: 在三个西边界流区域，模型到第120天保留了中尺度波数谱级联能量的百分之43.7至86.5；第60天异常相关系数为0.81至0.88，黑潮区域第120天达到0.66，为最佳基线的2.5至7.7倍。模型以0.25度分辨率训练后，可零样本迁移至全球0.125度网格，第10天空间相关系数为0.81。在全球0.25度、23层的120天三维海洋模拟及60天海气耦合预报中，模型在16项指标中有15项排名第一，并使全球海盆60至120天盐度均方根误差降低百分之13至27。

Conclusion: 将长期动力演化与高频细节保留解耦，有助于缓解自回归预测的频谱衰减和远期不稳定。实验结果表明，该方法兼具长期预测能力、细尺度结构保真度和跨分辨率迁移能力，并适用于全球海洋及海气耦合预测。

Abstract: Autoregressive neural networks exhibit spectral bias in long-horizon spatiotemporal prediction. As rollouts progress, high-frequency details decay, blurring the predicted fields and destabilizing long-range forecasts. Ocean dynamics offer a particularly stringent test because kinetic energy is concentrated in mesoscale eddies and fronts spanning tens of kilometers. This degradation stems from coupling large-scale phase evolution and small-scale energy retention in a shared feature space. Evolution on a coarse grid smooths out high-wavenumber eddies, whereas evolution on a fine grid amplifies small-scale phase errors and triggers rollout instability. We introduce QuantAtlas-OM (QuantAtlas Ocean Model), a scale-spectral decoupling framework that confines deep temporal evolution to a low-wavenumber latent manifold for long-term stability while routing full-resolution eddy and front residuals through an outer bypass to the decoder. Across physical regimes, the evolution core pairs non-local attention with resolution-invariant dilated spatial gating. Across three western boundary currents, QuantAtlas-OM retains $43.7\%$ to $86.5\%$ of mesoscale spectral energy along the $k^{-3}$ cascade at Day $120$, sustains anomaly correlation coefficients of $0.81$ to $0.88$ at Day $60$, and reaches $0.66$ at Day $120$ in the Kuroshio ($2.5\times$ to $7.7\times$ the best baselines). Trained at $0.25^\circ$, QuantAtlas-OM transfers zero-shot to a global $0.125^\circ$ grid ($0.81$ Day-$10$ spatial correlation) and leads global $0.25^\circ$, $23$-level 3D ocean simulations ($120$ days) and coupled ocean-atmosphere forecasts ($60$ days), ranking first on $15$ of $16$ metrics and cutting $60$- to $120$-day salinity RMSE by $13\%$ to $27\%$ across global ocean basins.

</details>


### [190] [SoU-RHS: Bayesian Structured Sparsity for Compact Architecture Recovery in CNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24166)
*Rafael Mouallem Rosa,Julyan Arbel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种贝叶斯结构化稀疏方法，可从过参数化卷积神经网络中稳定恢复更紧凑的架构，并在缩小模型的同时保持有竞争力的分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型卷积神经网络虽然预测效果较好，但内存、计算和部署成本较高。现有结构化稀疏方法在恢复紧凑架构方面仍有改进空间，因此本文旨在从大型参考模型中识别适合数据的有效滤波器结构，而不只是压缩给定模型。

Method: 作者提出一种贝叶斯结构化稀疏程序，将正则化马蹄收缩与不确定性来源稀疏全局—局部机制结合。该方法强化活跃与非活跃滤波器之间的区分，并通过改变初始网络宽度及剪枝阈值，考察恢复架构的稳定性。

Result: 在标准图像分类基准上，该方法恢复出的架构显著小于确定性结构化稀疏基线，同时保持有竞争力的准确率。在所有评估配置中，其负对数似然均更低，期望校准误差通常也更低。不同初始宽度和合理范围内的剪枝阈值下，恢复结果的总体规模及各层资源分配相近。

Conclusion: 结果表明，该方法能够较稳定地从过参数化网络中恢复与数据相适应的紧凑结构，并兼顾模型规模、预测表现与概率校准。架构恢复的一致性也支持这样一种解释：该方法识别的是数据所需的紧凑结构，而非仅分别压缩不同的初始模型。

Abstract: Highly parameterized convolutional neural networks often achieve strong predictive performance, but their memory, computation, and deployment costs motivate the search for methods that recover compact architectures from large reference models. We propose SoU-RHS, a Bayesian structured-sparsity procedure that augments Regularized Horseshoe Shrinkage (RHS) with the so-called Sources of Uncertainty Sparse Global-Local mechanism (SoU-SGL). The resulting model sharpens the separation between active and inactive filters, providing an effective mechanism for architecture recovery. A key property of SoU-RHS is stability: across different overparameterized starting widths and under a reasonable range of pruning thresholds, it recovers compact architectures with similar total size and layer-wise allocation. This supports the interpretation that the procedure is identifying a compact structure adapted to the data, rather than merely compressing each initial model separately. Across standard image-classification benchmarks, SoU-RHS recovers substantially smaller architectures than deterministic structured-sparsity baselines, while maintaining competitive accuracy, achieving lower negative log-likelihood in every evaluated configuration and generally lower expected calibration error.

</details>


### [191] [Adaptive Action Horizon Selection via Flow-Field Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24178)
*Idan Ben-Ami,Arnon Netzer,Sarah Keren,Idit Diamant*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种无需重训练、仅凭单次动作轨迹即可估计局部预测不确定性的自适应动作分块方法，在多个机器人操作基准上兼顾任务成功率与估计速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉—语言—动作模型需要可靠的不确定性估计，才能动态决定每次执行多长的动作序列。现有自适应动作分块方法依赖多次采样，推理开销较高，且会把单条轨迹内部的不确定性与不同有效策略之间的差异混在一起。

Method: 提出一种基于散度的不确定性感知自适应动作分块后处理方法。该方法利用预训练模型向量场的闭式流后验协方差表达式，通过雅可比—向量积，从单次生成的动作片段中计算动作级不确定性，无需重训练或采样多个终点。它衡量的是围绕当前待执行轨迹的局部不确定性，而非多个候选动作片段之间的整体分散程度。

Result: 该方法的估计结果与既有多样本方法高度相关，同时提供不同信息，并且较少受到多个彼此不同但均有效的动作策略影响。在多个仿真机器人操作基准上，基于该方法选择自适应执行时域的表现较好，在任务成功率与不确定性估计延迟之间取得了有利折中。

Conclusion: 研究表明，预训练模型的流向量场可以作为高效、依赖具体执行轨迹的预测不确定性来源，为机器人决策提供低开销的动作时域选择依据。

Abstract: Reliable uncertainty estimates are important for adaptive execution in vision-language-action (VLA) models. Existing approaches such as Adaptive Action Chunking (AAC) estimate action entropy from multiple sampled action chunks, introducing additional inference cost and capturing both uncertainty within an action trajectory and diversity across alternative strategies. We introduce Divergence-based Uncertainty-aware Adaptive Action Chunking (DUAC), a post-hoc method that extracts trajectory-conditioned predictive uncertainty from the vector field of a pretrained VLA. Building on a closed-form flow-posterior covariance formulation, DUAC derives action-level uncertainty scores from a single generated chunk using Jacobian-vector products, without retraining or multiple endpoint samples. DUAC strongly correlates with AAC, while capturing distinct information: AAC measures global dispersion across sampled chunks, whereas DUAC measures local uncertainty around the trajectory selected for execution. Consequently, DUAC is less sensitive to diversity among distinct but valid action strategies. Across LIBERO, LIBERO-Pro, LIBERO-Plus, and RoboCasa, DUAC-based adaptive horizon selection performs favorably among the evaluated methods, combining strong task success with low uncertainty-estimation latency. These results establish the flow vector field as an efficient, trajectory-conditioned source of predictive uncertainty for robot decision making.

</details>


### [192] [Learning with Unlearning: Synaptic Role Partitioning for Simultaneous Continual Learning and Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24205)
*Mohammad Ali Vahedifar,Behzad Bozorgtabar,Qi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出“边学习边遗忘”框架，在单个权重层面协调持续学习与机器遗忘；实验显示其整体表现优于或不逊于最强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习要求模型吸收新知识并保留旧知识，机器遗忘则要求删除指定知识而不损害其余能力。两者作用于同一组模型权重，更新目标彼此冲突，单独处理其中一项可能破坏另一项，因此需要统一的协调机制。

Method: 该框架分别依据应保留数据和应删除数据，对每个权重进行评估，并将网络划分为四类区域，采用不同更新策略：仅需保留的权重被冻结；仅需删除的权重被清除并释放为可用容量；同时涉及保留与删除的权重沿删除目标进行梯度上升，并将更新投影到与保留目标梯度正交的方向；其余容量则通过特权教师进行自蒸馏，以学习新任务。删除操作在训练后完成，无需重新训练模型。

Result: 在持续学习和机器遗忘任务中，该方法优于或匹配最强基线。论文还在其构建的物理人工智能数据集上展示了实际应用效果；在图像生成以及危险内容移除任务中，也优于最接近的基线。

Conclusion: 该研究通过在单个权重层面区分保留、删除与冲突需求，为持续学习和机器遗忘提供了统一方案。摘要报告的结果表明，这种细粒度的权重分区与定向更新有望缓解两类目标之间的干扰，并兼顾新任务学习和定向知识删除。

Abstract: Continual Learning (CL) asks a model to acquire new knowledge without forgetting what it already knows. Machine Unlearning (MU) asks it to destroy specific knowledge without damaging the rest. The challenge is integrating them in the same system; the two objectives pull on the same weights, and an edit made for one harms the other. To address this, we propose Learning with Unlearning (LwU), a unified framework that resolves the conflict where it occurs: at the individual synapse. LwU scores every weight twice, once against the data to keep and once against the data to delete, and partitions the network into four zones with separate update rules. Weights that only require retention are frozen. Weights that only require deletion are cleared and returned to the network as free capacity. Contested weights take a gradient-ascent step projected orthogonally to the retention gradient. The remaining capacity learns the incoming task by a privileged teacher via self-distillation. Deletion is served post hoc, and no retraining is required. Across both CL and MU jobs, LwU outperforms or matches the strongest baseline. Its real-world practicality has been demonstrated on the Physical Artificial Intelligence (PAI) dataset we conducted. Additionally, in the image generation task and in removing dangerous content, it outperformed the closest baseline, as shown in Fig.1.

</details>


### [193] [Continuous Noisy-Loop Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24213)
*An T. Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究已知正方格上的潜在自旋相邻一致性，经连续噪声观测后的学习、表示与推断。统计上可高效估计，但高精度表示仍可能需要很大的概率电路；另一方面，平面结构使因子化证据下的精确查询保持可计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在厘清高效学习和推断是否意味着存在紧凑的概率表示。研究对象是含有 d 条边的已知正方格，观测为潜在相邻自旋一致性的连续噪声读数；核心问题包括样本估计难度、概率电路的表示复杂度，以及利用平面结构进行精确推断的可能性。

Method: 在边际分布已知、噪声通道有界且满足利普希茨条件的设定下，分析最坏情形平方海林格风险，并构造样条估计器及指数规模混合估计器。进一步研究非负可分解概率电路的节点数下界和双向库尔贝克—莱布勒近似误差；利用局部环路恒等式，将联合分布散度联系到可识别的通道参数乘积，从而区分弱通道恢复与密度估计。最后利用证据的因子化性质，将查询归约为平面零场伊辛模型计算。

Result: 基于 N 个独立观测向量，最坏情形平方海林格风险为 Θ(min{1,dN^{-2/3}})，且该统计结论不要求可靠性存在正下界。若可靠性有固定正下界，样条估计可用多项式算术时间达到此速率；不作该要求时，指数规模混合仍可达到该速率。固定正可靠性下，要使总变差误差小于 1，非负可分解电路需要 exp(Θ(√d)) 个节点，即使每个叶节点最多涉及 αd 个坐标。对于至多 d^r 个节点、叶作用域大小 ℓ≤αd 的电路（r>1、0<α<2/3），双向最优库尔贝克—莱布勒近似误差均为 Θ(d/max{log d,√ℓ})；节点数不计叶节点内部计算。对三个固定掩码，当 ℓ≤(log d)^2 时，最优联合条件库尔贝克—莱布勒误差为 Θ(d/log d)。在给定局部积分后，因子化证据下的精确查询只需 O(d^{3/2}) 次算术运算及一次开方。

Conclusion: 论文表明，统计学习和结构化推断可以高效，并不代表目标分布能由小型概率电路准确表示。样本复杂度由边数与观测数量决定，弱可靠性主要影响高效估计器的构造条件；而电路近似仍面临随规模增长的表示下界。与此同时，局部环路结构有助于识别噪声通道参数，平面零场伊辛结构则支持高效精确查询。

Abstract: Efficient learning and inference need not imply a compact probabilistic circuit. We quantify this distinction for continuous noisy readings of agreement between adjacent latent spins on a known square grid with $d$ edges. With known margins and bounded Lipschitz channels, minimax squared Hellinger risk is $Θ(\min\{1,dN^{-2/3}\})$ from $N$ independent vectors, without a reliability lower bound. A spline estimator reaches this rate in polynomial arithmetic time given a fixed positive reliability bound; an exponential-size mixture removes that requirement. At fixed positive reliability, nonnegative decomposable circuits need $\exp(Θ(\sqrt d))$ nodes for fixed total-variation error below one, even when each leaf uses at most $αd$ coordinates, $0<α<1$. Optimal Kullback--Leibler (KL) approximation error is $Θ(d/\max\{\log d,\sqrt\ell\})$ in either direction with at most $d^r$ nodes and leaf scopes $\ell\leqαd$, for fixed $r>1$ and $0<α<2/3$. Node counts exclude leaf computation. For three fixed masks, optimal joint conditional KL has order $d/\log d$ when $\ell\leq(\log d)^2$. Local cycle identities relate joint divergence to identifiable channel products and separate weak-channel recovery from density estimation. Factorized evidence preserves a planar zero-field Ising model: given local integrals, exact queries cost $O(d^{3/2})$ arithmetic operations and one root extraction.

</details>


### [194] [Towards Reliable Scaling Laws for Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24214)
*Johannes Hog,Neeratyoy Mallik,Steven Adriaensen,Jan Hendrik Metzen,Tom Viering,Danny Stoll,Frank Hutter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究表格基础模型的扩展规律，发现仅拟合扩展曲线并不足以可靠预测新数据集上的表现；扩大预训练数据的行数与特征数范围，有助于提升性能并使扩展趋势更可预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常见基础模型的扩展规律常用于预测性能和分配计算资源，但表格基础模型主要在可配置分布生成的合成数据上预训练，最终却在真实数据集上评估。合成数据与真实数据之间的差异，使既有扩展规律能否可靠外推成为疑问。

Method: 研究者分析了此类表格基础模型在不同模型规模、训练计算量、合成预训练数据集规模及评估设置下的扩展表现，并考察扩展规律对未见数据集的预测可靠性，以及预训练时采样的最大行数和特征数对性能与趋势的影响。

Result: 合成数据与真实数据呈现不同的扩展行为。即使扩展规律拟合良好，也不代表它能可靠指导未见数据集上的表现；使用过少评估数据集选择配置，会系统性地高估性能。若要获得计算最优的扩展方案，除增加模型规模与训练计算量外，还需扩大合成预训练数据集规模的覆盖范围。提高预训练采样的最大行数和特征数，可以改善性能，并使扩展趋势更可预测。

Conclusion: 表格基础模型的扩展规律必须结合真实数据评估，并谨慎检验其跨数据集的泛化能力。训练时应同步扩展模型、计算量及合成数据的规模范围，尤其应增加预训练所覆盖的行数与特征数，以提升训练效率和预测可靠性。

Abstract: Scaling laws are widely used to predict foundation model performance and guide compute allocation. However, tabular foundation models (TFMs) differ from conventional foundation models in that they are pretrained on virtually unlimited synthetic data from configurable distributions, while ultimately being evaluated on real-world datasets. We investigate whether scaling laws for TabPFNv2-style models reliably extrapolate to larger compute budgets and generalize across evaluation settings. Our analysis reveals differences between synthetic and real-world scaling behavior and shows that accurately fitting a scaling law does not necessarily yield reliable prescriptions on unseen datasets. In particular, selecting configurations using too few evaluation datasets leads to systematically over-optimistic performance estimates. We further demonstrate that compute-optimal scaling requires expanding the range of synthetic pretraining dataset sizes alongside model size and training compute. Increasing the maximum numbers of rows and features sampled during pretraining improves performance and produces more predictable scaling trends. These insights guide the training of more efficient TabPFNv2-like models, using the open-source training pipeline of nanoTabPFN.

</details>


### [195] [TRESS: A Fixed-Size History Representation for Irregular Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24257)
*Shusen Ma,Yangchun Xie,Xudong Zuo,Haizhou Liang,Yun-Bo Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该方法为每个变量保留近期观测及其原始时间戳，并汇总完整历史，以固定规模表示不断增长的记录；在四个基准数据集上取得了较好的预测精度，并加快了多时点查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定数量的近期观测对不同变量可能覆盖截然不同的时间跨度，较早的测量虽不在最近窗口内，仍可能帮助判断当前值是否异常。模型需要兼顾观测的时间信息、变量历史统计特征与有限的存储和推理成本。

Method: 模型为每个变量保留指定数量的近期观测及原始时间戳，同时概括该变量的完整历史；因此，在变量集合和事件预算固定时，表示规模不会随记录增长。查询条件事件提升模块依据观测距今时间和预测时距，对相对于最新值的偏差加权。随后，按变量设置的解码器结合这些特征、历史统计量及跨变量共享摘要，预测对最新值的修正。

Result: 在四个基准数据集的对比中，每个变量保留八至十六条观测时，该方法的测试均方误差和平均绝对误差均为所比较方法中的最低值。此外，基于同一份编码历史连续处理三十二个查询时点，可使中央处理器推理时间降低至原来的约二分之一至二分之一点六七，即加速约二点一二至二点六零倍。

Conclusion: 摘要表明，将带时间戳的近期事件与全历史统计摘要结合，有助于在固定表示规模下提升多变量时间序列预测表现；复用历史编码也能降低连续多时点预测的计算开销。

Abstract: A fixed number of recent observations may span minutes for one variable and days for another. Earlier measurements can still reveal whether the latest value is unusual for that variable. We propose TRESS (Timestamped Recent Events and Statistical Summaries), a forecasting model that retains the latest observations at their original times and summarises the full history of each variable. For a given set of variables and event budget, this representation stays the same size as the record grows. Its query-conditioned event lifting module weights deviations from the latest value according to the age of each observation and the forecast horizon. A channel-specific decoder combines the resulting features with historical statistics and a summary shared across variables to predict a correction to the latest value. Retaining 8 to 16 observations per variable, TRESS records the lowest mean test MSE and MAE among the methods compared on all four benchmarks. Serving 32 successive query times from one encoded history reduces CPU inference time by a factor of 2.12--2.60 across the four datasets. The code is at https://github.com/SSMa913/NICLab-TRESS.

</details>


### [196] [Element-Wise Femoral Stress Prediction: A Hybrid Graph Neural Network Surrogate for Finite Element Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24274)
*Sikha O K,Abdollah Moslemi,Elham Alizadeh,Ludovic Humbert,Miguel A. Gonzalez Ballester,Jerome Noailly*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究提出一种结合图卷积、邻域聚合与残差学习的图神经网络，用于快速预测股骨有限元网格中的应力；其性能优于所比较的传统机器学习模型和基线图神经网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 髋部骨折会造成严重的残疾与死亡负担，且随人口老龄化而愈发常见。有限元分析能够评估股骨力学强度，但计算成本较高，限制了临床应用；传统机器学习虽预测较快，却难以利用有限元网格的空间关系和拓扑结构。

Method: 研究构建混合图神经网络框架，将有限元网格表示为图，并采用图卷积、邻域信息聚合和残差学习相结合的架构，以刻画网格单元间的空间依赖及应力传播。研究系统比较了14种模型，并通过针对性消融实验评估各架构组件的作用。

Result: 所提出的混合模型在全部评估模型中表现最佳，决定系数为0.8022，平均绝对误差为2.879兆帕。结果表明，在生物力学预测中纳入网格拓扑信息具有重要价值。

Conclusion: 该框架有望成为有限元分析的可扩展、高计算效率替代方案，支持骨科力学分析与骨折风险评估。摘要未提供临床验证或外部数据集测试的具体结果，因此其实际临床效用仍需进一步评估。

Abstract: Hip fractures represent a significant clinical challenge because they cause excess disability and mortality, and have a growing incidence in aging populations, as bone fragility increases. Although finite element analysis (FEA) provides biomechanical assessment of femoral strength, its clinical applicability is mainly limited by high computational cost. Conventional machine learning (ML) approaches have been explored as surrogate models for FEA; predictions are fast but fail to capture the spatial and topological structure of finite element meshes. To address these limitations, this study proposes a hybrid Graph Neural Network (GNN)-based framework for femoral stress prediction using graph-structured finite element modeling (FEM) data. The proposed GCN-SAGE-Residual architecture combines graph convolution, neighborhood aggregation, and residual learning to model spatial dependencies and stress propagation across mesh elements. We systematically evaluated 14 models in a primary comparison, spanning classical ML methods and baseline GNN architectures, complemented by a targeted architectural ablation that isolates the contribution of each component of the proposed design. Evaluation results demonstrate that the proposed hybrid architecture achieves the strongest predictive performance among all evaluated models. The proposed hybrid model achieves the best performance with an R^2 score of 0.8022 and mean absolute error of 2.879 MPa, highlighting the importance of incorporating mesh topology in biomechanical prediction tasks. The proposed framework provides a scalable and computationally efficient alternative for FEM-based orthopedic analyses and fracture risk assessment. The code is publicly available on GitHub.

</details>


### [197] [Supervised Hebbian Class Networks: Learning Without Backpropagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24314)
*John Loverich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种无需梯度计算和反向传播的监督式赫布分类网络，并在手写数字识别和图像分类任务上进行测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多层感知机和卷积网络通常依赖梯度计算与反向传播进行训练。论文旨在探索一种以赫布式活动关联为基础的替代方案，同时保留分层网络结构和类别监督能力。

Method: 在网络中为每个神经元分配类别标签，连接结构仍采用常规全连接或卷积形式。训练时，各层共享基于类别的监督规则，并使用经改造、适配赫布活动乘积的狮子优化器；所有层在整个训练过程中同时更新。网络各层的活动可直接用于类别预测，因此无需另行训练分类器。

Result: 摘要说明，实验在手写数字数据集上使用多层感知机，在彩色图像数据集上使用卷积网络进行测试；但所给摘要在实验描述处截断，未提供具体结果或性能指标，因而无法判断其准确率及与其他方法的比较表现。

Conclusion: 该方法尝试将类别监督与赫布式学习结合，以避免梯度计算和反向传播，并实现网络各层联合训练及直接预测。现有摘要表明其经过初步图像分类测试，但不足以支持对效果或适用范围作出定量结论。

Abstract: This paper introduces Supervised Hebbian Class Networks as a method for training multi- layer perceptrons and convolutional networks without gradient calculations or backpropa- gation. Each neuron in a learned layer is assigned a class, while connections follow the usual fully connected or convolutional pattern. The method combines a class-based supervision rule shared across layers with a Lion optimizer adapted to Hebbian activity products. All layers train together throughout the run, and their activities provide class predictions di- rectly, without a separately trained classifier. The experiments test the method on MNIST with a multilayer perceptron and on CIFAR-10 with a convolutional networ

</details>


### [198] [Structure-Preserving Neural Approximation of Convex, Semiconvex, and Quasiconvex Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24325)
*Yeachan Park,Yeoneung Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究如何以结构受约束的神经网络逼近多类具有凸性相关性质的函数，并给出逼近误差、网络复杂度及数值表现方面的理论与实验结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普通神经网络虽能逼近函数，却难以保证整个定义域上的凸性或拟凸性。论文希望扩展输入凸神经网络的结构保持能力，使其覆盖半凸、拟凸及可经变换转化为凸函数的情形，同时明确逼近精度与模型复杂度之间的关系。

Method: 对于紧凸域上的一阶导数满足利普希茨连续性的凸函数，采用由多个仿射函数取最大值构成的近似，并建立一阶索伯列夫范数下的极小极大误差分析；再将该仿射最大值模型精确表示为宽度有界、复杂度随仿射片段数线性增长的输入凸神经网络。对半凸函数使用二次提升，对可变换为凸函数的函数使用单调复合；对一般利普希茨拟凸函数，则利用函数沿仿射投影方向的单调响应和有限探测点构造保结构近似。此外，论文将理论推广至若干可由整流线性单元迁移的平滑激活函数，并开展偏微分方程近似实验。

Result: 对光滑度条件所述的凸函数，使用包含数量为 m 的仿射片段可达到一阶索伯列夫范数下的最优误差阶 m^{-1/d}，且相应模型可由输入凸神经网络以线性复杂度精确实现。半凸函数及可变换凸函数也具有相同的逼近误差阶。对一般利普希茨拟凸函数，论文给出具有一致范数误差保证的结构保持网络近似。数值实验显示，所提方法无需调节形状惩罚即可保证全局结构有效性，且逼近精度与参数量匹配的多层感知机和经过验证集调参的形状惩罚方法相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 论文表明，凸性及相关结构可通过恰当的网络架构和函数变换保留，并扩展到凸性以外的函数类别。核心观点是：可恢复的凸潜在结构，是实现梯度层面、结构保持逼近的关键机制。

Abstract: Input convex neural networks provide a natural way to enforce convexity globally through architectural constraints. We study how this structure-preserving approximation framework extends to semiconvex, quasiconvex, and convex transformable functions. For $C^{1,1}$ convex functions on compact convex domains, we establish the sharp $W^{1,p}$ minimax rate $m^{-1/d}$ under max-affine approximation with $m$ affine pieces, and show that every max-affine function admits an exact bounded-width ICNN representation with complexity linear in $m$. The same $W^{1,p}$ approximation rate extends to semiconvex functions through quadratic lifting and to convex transformable functions through monotone composition. For general Lipschitz quasiconvex functions, we develop a quantitative finite-probe approximation theory based on monotone responses to affine projections and obtain a structure-preserving IqCNN with an $L^\infty$ approximation guarantee. We further show that these approximation results extend to ReLU-transferable smooth activations, including Softplus and ELU. Numerical experiments on PDE-based approximation problems compare the proposed architectures with parameter-matched MLPs and validation-tuned shape penalties, demonstrating global structural validity without penalty tuning while maintaining competitive approximation accuracy. Overall, the results identify recoverable convex latent structure as the key mechanism enabling gradient-level structure-preserving approximation beyond convexity.

</details>


### [199] [Distribution-Free Uncertainty Quantification for Weather Forecasting: Conformal Prediction Under Spatiotemporal Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24329)
*Mariya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究比较了八种天气预报区间构造方法。尽管各网格点的长期覆盖率接近目标值，单日空间覆盖率仍大幅波动，且误差常连续多日聚集；按日预测误差尺度可降低这种波动，但尚无方法能同时取得最佳依赖性表现和区间评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测能够在有限样本下提供不依赖分布假设的边际覆盖保证，但这种保证只描述某个位置长期来看被区间覆盖的比例，无法说明某一天的覆盖情况如何在空间和时间上共同变化。对于网格天气预报，空间误差相关和连续失准会影响预报区间的实际可靠性，因此有必要评估这些依赖特征。

Method: 研究使用再分析资料，以五点六二五度分辨率评估八个预报时效（六小时至十天）的二米气温，并采用一个较简单的卷积预报模型和欧洲中期天气预报中心的业务预报。以二〇一七至二〇一八年校准、二〇一九至二〇二一年测试，比较八种区间构造方法，包括高斯区间、合并或逐网格点的分割共形校准、季节条件校准、自适应共形推断及滚动窗口。除覆盖率、区间宽度和区间评分外，还分析空间过度离散、每日空间覆盖率分位数，以及相对于随机打乱日期基准的失准持续性；并检验利用当日平均归一化误差进行事后尺度调整的效果，以及根据季节信息和已验证误差预测日尺度的效果。

Result: 逐网格点校准方法的边际覆盖率接近目标值，但每日被覆盖网格比例的方差约为独立网格情形的三至十倍，失准也会在时间上聚集。两种现象均出现在业务预报中，并随卷积模型的预报时效增加；不同校准方法都无法消除这些现象。事后按日平均误差尺度调整，可解释并消除卷积模型百分之八十一至九十二、业务预报百分之九十三至九十八的过度离散。根据季节项和已验证误差预测日尺度，能够在不加宽区间的情况下降低过度离散，但区间评分最多只改善百分之零点六。业务预报在十二小时至三天时，日尺度预测的过度离散指标最低；自适应共形推断或季节条件校准的区间评分则更优。

Conclusion: 仅达到边际覆盖率，并不意味着每日、全空间范围内的覆盖表现稳定。天气预报中的大范围日际误差变化是空间覆盖波动的主要来源，且失准存在时间持续性。日尺度误差建模有助于降低空间过度离散，却未带来显著的区间评分提升；目前不存在同时在依赖性控制和区间评分上最优的方法。结论的适用范围有限，研究仅涉及一种变量、较粗网格、再分析目标及两种预报系统。

Abstract: Conformal prediction gives finite-sample, distribution-free marginal coverage and has begun to be applied to gridded weather forecasts. Marginal coverage concerns the long-run fraction of covered points at a location, not how coverage behaves jointly across space and time on a given day. I benchmark eight interval-construction procedures (Gaussian, pooled and per-cell split conformal, per-season, adaptive conformal inference and rolling windows) for gridded 2 m temperature forecasts at eight lead times from 6 hours to 10 days, using ERA5 at 5.625 degree resolution, a deliberately modest convolutional forecaster and ECMWF's operational HRES, with calibration in 2017-2018 and testing in 2019-2021. Alongside coverage, width and interval score, I report dependence diagnostics: a spatial overdispersion index, quantiles of daily spatial coverage and miss persistence relative to a shuffled-days null. Per-cell methods reach near-nominal marginal coverage, yet the day-to-day variance of the fraction of cells covered is roughly 3 to 10 times what independent cells would give, and misses cluster in time; both effects grow with lead time for the CNN, also appear for HRES, and are shared by all methods. An oracle that divides each day's normalised errors by that day's mean removes 81 to 92 percent of the excess dispersion for the CNN and 93 to 98 percent for HRES, so days of large errors across much of the grid account for most of it. A day-scale predicted from seasonal terms and already-verified errors lowers the overdispersion index without widening intervals but improves interval score by at most 0.6 percent; on HRES from 12 hours to 3 days it has the lowest overdispersion index while ACI or season-conditional calibration has the best interval score, so no method is best on both. The study covers one variable, one coarse grid, a reanalysis target and two forecasters.

</details>


### [200] [OpenGNS: An Open Dataset of the Gradient Noise Scale across Vision, Language, and Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24331)
*Marcel Aach,Jiangtao Wang,Stefan Kesselheim,Andreas Lintermann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发布涵盖多种现代模型与任务的梯度噪声尺度数据集，并系统研究该指标与模型规模、训练进程及临界批量大小之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度噪声尺度可用于衡量随机梯度中有效信号与噪声的相对大小，并近似估计临界批量大小。但此前缺少覆盖现代架构的开放测量数据，限制了对该指标的系统研究。本文旨在弥补这一数据缺口，并检验梯度噪声尺度在不同任务和训练设置下的规律。

Method: 作者发布开放数据集，包含四百余条梯度噪声尺度测量轨迹，覆盖残差网络图像分类、自回归生成式预训练语言模型以及扩散变换器图像生成，模型规模约为一千一百万至十亿参数。所有模型均采用最大更新参数化训练，并进行密集的批量大小与学习率扫描。作者据此分析指标对超参数、模型规模和训练进度的依赖，并比较其与临界批量大小的关系。

Result: 研究发现，在最大更新参数化下，模型规模不同但损失相当时，梯度噪声尺度总体保持稳定，表现得像一种一致的“训练温度”。它与临界批量大小的关系则具有任务差异：在分类任务中倾向于高估临界批量大小，在生成任务中倾向于低估；这一偏差方向在常见的临界批量大小定义下均保持一致。

Conclusion: 该数据集为研究梯度噪声尺度提供了跨架构、规模和任务的开放基础。结果支持将其视为具有一定规模稳定性的训练状态指标，但也表明它不能不加区分地替代临界批量大小估计，应用时需考虑任务类型及估计定义。

Abstract: The Gradient Noise Scale (GNS) metric measures the signal-to-noise ratio of gradients in stochastic gradient descent and, as shown by McCandlish et al. (2018), can be used to approximate the Critical Batch Size (CBS) during neural network training. Despite its appearance in technical reports of frontier models, no open dataset of GNS measurements across modern architectures exists, limiting systematic study of the metric. We close this gap by releasing OpenGNS, a dataset of more than 400 GNS measurement trajectories spanning three domains: image classification with ResNets, autoregressive language modeling with Generative Pre-trained Transformers (GPTs), and image synthesis with Diffusion Transformers (DiTs), covering models from 11M to 1B parameters trained under Maximal Update Parameterization (muP) with dense batch-size and learning-rate sweeps. Building on this dataset, we study how the GNS depends on hyperparameters, scale, and training progress, and how it relates to the CBS. We find that the GNS behaves as a consistent "temperature of training", remaining stable across model sizes under muP at equivalent loss, and our results suggest task-dependent biases in its relation to the CBS: a tendency to overestimate the CBS for classification and to underestimate it for generative tasks, consistent in direction across common CBS definitions. The dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/olympique-marcel/OpenGNS

</details>


### [201] [Tell Me How Sure You Are: Communicating Uncertainty in Auxiliary Evidence to Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24351)
*Soumyajit Chatterjee,Baturalp Buyukates,Abhirup Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文表明，在向基础模型提供上下文时明确传达信息的不确定性，能为后续决策提供有效线索，并显著提升分类表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式基础模型接收的上下文信息常包含不确定性，例如对物理世界的描述；但传统提示方式通常忽略这些不确定性。论文旨在检验：将不确定性一并传达，是否能改善模型的后续决策。

Method: 研究将问题设定为由辅助文本特征支持的零样本图像分类任务，利用五种下游基础模型和三个数据集进行评估。实验考察人类及学习模型在观察或预测辅助证据时产生的不确定性，并比较传达与不传达不确定性时的分类表现；同时分析模型如何处理这些不确定性信息。

Result: 在所有评估设置中，传达不确定性都能显著提升下游模型的分类表现；相较于不提供不确定性信息，宏平均F值最高提升26.18个百分点。研究还发现，辅助信息中的不确定性能够预测下游决策是否正确。

Conclusion: 不确定性并非应被提示流程丢弃的噪声，而是对下游推理有用的上下文信息。未来的基础模型推理流程应更系统地表示和传达不确定性。

Abstract: In many queries, the context information provided to generative foundation models, such as LLMs and VLMs, is inherently uncertain, including its representations of the physical world. While conventional prompting discards the associated uncertainty, this paper, for the first time in the literature, systematically demonstrates that communicating such uncertainty in context provides useful additional cues for downstream decisions and yields improvements across a wide range of settings.
  We formulate the decision-making problem as a zero-shot foundation model-assisted image classification task supported by auxiliary text-based characteristics. We choose this due to the availability of well-defined auxiliary properties, associated uncertainty, and unambiguous task correctness. Using five downstream foundation models across three datasets, we extensively evaluate uncertainty arising from both humans and learned models when observing or predicting auxiliary evidence. Across all settings, we find that communicating uncertainty substantially improves downstream LLM classification performance: macro-F1 improves up to $26.18$ percentage points compared to when the uncertainty information is not communicated.
  We further observe how the foundation models process such uncertainty information and find that auxiliary uncertainty contains predictive information about the correctness of downstream decisions. Together, these results establish uncertainty as useful contextual information and motivate more systematic consideration of uncertainty communication in future foundation-model inference pipelines.

</details>


### [202] [VAFL: A Vertical Analytic Federated Learning Mechanism for Efficient Crowdsourced Services via Closed-Form Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24367)
*Yajiang Huang,Anfeng Liu,Tian Wang,Mianxiong Dong,Houbing Herbert Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向垂直联邦学习的分析式建模框架，在不集中原始数据的前提下协同训练分类或回归预测头，并研究降低计算开销的两类方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能服务往往需要利用不同机构各自持有的互补信息，但集中汇集原始数据可能不可行。现有协作还面临模型拟合开销问题，因此需要兼顾隐私保护、预测效果与计算效率的联邦分析方法。

Method: 该框架在各方固定的本地表示上，采用带正则化的最小二乘法拟合分类或回归预测头，并通过代理参与的受保护计算流程支持双方及多方协作。为降低拟合开销，论文提出直接将分块因子作用于目标矩阵的全维等价求解，避免构造完整逆矩阵；此外研究两种降宽策略：一是本地谱投影后提升系数，压缩预测空间；二是在本地保留全维拟合结果，再以低秩跨方修正进行协作，从而保留协作子空间之外的本地成分。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上的实验展示了两种降宽策略在预测精度与计算成本之间的权衡，并验证了该框架可兼容多种冻结的特征提取骨干网络。实验还揭示了不同策略的适用运行点及压缩能力的局限。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，基于固定本地表示的正则化最小二乘联邦分析能够支持多方协作，并可通过等价求解和降宽策略控制计算成本。两种降宽方法保留信息的方式不同，实际选择应依据精度要求、训练时间与可压缩程度权衡。

Abstract: Intelligent services often rely on complementary information held by different organizations, motivating Vertical Federated Learning without centralized raw-data pooling. We propose VAFL, a Vertical Analytic Federated Learning framework that fits classification and regression heads on fixed local representations through regularized least squares. An agent-assisted protected-computation workflow supports two-party and multi-party collaboration. To reduce fitting overhead, we develop a full-dimensional equivalent solve that applies block factors directly to the target matrix, avoiding construction of the full inverse. We further study two width-reduction strategies: local spectral projection with coefficient lifting, and full-dimensional local fitting with low-rank cross-party correction. The first compresses the prediction space, whereas the second retains local components outside the collaboration subspace. We establish their algebraic properties and analyze their computational scope. Experiments on five benchmarks show the accuracy--cost tradeoffs of these strategies and compatibility with diverse frozen backbones. Predictive performance and training time identify useful operating points and the limits of compression.

</details>


### [203] [LLM-Assisted Multi-Fidelity Bayesian Optimization: Fusing Expert Knowledge with Numerical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24377)
*Dongjun Han,Mingyu Lee,Ikjin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究将大语言模型解读的专家定性知识融入多保真贝叶斯优化，帮助在高保真数据稀缺时更有效地搜索设计空间；在锂离子电池设计问题上，优化后悔面积较传统方法降低百分之六十一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多保真贝叶斯优化通过结合成本较低但精度有限的低保真模型与少量高精度的高保真评估来降低工程设计成本。然而，当高保真数据稀缺，且低保真模型遗漏的物理效应会随设计变化时，仅凭数值数据难以准确学习两种模型之间的偏差。专家通常了解遗漏了哪些物理机制，以及这些机制在哪些条件下影响更大，但现有多保真模型难以直接利用这类定性知识。

Method: 提出大语言模型辅助的多保真贝叶斯优化方法，在传统多保真模型中加入知识基函数。大语言模型根据专家对遗漏物理机制的描述，比较两个设计所需的带符号残差修正大小；随后，成对高斯过程将这些比较判断转化为知识基函数。高保真数据用于校准该基函数的贡献及符号，避免未经验证地盲信专家知识。

Result: 在锂离子电池设计问题上，该方法使传统多保真贝叶斯优化的后悔面积曲线下面积降低百分之六十一。受控实验分别改变知识质量与大语言模型选择，发现搜索加速效果主要取决于知识质量，而非所用模型。知识层级消融实验还量化了各条知识陈述的贡献。

Conclusion: 大语言模型能够将专家对遗漏物理机制的定性认识转化为多保真优化可用的信息，并与数值数据互补。该方法在高保真数据有限时有望提升搜索效率，但收益取决于所提供知识的质量，且仍需高保真数据对知识贡献进行校准。

Abstract: Multi-fidelity Bayesian optimization (MFBO) reduces engineering design cost by combining inexpensive low-fidelity (LF) models with a few accurate high-fidelity (HF) evaluations and sequentially selecting designs to evaluate. However, when HF data are scarce, the LF-HF discrepancy is difficult to learn from numerical data alone, especially when physics omitted in the LF model makes it design-dependent. Yet even without sufficient data, domain experts often know which physics the LF model omits and under which conditions its effect grows. Existing multi-fidelity (MF) models cannot use such qualitative knowledge directly, but large language models (LLMs) can interpret it and compare designs accordingly. The proposed LLM-Assisted MFBO adds a knowledge basis to the conventional MF model: from an expert description of the omitted physics, an LLM judges which of two designs requires the larger signed residual correction, and a pairwise Gaussian process converts these judgments into the basis. HF data calibrate its contribution and sign, so the knowledge is not trusted blindly. On a lithium-ion battery design problem, the proposed method reduced the regret area under the curve of conventional MFBO by 61%. Knowledge quality, rather than the choice of LLM, determined whether the search was accelerated, as shown by a controlled study that varied the two independently. A knowledge-level ablation further quantified the contribution of each knowledge statement. These results show that LLM-interpreted expert knowledge can complement numerical data as an information source in MFBO.

</details>


### [204] [How to Turn an Attention Neural Network into an Interpretable Hard-Logic Predicate Circuit: Truth-Table Predicate Extraction and State-Machine Merging under Ship-of-Theseus Replacement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24381)
*Yuchen Li,Zongqi Fan,Xintong Li,Nguyen H. Tran,Ken-Tye Yong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将训练好的注意力网络逐块替换为可综合的硬逻辑谓词电路。电路推理无需乘法器或权重传输，速度显著提升，但准确率低于原教师网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力网络在小型设备上的推理往往受计算与权重存取开销限制。论文希望把已训练网络转换为结构明确、可直接部署的硬件逻辑，同时尽量保留教师模型的分类能力。

Method: 将网络中的单元表示为针对词元的零一谓词，并以最小化真值表描述谓词间映射：短映射精确最小化，宽特征映射则通过容忍噪声的最小化转化为蕴含规则。研究者从输出端开始逐块替换网络，每次替换后让尚未替换的软网络重新适应已冻结的电路。注意力选择采用逐行整数分数比较或学习得到的规则两种方式；两者仅用常规训练集拟合，并只使用教师模型分类正确的样本。

Result: 在推文情绪分类任务上，蒸馏型生成式预训练变换器第二代的完整电路，采用分数排序选择时准确率为百分之六十六点五正负一点三，采用规则选择时为百分之五十七点四正负二点五；教师模型为百分之七十四点七，同数据训练的词片段词袋模型为百分之六十四。基础版双向编码器表示变换器的电路准确率分别为百分之七十点零正负零点五和百分之六十三点七正负一点二，教师模型为百分之七十七点八。综合估算显示，电路在现场可编程门阵列上处理一条推文约需一至五微秒，在掌上型设备上约需五至九微秒；相较批量为一时图形处理器上的网络推理，快约两个至三个数量级。种子零的综合电路准确率代价分别为八点五和八点六个百分点，且导出的硬件描述可逐位复现电路。

Conclusion: 该方法展示了把注意力网络转化为高效、可复现硬件逻辑的可行性，尤其适合重视低延迟与低硬件复杂度的部署场景。不过，电路化会带来明显的准确率损失；分数排序选择整体优于规则选择，实际效能仍需结合任务精度要求和硬件实测评估。

Abstract: We convert a trained attention network as a whole, attention included, into a hard-logic predicate circuit that runs in its place. Every unit of the circuit is a 0/1 predicate on tokens, and every map between predicates is a minimised truth table. Short tables, such as the product of a query code and a key code, are minimised exactly, and wide feature-to-feature transitions become implication rules by noise-tolerant minimisation. Blocks are replaced one at a time from the output down, in the manner of the Ship of Theseus, and after each replacement the remaining soft part re-adapts to the frozen circuit. Attention selection takes two forms, a row-wise comparison of integer scores (order selection) and a learned rule (rule selection). Both are fitted on the ordinary training set alone, and only on the examples that the teacher classifies correctly, so that the circuit imitates the teacher where the teacher is right. On TweetEval-emotion, the complete circuit of DistilGPT2 reaches a test gold accuracy of 66.5 $\pm$ 1.3% with order selection and 57.4 $\pm$ 2.5% with rule selection over three seeds, against 74.7% for the teacher and 64.0% for a bag of word pieces fitted on the same tweets, and that of BERT-base reaches 70.0 $\pm$ 0.5% with order selection and 63.7 $\pm$ 1.2% with rule selection against 77.8%. The circuit needs no multiplier and no weight traffic, and its exported hardware description reproduces it bit for bit. By a synthesis-based estimate it classifies one tweet in about 1-2 $μ$s (DistilGPT2) and 3-5 $μ$s (BERT) on an FPGA card and in 5-9 $μ$s on a palm-sized board whose module draws 7.5-15 W, two to three orders of magnitude faster than the network on a GPU at batch 1 (measured, 0.9-2.6 ms for DistilGPT2 including an optimised CUDA-graph baseline), at a cost of 8.5 (DistilGPT2) and 8.6 (BERT) points of gold accuracy for the synthesised seed-0 circuits.

</details>


### [205] [Temporal Rank in Recurrent Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24394)
*Leo Dobson,Anton Ragni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出，循环神经网络的长期建模能力不仅受梯度消失或爆炸影响，也受隐藏状态动力学的秩约束所造成的信息瓶颈限制；通过结构化的高维状态动力学，可保留更多输入方向并提升序列任务上的学习速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环神经网络虽适合处理时间序列，却常因计算难以并行以及难以捕捉长期依赖而受到质疑。论文提出，除梯度稳定性问题外，还存在一个研究不足的限制：隐藏状态动力学的秩约束会压缩随时间保留的独立输入方向，形成信息瓶颈并降低时间建模容量。

Method: 作者分析输入到状态映射的秩及其随时间的演化，指出秩受限会独立于梯度稳定性限制模型的时间容量。为缓解这一问题，论文引入结构化的高维状态动力学，以维持时间上的可分性，避免输入到状态雅可比矩阵随时间趋于饱和；该方法仍属于标准循环神经网络框架。

Result: 在受控序列任务上，采用该状态动力学的模型表现出较快的学习速度。结果表明，模型能够否学习并保留多少时间信息，与输入到状态映射的秩如何随时间变化密切相关。

Conclusion: 循环神经网络的性能不仅取决于梯度是否稳定，也取决于时间容量是否得到保留。单独缓解梯度问题并不足够；设计模型时还需显式维护时间秩，避免输入信息随时间被压缩。

Abstract: Recurrent Neural Networks (RNNs) are a natural framework for temporal modelling, yet are often dismissed due to their sequential, non-parallelisable computation and difficulty in capturing long-range dependencies, typically attributed to vanishing and exploding gradients. We identify a distinct and under-explored limitation: an information bottleneck induced by rank-constrained hidden state dynamics, which limits the number of independent input directions that can be preserved over time, as reflected in the rank of the input-to-state mapping. We show that this constraint restricts temporal capacity independently of gradient stability. To address this, we introduce structured high-dimensional state dynamics that preserve temporal separability and prevent saturation of the input-to-state Jacobian over time. On controlled sequence tasks, the resulting model exhibits rapid learning despite remaining strictly within a standard RNN formulation. Our results demonstrate that RNN performance is governed by the evolution and preservation of temporal rank, and suggest that mitigating gradient pathologies alone is insufficient without explicitly maintaining temporal capacity.

</details>


### [206] [RSIBench-Infra: Can Agents Optimize Their Own Training and Inference Infrastructure at Production Scale?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24395)
*Hanlin Wu,Yifei Wang,Zhicheng Cai,Huan-ang Gao,Wei-Ying Ma,Ya-Qin Zhang,Jingjing Liu,Hao Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出覆盖训练与推理、面向端到端基础设施优化的基准测试。实验显示，全部任务结果中有百分之六十六点二实现了经验证的至少一点一倍加速，但稳定获得端到端收益仍具挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练和推理基础设施效率是递归式自我改进的实际组成部分。现有智能体优化基准多聚焦于单一软件层，例如计算内核，因此尚不清楚智能体能否在生产级工作负载中带来整体性能提升。

Method: 研究提出基础设施优化基准，包含二十项训练与推理任务，覆盖多种模型系列和模态，部署规模最高达三十二个加速器，使用英伟达图形处理器和昇腾神经网络处理器。智能体从依据官方配置构建的可运行基线出发，可修改执行引擎、计算内核及指令层。提交采用开放容器倡议镜像分层协议，以便复现部署；独立评测则在各任务的质量约束下衡量端到端性能。
