Result: 在所有任务结果中，百分之六十六点二达到经验证的至少一点一倍加速。这表明当前智能体能够改善生产规模训练与推理的性能，但有效优化策略的发现，以及将局部改进稳定转化为端到端收益，仍然困难。

Conclusion: 该基准为评估智能体在真实、跨层训练与推理基础设施中的优化能力提供了系统化途径。实验验证了智能体具备带来生产级性能提升的潜力，同时凸显了优化策略发现和端到端收益稳定性方面的不足。

Abstract: Improving the efficiency of training and inference infrastructure is a practical component of recursive self-improvement. Existing benchmarks for agent-driven optimization largely focus on individual software layers such as kernel, leaving unclear whether agents can deliver end-to-end performance gains under production workloads. We introduce RSIBench-Infra, a benchmark of 20 training and inference tasks spanning diverse model families and modalities, with deployments of up to 32 accelerators on NVIDIA GPUs and Ascend NPUs. Agents start from runnable baselines derived from official recipes and can modify the software stack across engine, kernel, and instruction levels. An OCI image-layer submission protocol captures these changes for reproducible deployment, while independent evaluation measures end-to-end performance under task-specific quality constraints. Across all task results, 66.2% exceed $1.10\times$ validated speedup. These results demonstrate that current agents can improve production-scale training and inference performance, but identifying effective optimization strategies and consistently translating local improvements into end-to-end gains remain challenging.

</details>


### [207] [MINT: Meeting-time Indicators for Truncation in Multi-Step Off-Policy RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24413)
*Seungyub Han,Taehyun Cho,Dohyeong Kim,Kyungjae Lee,Jungwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出会合时刻截断法，以行为策略与目标策略轨迹首次会合为依据，自适应决定多步备份何时停止；理论分析其方差与收缩性质，实验显示其在多类强化学习任务上表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多多步离策略时序差分方法通过连乘逐步迹系数处理行为策略与目标策略不匹配，但这会在控制方差和保留长程学习信号之间形成权衡，使最大迹长度成为敏感的调节参数。论文希望获得一种能随轨迹特性调整备份长度、同时控制目标方差的方法。

Method: 提出会合时刻截断法：用二值停止指标取代逐步迹系数的连乘——耦合的行为策略轨迹与目标策略轨迹首次会合前取一，此后取零。在真实价值函数处，指标的幂等性使方差界由会合时间的存活概率控制。实际算法则利用单个采样动作构造无模型停止规则，不需要转移模型或混合时间估计。论文还分别给出固定策略评估中状态耦合算子及其实用近似算子收缩的充分条件。

Result: 在连续控制基准任务上，该方法优于论文报告的多步基线；主要连续控制比较采用共同的截断上限，而非逐任务调节上限。论文还在雅达利十万步、真实机器人强化学习及开放式目标基准实验中验证其效果。与长固定时域备份相比，该方法能依据任务与动力学调整备份时域，并降低目标值波动。

Conclusion: 会合时刻截断为离策略多步学习提供了一种无需显式模型、能自适应调整备份时域的替代方案。理论分析解释了其方差控制与收缩条件，实验结果显示其在多类任务上具有竞争力；不过，所述理论条件主要针对固定策略评估。

Abstract: Many multi-step off-policy temporal-difference methods handle policy mismatch through products of per-step trace coefficients, leading to a trade-off between variance control and long-horizon signal. The maximum trace length consequently becomes a sensitive hyperparameter. We propose MINT (Meeting-time INdicators for Truncation), which replaces these products with a binary stopping indicator: one until coupled behavior and target trajectories first meet, zero thereafter. At the true value function, the indicator's idempotence yields a variance bound governed by meeting-time survival. For practical implementation, a single sampled action provides a model-free surrogate for the stopping rule, requiring no transition model or mixing-time estimate. We derive separate sufficient conditions for contraction of the state-coupled operator and its practical surrogate under fixed-policy evaluation. On MuJoCo and DeepMind Control tasks, MINT outperforms the reported multi-step baselines; the main MuJoCo comparison uses a common cap without per-task cap-length tuning. We also demonstrate the effectiveness of MINT on Atari 100k, RWRL, and OGBench experiments; MINT adapts the backup horizon to reduce target variability relative to long fixed-horizon backups across tasks and dynamics.

</details>


### [208] [Probing Physics in Motion Diffusion Models Against Their Own Estimate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24423)
*Saeed Ghorbani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究检验运动扩散模型是否藏有自身生成估计尚未呈现的物理知识。统一调优读出后，平衡与腾空方面几乎没有稳定增益；跳跃下落率在部分模型的去噪早期存在较明显例外。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究发现，从视频扩散模型内部激活中更容易解码物理属性，便据此认为模型内部掌握了输出未体现的知识。但模型的干净样本估计本身也由这些激活计算而来，因此比较激活与输入或输出，可能只反映读出难易不同，不能直接证明模型隐藏了信息。本文旨在更公平地检验运动生成模型是否确有未呈现在自身估计中的物理知识。

Method: 研究者逐层记录两种运动文本生成扩散模型在采样轨迹中的激活，并利用运动序列的精确运动学计算各中间估计的物理属性。实验比较模型内部层与其自身干净样本估计对平衡、腾空和跳跃下落率的预测能力，并对读出方法作一致调优；另以正向对照检验实验能否发现真实增益。研究还测试了无需训练即可识别漂浮样本的方法及其节省计算的效果，并在万二点一视频模型的干净潜变量上考察固定非线性变换后的可读性。

Result: 采用常见基线时，内部层在平衡和腾空预测上总体优于模型自身估计；但统一调优读出后，层特征带来的增益至多为零点零一，正向对照表明实验足以检测这一量级的效果。明显例外是跳跃下落率：在去噪早期，一种运动模型的内部层带来约零点零八的决定系数增益，重新拟合后最高约零点一一；该优势随采样推进而减弱。在另一种运动模型上，增益较小且不稳健。模型自身估计还能在无需训练时标记漂浮样本，但据此中止生成节省的计算量不足十分之一。视频模型实验则显示，经过一种固定非线性变换后，干净潜变量中可读出线性探针原本遗漏的合理性信息。

Conclusion: 内部激活比模型输出更容易解码，不足以证明模型普遍隐藏了额外物理知识；在公平调优后，平衡与腾空上的多数差距基本消失。跳跃下落率在特定模型的去噪早期存在较可信但会逐渐消退的隐藏信息迹象。结果也说明，结论会受到读出方法影响，且运动模型上的发现不能直接推广到视频模型。

Abstract: Recent studies report that video diffusion models "know more than they show": physical properties decode better from their activations than from their inputs or outputs. But a diffusion model computes its clean-sample estimate from those same activations, so such a comparison measures how easily a readout extracts the information. Whether the model hides anything is a separate question. We set out to find hidden physical knowledge in kinematic text-to-motion diffusion models, where the physics of every intermediate estimate can be computed exactly, and recorded every layer along the sampling trajectories of MDM (two checkpoints) and MotionDiffuse. Against the usual baselines the layers do look ahead of the model's own estimate on balance and flight. With every readout tuned the same way, the estimate closes the gap: for balance and flight, adding a layer gains at most 0.01, a size that a positive control shows we would detect. The fall rate of jumps is the one clear exception. Early in denoising, MDM's layers add about 0.08 in $R^2$ (up to 0.11 in a refit) that the estimate does not yet show, and the gain fades as sampling proceeds; on MotionDiffuse it is small and not robust. The estimate also flags floating samples without training, but aborting them saves under a tenth of the compute. In a video model, plausibility that linear probes miss in Wan 2.1's clean latent becomes readable after one fixed nonlinearity.

</details>


### [209] [Choosing the Right Representations: Task-Aware Fusion for Multimodal Materials Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24641)
*Huu Binh Ta,Alex Morehead,N. Benjamin Erichson,Ferdinando Fioretto,Sandeep Madireddy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出任务条件注意力融合方法，按预测任务从多个结构编码器的不同层级中选择并融合信息，在提升材料性质预测效果的同时减少训练参数与时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态语言模型需要将三维材料结构有效接入语言模型。现有方法依赖较大的桥接模块和独立的对比对齐训练，成本较高，也未能充分利用不同结构编码器及其不同深度的互补信息。研究发现，生成式材料编码器的信息并非集中在最后一层，最有用的表示会随目标性质而变化。

Method: 提出任务条件注意力融合方法：以任务条件化的潜在查询为基础，构建轻量级注意力桥接模块，融合监督式材料结构编码器的特征与生成式编码器的中间层表示。该模块直接在下游预测任务上训练，使表示选择与材料—语言对齐能够联合学习，无须单独进行对比预训练；研究还测试了加入第三种结构编码器后的组合。

Result: 在分类和回归任务中，该方法优于物质对话模型，均方根误差最高降低47.8%，同时使用的可训练参数显著更少，总训练时间约减半。加入第三种编码器后，三编码器组合的宏平均分类准确率最高达到89.61%。

Conclusion: 材料性质预测可受益于依据目标任务动态选择不同编码器及不同层级的结构表示。轻量级任务条件融合不仅提升了预测性能，也降低了训练成本，并能灵活扩展到多种编码器组合。

Abstract: Multimodal language models for materials science rely on effective mechanisms for incorporating three-dimensional structural representations. MatterChat addresses this challenge using a large bridge module between a pretrained structural encoder and the LLM, together with a separate contrastive alignment stage. We instead ask whether this bridge can adaptively select and combine representations from multiple structural encoders according to the target property. We find that supervised and generative material encoders provide complementary information for property prediction. We found that, for Zatom-1, this information is not concentrated in the final layer. Instead, the most informative representations occur at different depths depending on the target property. This motivates Task-Conditioned Attention Fusion (TCAF), a lightweight attention-based bridge that uses task-conditioned latent queries to combine CHGNet features with intermediate Zatom-1 representations. The bridge is trained directly on downstream prediction tasks, allowing representation selection and material-language alignment to be learned jointly without separate contrastive pretraining. Across classification and regression tasks, TCAF outperforms MatterChat, with up to 47.8% lower RMSE, while using substantially fewer trainable parameters and halving the overall training time. Further experiments incorporating UMA-S demonstrate TCAF's flexibility across encoder combinations, with the three-encoder configuration achieving the highest macro-average classification accuracy of 89.61%.

</details>


### [210] [Predicting Process-Level Behavior: A Benchmarking Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24425)
*Michael Grohs,Lukas Kirchdorfer,Jana-Rebecca Rehse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过综合基准测试比较多类方法在流程层级预测中的表现，发现将单个轨迹的预测汇总到流程层级会累积误差，因此流程层级预测应作为独立任务研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测性流程监控主要预测单个流程实例的未来，但流程管理者需要流程整体层面的信息来安排人员、计算关键绩效指标等。研究关注现有方法能否有效支持这类管理决策，以及轨迹层级预测能否迁移到流程层级。

Method: 研究评估两项流程层级预测任务：并发轨迹数量预测和平均周转时间预测。基于合成与真实事件日志，对比统计方法、机器学习方法、基础模型、业务流程仿真，以及将轨迹层级预测汇总为流程层级结果的既有方法。

Result: 不同方法的预测表现差异显著。尤其是，将单个轨迹的预测汇总到流程层级时，误差会逐步累积并降低准确性，说明轨迹层级方法并不能直接、可靠地解决流程层级预测问题。摘要未提供具体数值或各方法的详细排名。

Conclusion: 流程层级预测具有独特性，不能简单视为轨迹层级预测的汇总应用。未来研究应针对流程整体行为开发并评估专门方法，以更有效地支持流程管理决策。

Abstract: Predictive process monitoring (PPM) typically forecasts the future of individual process instances, hence operating on trace level. Process managers, however, typically operate on process level, e.g., when assigning staff or calculating KPIs. The question is how well such process-level managerial decisions are supported by existing approaches, given that PPM research has so far not focused on process-level predictions. We answer this question through a comprehensive benchmarking study of two process-level prediction tasks: concurrent trace prediction and average throughput time prediction. Across synthetic and real-life event logs, we compare statistical and machine-learning-based methods, foundation models, business process simulation, and, importantly, established trace-level PPM approaches, aggregating their predictions to the process-level. We assess which modeling paradigms are most suitable for predicting process-level behavior and whether existing trace-level PPM can be effectively repurposed for this task. Our results show substantial variation in performance across approaches. Notably, aggregated trace-level predictions cause errors to compound on process-level, reducing accuracy and highlighting process-level prediction as a distinct predictive task that warrants dedicated research.

</details>


### [211] [How Generative Models Generate: A Concise Guide to the Generative AI Landscape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24728)
*Tianhua Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个以生成过程为中心的简明框架，帮助读者理解并比较多种生成式人工智能方法及其相互联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式人工智能方法在模型架构、学习目标、数学表示和采样过程上差异显著，领域日益复杂。本文旨在提供一份结构清晰、便于入门的技术指南，帮助读者建立连贯的整体认识。

Method: 文章采用两层分析视角：第一层按生成路径区分三类方法，即直接变换或解码、逐步条件构造、迭代更新或传输；第二层考察支撑这些路径的建模与学习原则。文章围绕代表性方法的关键组成、学习目标和采样过程展开介绍，并分析不同架构与生成机制之间的联系、重叠及组合方式。

Result: 本文构建了一个紧凑的概念框架，将多种生成方法置于共同的分析视角下，并梳理其核心机制及彼此关联。摘要未报告定量实验或具体性能比较。

Conclusion: 以生成过程为主线组织生成式人工智能知识，有助于读者理解陌生方法、识别已接触模型之间的联系，并在更广阔的技术版图中定位不同方法。

Abstract: The rapidly expanding generative AI landscape brings together methods that differ in architecture, learning objective, mathematical representation and sampling procedure. A concise, selectively organised technical guide can provide an accessible starting point for developing a coherent understanding of this increasingly complex landscape. This article takes a generation-centred approach, connecting how models produce new observations with the modelling and learning principles that make generation possible. We develop a two-level perspective: first, distinguishing three recurring generation routes-direct transformation or decoding, sequential conditional construction, and iterative updating or transport; and second, examining the modelling and learning principles underlying these routes. Representative approaches are introduced through their key components, learning objectives and sampling procedures. We then examine their broader connections, including how different architectures and generation mechanisms can overlap or be combined. By bringing together essential technical explanations within a compact conceptual framework, the article aims to help readers develop a working understanding of unfamiliar approaches, recognise connections among models they have encountered, and navigate different approaches within the broader generative AI landscape.

</details>


### [212] [DyNA-Mixer: Decoupling Time and Topology for Scalable Dynamic Node Affinity Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24426)
*Yuanxing Zheng,Liping Wang,Xuemin Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出动态亲和力混合模型，用于预测连续时间动态图中节点对未来交互强度，并在四个基准数据集上取得最佳排序效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态链接预测通常只判断未来是否发生交互，无法表达交互强度，也难以满足对所有候选节点对进行排序的需求。解决这一问题既要捕捉不同时间尺度上的变化和全图协同关系，又要避免对所有节点对逐一计算带来的高昂开销。

Method: 方法将时间预测与协同结构建模分开处理：先以多尺度时间预测器估计特定节点对的亲和力变化；再构建虚拟协同图，推断查询时刻的节点关系，并汇总相关交互趋势；随后通过门控残差机制融合两类信息。此外，采用稀疏性感知评估策略，将计算密集的预测限制在近期存在非零亲和力的节点对上。

Result: 在四个连续时间图基准数据集上，该方法的前十名归一化折损累计增益均达到最佳。在规模最大的数据集上，相较最强基线，测试表现相对提升百分之十七点八。

Conclusion: 将节点对自身的时间演变与图范围内的协同信息解耦建模，并结合稀疏性降低计算开销，可以有效提升动态节点亲和力预测的排序质量。

Abstract: Continuous-time dynamic graphs preserve the ordering, timing, and evolving strength of interactions that static snapshots discard, making dynamic graph mining essential for evolving systems such as recommender systems, trade networks, and transaction platforms. Research in this area has centered on dynamic link prediction, which forecasts only whether a future interaction will occur, whereas many applications also need its intensity. The dynamic node affinity prediction (DNAP) problem generalizes this task to continuous targets and full-candidate ranking, making it more broadly applicable but substantially harder. This raises two coupled difficulties: ranking requires a predictor expressive enough to capture both multi-scale temporal evolution and graph-wide collaborative patterns, yet running such a predictor over every source--destination pair is computationally prohibitive. We propose DyNA-Mixer, which decouples temporal forecasting from collaborative structure modeling. It adopts a multi-scale temporal predictor for pair-specific affinity evolution, constructs a Virtual Collaborative Graph that infers query-time relationships and aggregates trends from related interactions, and fuses the two through a gated residual mechanism. A sparsity-aware evaluation strategy further restricts expensive forecasting to pairs with recent non-zero affinities. Experiments on four Temporal Graph Benchmark datasets for the DNAP problem show that DyNA-Mixer achieves new state-of-the-art NDCG@10 on every dataset, including a 17.8\% relative test improvement over the strongest baseline on tgbn-token, the largest dataset.

</details>


### [213] [Elysium: Simulation-Free Unbalanced Schrödinger Bridges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24763)
*Zihan Wang,Jianzhe Li,Zhenyi Zhang,Peijie Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 伊利西姆提出一种无需训练期轨迹模拟的求解方法，可从不同时间点的单细胞快照重建细胞状态变化、群体规模变化及相关事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单细胞测量通常具有破坏性，无法连续追踪同一细胞；而细胞群体变化还包含随机的增殖与凋亡。仅用密度变化率描述群体规模，不能将细胞的进入和退出归因于个体；现有非平衡薛定谔桥神经求解器则需要在训练时模拟轨迹，计算负担较大。

Method: 伊利西姆联合学习细胞状态转移与群体质量变化。它以带杀死机制的布朗运动及其时间反向过程为参考，利用最优端点耦合和条件桥分布构造直接回归目标，无须在训练中模拟轨迹。论文证明，期望训练目标的极小化解可恢复最优非平衡薛定谔桥。

Result: 在具有已知真值的模拟基准上，该方法能够重建状态转移、群体规模及事件分布，并优于已有的确定性方法和桥接方法。在单细胞时间序列数据上，它能够插补未观测时刻的细胞状态分布，追踪群体扩张与收缩，并捕捉药物反应的双相动态。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种无需训练期轨迹模拟的非平衡细胞动力学重建方案，能够同时描述细胞状态演变和群体数量变化，为从离散单细胞快照推断细胞增殖、凋亡及药物响应动态提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Reconstructing cellular dynamics from snapshots is challenging because the destructive nature of single-cell measurements makes tracking the same cells over time impossible. Cellular dynamics are inherently stochastic and involve changes in population size through proliferation and apoptosis. Growth-based methods represent population change as a rate on the density, which does not assign exits and entries to individual cells. Unbalanced Schrödinger bridges model individual events, but neural solvers for killed diffusion bridges simulate trajectories during training. We introduce Elysium, a simulation-free solver that jointly learns state transition and mass change. Using a killed Brownian reference and its time reversal, Elysium constructs direct regression objectives from an optimal endpoint coupling and conditional bridge distributions, without simulating trajectories during training. We show that the minimizers of the expected training objectives recover the optimal unbalanced Schrödinger bridge. Empirically, Elysium reconstructs state transitions, population size, and event distributions on simulated benchmarks with known killed-diffusion ground truth, outperforming existing deterministic and bridge-based solvers. On single-cell time courses, it interpolates cell state distributions at unobserved time points, tracks population expansion and contraction, and captures biphasic drug-response dynamics.

</details>


### [214] [ICICLE: In-Context Inference for Curve-Learning Extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24469)
*Laurits Fredsgaard,Michael Riis Andersen,Mikkel N. Schmidt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出冰柱：一种用于冻融贝叶斯优化的上下文学习代理模型，可处理数量可变的超参数。其参数量仅为既有模型的百分之八点七左右，在多个基准上表现相近，并在部分元相册任务上优于快速调优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻融贝叶斯优化需要根据部分观测到的学习曲线预测其后续表现，以决定将训练预算分配给哪些配置。既有方法对超参数数量设有限制，模型规模也较大；此外，评估其在新任务上能否不依赖任务级预训练或元训练仍具竞争力，也是重要问题。

Method: 冰柱采用上下文学习代理模型，以共享集合编码器接收数量可变的超参数。模型通过合成先验任务训练，覆盖不同类型的超参数、重尾噪声、量化效应和训练发散情形。其参数量约为一百二十八万，并提供在线生成合成训练任务的机制，以及可供调度器集成和部署推理的实现。

Result: 在学习曲线基准、深度学习性能基准和任务集基准上，冰柱的平均遗憾变化轨迹与既有冻融先验数据拟合网络相近。在包含八十六项任务、三十三个超参数的元相册基准上，冰柱总体及扩展任务子集的归一化平均遗憾低于快速调优；在迷你和微型子集上则没有明显差异。冰柱未在元相册任务上进行预训练或元训练，而快速调优使用了相关任务进行元训练。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于合成先验训练的轻量级上下文模型能够支持可变超参数输入，并在多类任务上保持有竞争力的曲线外推表现。它在部分基准上的优势尤其值得关注，因为这些表现并非依赖目标基准任务的预训练或元训练。

Abstract: Freeze-thaw Bayesian optimization allocates training budget by predicting how partially observed learning curves will continue. LC-PFN first applied prior-data fitted networks (PFNs) to learning-curve extrapolation, and FT-PFN extended this approach to configuration-aware freeze-thaw optimization with a ten-slot hyperparameter encoder. We introduce In-Context Inference for Curve-Learning Extrapolation (ICICLE), an in-context surrogate that accepts a variable number of hyperparameters through a shared set encoder and is trained on a synthetic prior covering typed hyperparameters, heavy-tailed noise, quantization, and divergence. ICICLE has 1.28 million parameters (8.7% of FT-PFN's count) and produces similar mean-regret trajectories to FT-PFN on LCBench, PD1, and TaskSet. On the 86-task Meta-Album benchmark with 33 hyperparameters, ICICLE reaches lower mean normalized regret than Quick-Tune overall and on extended, with no clear difference on mini or micro. It does so without pre-training or meta-training on Meta-Album tasks, whereas Quick-Tune is meta-trained on related Meta-Album tasks. Our release samples synthetic training tasks on the fly and provides a freeze-thaw scheduler for Ray Tune and an ONNX model for inference without JAX or PyTorch.

</details>


### [215] [Horizontal Flow Matching for Single-Cell Spatiotemporal Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24764)
*Zihan Wang,Jianzhe Li,Zhenyi Zhang,Peijie Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种能排除组织整体旋转和平移影响的细胞时空建模方法，在模拟数据和四组空间转录组时间序列上提升了组织重建与阶段插值效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间转录组的不同时间点往往来自不同细胞和不同坐标系。组织切片的旋转或平移可能被误认为细胞运动，进而扭曲细胞对应关系及组织动态推断。研究旨在区分组织内部真实变化与整体刚性运动。

Method: 提出水平流匹配方法，以连续性—反应方程描述细胞空间分布、分子状态和细胞丰度的变化，并令整体共享刚性运动在作用量中不产生代价。通过水平投影，在训练时从空间预测误差、生成时从预测速度中去除整体刚性运动。该方法还将问题等价为在瓦瑟斯坦—费舍尔—拉奥几何中联合估计细胞对应关系与相对位姿，并利用解析路径产生速度和丰度变化的回归目标。模型采用等变变换器，根据当前组织预测速度和丰度变化，从单个初始快照连续生成组织。

Result: 在坐标系受扰动的模拟组织中，位姿对齐改善了重建效果，水平投影减少了整体刚性漂移。在四组空间转录组时间序列上，该模型在组织几何、基因表达和细胞丰度重建方面优于现有空间建模及非平衡轨迹方法，并能插值重建未观测的发育阶段。

Conclusion: 该研究将组织整体刚性运动与组织内部的生物学变化明确分离，为从空间转录组快照学习细胞时空动态提供了更稳健的框架。结果表明，联合处理细胞对应关系、相对位姿、空间运动和细胞丰度变化，有助于提高组织重建与发育过程插值的准确性。

Abstract: Learning the spatiotemporal dynamics of cells from spatial transcriptomics requires connecting snapshots of different cells acquired in different coordinate frames. A rotation or translation of a section can appear as cell movement and distort the inferred correspondence. We introduce horizontal flow matching, which learns changes in tissue organization, molecular state, and cell abundance up to shared rigid motion. The dynamics follow a continuity--reaction equation whose action assigns no cost to shared rigid motion, and the resulting horizontal projector removes this component from spatial prediction errors during learning and from predicted velocities during generation. An equivalent endpoint problem jointly estimates correspondence and relative pose in Wasserstein--Fisher--Rao geometry and yields analytic paths whose velocity and growth serve as regression targets. Regressing these targets recovers the horizontal dynamics under ideal population conditions. Our model, HORIZON, uses an equivariant transformer to predict velocity and growth from the current tissue, so that a tissue can be generated continuously from a single initial snapshot. On a simulated tissue observed in perturbed coordinate frames, alignment improves reconstruction and projection reduces rigid drift. On four spatial transcriptomics time courses, HORIZON reconstructs tissue geometry, gene expression, and cell abundance more accurately than existing spatial and unbalanced trajectory methods, and interpolates held-out developmental stages.

</details>


### [216] [Latent Action Policies from Suboptimal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24482)
*Donghyeon Ki,Xiaoyu Chen,Jun Ho Seo,Hayeong Lee,Kyungyoon Kim,Byung-Jun Lee,Li Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向目标条件控制的潜在策略改进方法，利用观测到的状态—潜在动作转移进行价值优化，并通过条件流策略处理潜在动作的多模态结构；该方法在多数任务上优于基线，尤其适用于视觉操作和动作标注极少的场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在动作模型能够从无动作标注的视频中学习控制表示，但现有方法通常仅用行为克隆训练策略，缺少明确的策略改进目标。直接将离线强化学习用于潜在动作空间也并不可靠：优化出的潜在动作可能无法通过解码后实际执行；此外，同一潜在表示可能对应奖励不同的物理控制，导致潜在空间中的最优策略与物理空间中的最优策略不一致。

Method: 论文分析了潜在空间中的策略改进何时能够迁移到解码后的实际执行，并推导潜在动作最优解与物理控制最优解对齐的条件。在此基础上，提出面向目标条件任务的潜在策略改进方法：以观测到的状态—潜在动作转移为依据进行价值驱动的策略改进，并采用条件流策略刻画潜在动作的多模态分布，避免将其不恰当地压缩为单峰策略。

Result: 该方法在多数评测任务上优于基线，提升在视觉操作任务中最为明显。即使仅有百分之一的动作标注数据，方法仍能保持有效，而其他竞争方法大多性能崩溃。

Conclusion: 在潜在动作空间中进行策略优化，必须考虑潜在表示与可执行物理控制之间的对应关系，以及动作分布的多模态性。论文通过理论条件分析与基于观测转移的价值改进机制，表明即使动作标注极少，也可以有效提升从视频学习得到的控制策略。

Abstract: Latent Action Models (LAMs) learn control representations from action-free video, but existing pipelines typically train latent policies with behavioral cloning, without an explicit policy improvement objective. Although offline RL offers a natural approach to policy improvement, standard methods do not transfer directly to learned latent action spaces. We find that the structure of learned latent actions makes naive offline RL unreliable: directly maximizing value over latent actions or fitting a unimodal policy can produce latent actions that cannot be realized by physical execution, while the latent representation can merge physical controls that receive different rewards. To understand when latent-space optimization remains valid, we derive conditions under which policy improvement transfers to decoded execution and under which latent and physical optima align. Building on these insights, we propose LAPIS for goal-conditioned latent policy improvement, grounding value-based policy improvement in observed state-latent transitions and using a conditional flow policy to capture their multimodal structure. LAPIS outperforms baselines on most tasks, with the clearest gains in visual manipulation, and remains effective even with only 1% action labels, where competing methods largely collapse.

</details>


### [217] [Counterfactual World Models: JEPAs That Reason About "What If"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24778)
*Randall Balestriero,Yann LeCun,Kerda Varaku*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种无需反事实训练数据、额外参数或重新训练的反事实世界模型：利用物理系统中动作与隐藏扰动的平滑、可分离作用，从事实预测中估计替代动作的结果，并在多种模拟环境中显著提升预测与回溯规划表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的世界模型无法完全观测环境，隐藏扰动可能改变动作结果。因此，评估某次失败究竟源于决策不当还是运气不佳，需要回答一个关键问题：在遭遇相同扰动时，若采取另一动作，结果会如何？论文指出，仅凭事实转移数据训练的世界模型，单独无法回答这一反事实问题，无论数据量或模型容量如何。

Method: 作者利用许多物理系统的平滑性：当动作与扰动作为相互独立的力发挥作用时，两者的交互效应在短时间内属于二阶量。因此，替代动作下的反事实结果可近似为事实观测结果，加上世界模型对替代动作与事实动作预测结果之差。该方法不需要反事实样本、额外参数或重新训练。论文还推导了反事实误差上界与匹配的极小极大下界，并分析了方法在不连续、坐标折叠以及扰动会缩放控制指令等情况下的失效条件。

Result: 在四种渲染模拟器中，该方法将反事实决定系数从百分之三十八至百分之四十六提升到百分之八十六至百分之九十六；在水下航行器任务中，使用多十六倍事实数据后达到百分之九十至百分之九十四。更换为四种冻结视觉编码器后，在相同模拟器上仍达到百分之八十一至百分之九十五。回溯规划中，该方法在百分之三十五至百分之六十九的事件中找到原本可抵达目标的动作，而仅知道模拟器、但不知道实际结果的预言式规划器为百分之十四至百分之二十三。

Conclusion: 论文表明，尽管事实数据本身不足以识别一般的反事实结果，物理系统的局部平滑结构仍可提供有效约束，使模型从事实预测中构造准确的反事实估计。该方法在多种视觉表征和模拟任务上表现稳健，并有助于基于事后信息改进决策；但其适用性取决于动作与扰动的作用方式及动力学的平滑性。

Abstract: World models operate in a world they cannot fully observe. When a hidden disturbance, such as a current pushing a boat, changes the outcome of an action, a counterfactual question follows: what would have happened under a different action, subject to the same disturbance? Its answer separates bad decisions from bad luck. We prove that world models trained on factual transitions cannot answer it on their own, whatever their data or capacity. For many physical systems, the observed outcome and a simple property of the dynamics suffice: natura non facit saltus, nature makes no leaps. When actions and disturbances act as separate forces, their interaction is second-order in time, so the counterfactual outcome is, to first order, the observed outcome plus the difference between the world model's predictions under the alternative and factual actions. This yields a counterfactual world model with no counterfactual data, extra parameters, or retraining. For transitions of duration $h$, we prove an $O(h^2)$ bound on the counterfactual error relative to the disturbance variance, establish a matching minimax lower bound from unpaired factual data, and characterize failures at discontinuities, coordinate folds, and disturbances that scale the command. We validate these predictions from pixels with JEPA world models on frozen DINOv2 and masked autoencoder (MAE) encoders. On four rendered simulators, our approach improves counterfactual $R^2$ from $38$--$46\%$ to $86$--$96\%$ with the same world models; on a fifth, an underwater vehicle, it reaches $90$--$94\%$ with sixteen times more factual data. Four further frozen encoders, DINOv3, SigLIP~2, CLIP and EVA-02, give $81$--$95\%$ on the same simulators. In hindsight planning, it finds the action that would have reached the goal in $35$--$69\%$ of events, against $14$--$23\%$ for an oracle planner that knows the simulator but not the outcome.

</details>


### [218] [The geometry of neural network decision boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24557)
*Dylan M Paiton,David L Schultheiss,Matthias Kümmerer,Zac Cranko,Matthias Bethge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出直接在高维输入空间中可视化并量化神经网络决策边界的方法，发现训练方式对边界几何的影响显著强于网络架构或测试准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工神经网络虽由明确的数学运算构成，但其复杂决策过程难以直观理解。传统投影方法可能扭曲高维空间中的几何结构，因此需要直接研究输入空间内的决策边界。

Method: 提出一种在高维输入空间中直接分析决策边界几何的方法，测量边界的距离与曲率，并比较不同输入、网络架构和训练方法下的结果；同时考察对抗训练后，数据样本附近边界距离与曲率的变化。

Result: 随着所考察子空间增大，边界距离平稳、缓慢增长，曲率也保持一致，表明决策边界可能位于较大且连续的子空间中，并与输入样本较为接近。对抗训练会降低样本附近的边界距离和曲率。总体而言，训练方法对这两项几何量的影响明显强于架构或测试集准确率。

Conclusion: 该方法为直接研究神经网络决策边界提供了分析与实证基础，有助于检验既有边界行为理论，并推动网络可解释性及决策机制研究。

Abstract: Artificial neural networks (ANNs) are built using well-understood mathematical operations, yet their complex nature poses significant challenges to intuitively understanding their decision-making processes. This work introduces a method for visualizing and measuring ANN decision boundary geometry directly in the high-dimensional input space, avoiding distortions found with traditional projection-based techniques. We analyze boundary curvatures and distances in diverse inputs, architectures, and training methods. Boundary distances grow smoothly and slowly with consistent curvature as the size of the subspace increases, suggesting that decision boundaries are close to input samples within large, continuous subspaces. We also quantify how much boundary distances and curvatures reduce near dataset samples after adversarial training. Training methodology has a significantly stronger impact on boundary distance and curvature than architecture or test-set accuracy. These findings are contextualized within prior work, supporting or refuting several previously proposed theories of boundary behavior. Our analytically and empirically supported methodology lays the foundation for future studies to improve network interpretability and understanding.

</details>


### [219] [Learning Trajectories Leave Decodable Traces in Neural Network Weight Space and Internal Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24782)
*Patrick Krauss,Achim Schilling,Andreas Maier,Thomas Kinfe,Claus Metzner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 神经网络完成相同任务后虽形成彼此相关的成功解，但最终权重仍保留有关初始化、任务顺序和部分学习过程的信息，而非完整记录全部训练轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相同任务训练出的网络通常具有相似的权重结构，但彼此仍可区分。研究旨在判断这种差异是否只是不同的有效解，还是也保存了各自的学习历史。

Method: 研究者比较了网络训练前后的权重距离与隐藏层表征，并检验能否从最终权重识别初始化、解码先前任务的学习顺序、预测中间权重状态，以及重建初始权重。研究还考察了扰动后的网络是否沿原训练路径恢复，并通过权重插值评估不同解之间的损失障碍。

Result: 训练前的网络距离不能稳定预测训练后的距离，但所有三十个网络都能在每个数据集的十个候选初始化中被正确匹配。共享初始化的网络即使采用不同批次安排，最终隐藏表征仍具有可辨识的相似性；不过，无法仅凭与初始活动的直接比较识别网络。经过共同任务训练十轮后，线性探针仍能以百分之百的准确率解码八十个留出网络此前两项任务的顺序。中间权重状态只能部分预测，初始权重则无法可靠重建。扰动后的网络虽恢复了分类性能，却没有回到原有训练路径；这一现象在不同数据集、干预时点和网络层中均存在。二千一百六十对对齐终点中有二千一百五十七对在采样插值点上未出现正损失障碍，说明不同解之间存在低损失连接，而非都收敛到同一权重向量。

Conclusion: 共同任务会促使网络形成相关且有效的解，但不会抹去个体学习历史。最终权重选择性地保留了网络从何处开始、学习顺序如何等信息，却不是完整的训练轨迹记录；性能恢复也不意味着回到原先的学习路径。

Abstract: Neural networks trained on the same task develop related weight-space structures, yet remain distinguishable after learning. Do these differences merely reflect alternative solutions, or do they preserve information about individual learning histories? We tested whether final weights reveal initialization, task order, and earlier trajectory states. Training reorganized the ensemble: initial pairwise distances did not consistently predict final distances. Nevertheless, all 30 trained networks were correctly matched to their initial weights among ten candidates per dataset. Networks sharing an initialization also developed recognizable final hidden representations across different batch schedules, although direct comparisons with initial activity did not recover identity. Learning order remained readable as well. A linear probe decoded the order of two earlier tasks with 100% accuracy on 80 held-out networks after ten epochs of a common final task. Earlier intermediate weight states were partly predictable, but individual initial weights could not be reliably reconstructed. Final weights thus retain selective historical information rather than a complete trajectory record. Perturbed networks recovered classification performance without returning to their unperturbed training paths. This separation persisted across datasets, intervention times, and network layers. Moreover, 2,157 of 2,160 aligned endpoint pairs showed no positive loss barrier at sampled interpolation points, indicating low-loss connections between distinct solutions rather than convergence to one weight vector. Together, these findings show that a shared task constrains networks to related successful solutions without erasing their individual histories. Final weights reflect not only what a network has learned, but also where learning began and how it unfolded.

</details>


### [220] [From Activity Recognition to Shift Detection: Variational Wi-Fi Sensing with Evidential Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24596)
*Luca Cotti,Marco Cominelli,Francesco Gringoli,Lance M. Kaplan,Mani B. Srivastava,Trevor Bihl,Erik Blasch,Kara Combs,Daniel O. Brigham,Federico Cerutti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用多天线无线信道信息识别人类活动并检测部署环境变化的方法，在活动识别和跨环境变化检测方面取得较好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人体运动会改变无线信道，使普通无线保真设备能够用于无设备活动识别。然而，信道状态信息还受到人员、接收器位置、采集时段及周围环境影响，导致模型部署条件偏离训练条件后仍可能输出不可靠的活动标签。因此，除了提高识别准确率，还需要检测部署数据是否发生变化。

Method: 作者提出多天线架构“无线保真人体活动识别”。四个变分自编码器分别从原始第六代无线保真信道状态信息幅度中学习紧凑分布，再由延迟融合分类器综合各天线潜变量的均值与对数方差进行活动分类。针对部署变化，方法复用同一潜在表示，并以基于证据深度学习的类别条件不确定性分数进行检测；检测过程不使用变化后的样本进行模型选择或校准。潜在表示还可用于浅层决策树分析。

Result: 在包含十二种活动的公开基准上，多天线模型的识别准确率中位数为百分之九十六点六，高于独立优化的单天线模型（百分之七十点八至百分之八十一点五），表明不同天线提供了互补信息。在涉及接收器位置、参与者、采集时段或房间变化的十一个数据集上，变化检测的宏平均受试者工作特征曲线下面积为零点八九七；在实际分布内拒绝率为百分之五点二时，检出了百分之八十二点五的变化样本。不过，不同接收器位置下的检测表现差异明显。

Conclusion: 多天线融合能够提升无线保真人体活动识别效果，基于潜在表示的不确定性评估也能在多类部署变化下识别相当比例的分布外样本。此外，该紧凑表示支持较简单的决策树分析。需要注意的是，不确定性对物理环境变化的响应并不一致，实际部署时仍需关注具体位置条件下的检测可靠性。

Abstract: Human motion perturbs wireless channels, making commodity Wi-Fi devices a practical platform for device-free activity recognition. Yet Channel State Information (CSI) also depends strongly on the person, receiver position, recording session, and surrounding environment, so a classifier can continue producing activity labels after its deployment conditions have moved away from those seen during training. We propose Wi-HAR, a multi-antenna architecture in which four Variational Auto-Encoders learn compact distributions from raw Wi-Fi 6 CSI magnitudes and a delayed-fusion classifier combines their means and log-variances. On twelve activities from a public benchmark, Wi-HAR reaches 96.6% median recognition accuracy, compared with 70.8-81.5% for independently optimized single-antenna models, showing that the antennas provide complementary views of the same activity. We then use the same latent backbone to detect deployment shifts without using shifted samples for model selection or calibration. A class-conditional uncertainty score derived from Evidential Deep Learning achieves a macro-average ROC-AUC of 0.897 across eleven datasets that change receiver position, participant, recording session, or room, and detects 82.5% of shifted samples at a realized 5.2% in-distribution rejection rate. Detection performance nevertheless varies substantially across receiver positions, showing that the same physical change does not induce a uniform uncertainty response. The compact representation also supports shallow decision-tree analysis using variables from several antennas.

</details>


### [221] [Bias-Only Training Dynamics in ReLU Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24797)
*Shreyas Chaudhari,Jose M. F. Moura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究表明，固定随机权重、仅训练偏置的单隐层整流线性网络，其训练可视为由激活模式变化串联起来的一系列最小二乘问题；网络足够宽时，激活模式变化趋少，训练近似于基于初始激活模式的线性最小二乘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定随机权重、仅训练偏置的神经网络虽具有通用逼近能力，但其训练动力学尚未得到充分研究。论文旨在刻画这类网络如何训练，以及网络宽度对训练过程的影响。

Method: 以激活码（记录各隐藏神经元是否激活的二进制模式）为分析工具。作者证明，偏置梯度对每个输入的依赖可完全由其激活码描述；当激活码保持不变时，训练等价于以激活码为特征的线性最小二乘问题。激活状态发生切换时，训练则转入新的最小二乘问题，由此将整体训练描述为一系列分段过程。

Result: 理论分析显示，网络宽度增加时，发生激活切换的神经元比例趋于零；在无限宽极限下，训练动力学趋近于基于初始激活码的线性最小二乘。实验结果与理论一致：较窄网络中的激活切换可能显著影响训练，而其相对频率及对训练动力学的影响会随宽度增加而减弱。

Conclusion: 激活码为理解偏置专属训练提供了有效框架。该训练可解释为随激活状态变化而切换的一系列最小二乘问题，并在无限宽时简化为基于初始激活结构的线性训练过程。

Abstract: Neural networks with randomly initialized, fixed weights and trainable biases are universal approximators, yet the dynamics of bias-only training remain largely unexplored. We study these dynamics in one-hidden-layer ReLU networks through activation codes, the binary patterns of active neurons that partition the input space into convex polyhedral regions. We show that the gradients with respect to the biases depend on each input only through its activation code, and hence the training dynamics can be characterized with these codes. In particular, as long as the activation codes remain fixed, training reduces to a linear least-squares problem with the codes as features. Training can then be understood as a sequence of least-squares problems, with transitions occurring when neurons change their activation states. We show that the fraction of switching neurons vanishes as the network widens, and the training dynamics approach those of linear least squares on the initial activation codes in the infinite-width limit. Our experiments support these theoretical findings, showing that while activation switches can noticeably affect training in narrower networks, their relative frequency and the associated changes in the dynamics diminish as the width increases.

</details>


### [222] [Morpho-Spectral Projection Analysis (MSPA) for Plant Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24599)
*Lucas Prado Osco,Marcelo Santos da Silva,Filipe Vieira Silva,Liang Zhao,Ana Paula Marques Ramos,Ednaldo Jose Ferreira,Roberto Dantas de Medeiros,Amaury Burlamaqui Bendahan,Lucio Andre de Castro Jorge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出形态—光谱投影分析方法，将连续植物光谱压缩为兼顾吸收特征局部形态与全局判别信息的低维表示；在大幅降维的同时，整体分类表现优于原始光谱及多种传统降维方法，并显著加快推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 植物在可见光、近红外和短波红外中的连续反射光谱包含叶片生化与解剖性状信息，但相邻波段高度相关，且波段数量相对样本量过多，给预测建模带来困难。常见降维方法将波段视为无序特征，忽视连续光谱中吸收结构的局部变化及其微分几何特征，因此可能损失有用信息。

Method: 提出一种有监督的形态—光谱投影算子，包含三个互补部分：一是提取去连续谱后的吸收深度、吸收面积和红边拐点变化特征；二是在连续辐射子窗口内计算零阶、一阶和二阶导数对应的局部多阶奇异特征模态；三是构建由费舍尔判别信息加权的全局典型子空间，并通过迹归一化的各向同性协方差收缩进行正则化。研究使用六种经典机器学习分类器和四种表格基础模型进行验证。

Result: 验证涵盖五类植物系统、8,963条光谱。将原始1,580个波段压缩约96%后，该方法在92.0%的基准测试中优于完整光谱，在90.0%的模型—作物评估中优于传统降维方法。全局平均宏平均F1值由0.7756提升至0.8520，情境学习推理速度最高提升126.5倍。

Conclusion: 结果表明，保留连续光谱的局部形态结构，并结合全局判别信息，有助于在大幅降低维度的同时改善分类效果和推理效率。该方法可作为独立的光谱投影步骤接入光谱分析流程；其适用范围和泛化能力仍需结合更多植物、任务及外部数据进一步评估。

Abstract: Plant reflectance spectroscopy across the visible, near-infrared, and shortwave infrared domain encodes foliar biochemical and anatomical traits into contiguous spectral signatures with locally differentiable structure; however, extreme inter-band collinearity and unfavorable sample-to-feature ratios challenge predictive modeling. Conventional dimensionality reduction methods routinely adopted in spectroscopy, are column-permutation invariant: they treat adjacent wavelengths as unordered tabular columns and discard the sequential differential geometry of absorption features. Here, we propose Morpho-Spectral Projection Analysis (MSPA), a supervised dimensionality reduction operator that projects contiguous spectra into a compact morpho-spectral subspace through three complementary blocks: (i) additive continuum-removed absorption depth, area, and red-edge inflection kinematics; (ii) local multi-order singular eigenmodes (0th, 1st, and 2nd derivatives) partitioned across contiguous radiative sub-windows; and (iii) global Fisher-weighted canonical subspaces regularized via trace-normalized isotropic covariance shrinkage. We validated MSPA across 8,963 spectra spanning five plant systems, coupled with six classical machine learning classifiers and four Tabular Foundation Models (TabPFN v3, TabICL v2, TabDPT v1.2, and TabFM). Despite compressing the 1,580-band spectrum by 96%, MSPA outperformed the full spectrum in 92.0% of all benchmarks and conventional reducers in 90.0% of all model-crop evaluations, raising the global mean macro-F1 from 0.7756 (full spectrum) to 0.8520, accelerating in-context inference by up to 126.5x. Designed as a self-contained spectral projection operator, the MSPA method can be directly integrated into spectroscopy workflows via the open-source code available at GitHub: https://github.com/Lucas-Osco/mspa.

</details>


### [223] [Auditing black-box clinical prediction models with a large language model through queries alone: a blinded test with planted flaws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24877)
*Zhiyu Liu,Xinyu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究检验大语言模型能否准确解释临床预测模型、发现人为植入的缺陷并从模型中提出新关系。结果显示，它能较忠实地近似模型并识别大多数植入缺陷，但其问题报告和新发现仍须分别用模型行为与独立患者数据核验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型被寄望于解释临床预测模型的计算逻辑、发现模型错误，甚至揭示新的医学知识。然而，这些能力需要通过可检验的标准评估，尤其要区分模型确实包含的规律与语言模型的误报或过度解读。

Method: 研究者利用国际卒中试验数据，为两种结局训练了四类预测模型：逻辑回归、可解释提升机、梯度提升树和神经网络。研究在二十四个模型副本中各植入一种缺陷，并与八个无缺陷模型混合。受测大语言模型在盲测条件下，每个模型审查三次，只能查询模型预测；评判规则预先确定，并由程序执行。研究还评估其替代模型对原模型的拟合程度、缺陷识别能力、问题报告准确性，以及从无缺陷模型中读出的关系能否在后续随机入组患者中验证。

Result: 大语言模型生成的替代模型平均包含一百二十八个项，对原模型对数优势的平均决定系数为百分之九十七点六；分析人员拟合的四十二项加性替代模型为百分之九十五点九。它在七十二次审查中识别出六十九次植入缺陷，检出率为百分之九十六；专门编写的程序则识别出全部二十四个缺陷。另一方面，在一千零八十九条可核验的问题报告中，百分之六十未通过预先规定的模型平均行为检验；事后复查显示，其中多数对应真实但幅度较小的模型变化，另有百分之十五的报告并未出现在模型中。对于从无缺陷模型中读出的六百零一项意外关系，百分之十一在后续患者中成立，低于回归扫描所筛出的强候选关系的百分之二十八。

Conclusion: 仅凭查询权限，大语言模型能够较忠实地描述黑箱临床预测模型，并识别绝大多数人为植入的缺陷；但它并未显示出超越专用程序的缺陷检测优势。其对模型问题的报告可能误判或夸大细微变化，其提出的新关系也只有一部分能在独立患者数据中复现，因此必须分别对照模型本身和独立数据进行验证。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are used to explain clinical prediction models, on the promise that they can say what a model computes, find its faults and even reveal new knowledge in it. We tested the three promises where the answers could be checked. From the International Stroke Trial we trained four kinds of model (logistic regression, explainable boosting machine, gradient-boosted trees, neural network) for two outcomes on patients randomised earlier, planted one flaw in each of 24 copies (an under-predicted subgroup, a reversed risk factor or an implausible step) and mixed them with the 8 clean models. GPT-6 Astra audited each model three times, blind, through a tool that only returned the model's predictions; the judging rules were committed before any run and applied by program. Its surrogate models, with a median of 128 terms, reproduced the audited models' log-odds with a mean $R^2$ of 0.976 (0.959 for an additive surrogate of 42 terms fitted by an analyst). It found the planted flaw in 69 of 72 runs (96%; 95% CI 80% to 99%); a script written for the purpose found all 24. It also reported many more problems: 60% of 1,089 checkable reports failed the pre-specified check against the model's average behaviour; post hoc, most of these were real but small steps, and 15% of the checkable reports were not found in the model. Of 601 unexpected relationships it read out of the clean models, 11% held in patients randomised later (random pairs 6%; a regression scan's shorter list of its strongest candidates, 28%); after a check on the training data, 33% against 30%. With query access alone, an LLM can describe a black-box model faithfully and find nearly every planted flaw, as a purpose-written script also did; its reports of problems must be checked on the model and its discoveries on data.

</details>


### [224] [Evaluating the Effectiveness of Location Encoding Methods for Species Distribution Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24622)
*Christian Lange,Oisin Mac Aodha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立标准化评估框架，比较不同地点编码对物种分布范围估计的作用，并覆盖局部与全球尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物种空间分布估计是生态学的重要问题。现有研究多关注统计模型或机器学习方法的选择，但输入特征同样会影响估计效果。气候变量、学习式编码、地球嵌入表示和变换后的地理坐标等选项日益丰富，亟需在统一标准下进行比较。

Method: 提出一套标准化评估框架，以稀疏物种观测为基础，考察不同地点编码在物种分布范围估计任务中的效果，并分别在局部尺度和全球尺度开展评估。

Result: 摘要说明研究对不同地点编码的有效性进行了量化比较，但未提供具体的比较结果、性能指标或哪种编码表现最佳，因此无法据此判断各类编码的优劣。

Conclusion: 该研究将地点编码作为独立于模型选择的重要因素加以系统评估，为比较物种分布估计中的输入特征提供了统一框架。摘要未披露具体实验结论，不能进一步推断最佳编码方案。

Abstract: Understanding the spatial distribution of species is a key question in ecology. A variety of solutions, from statistical, traditional machine learning, through to deep learning-based methods have been proposed. These methods take sparse observations for a species of interest as input and aim to generate dense spatial outputs indicating the spatial range of the species. Orthogonal to the choice of statistical model used is the question of what input features are most informative. Thanks to recent developments, the number of options has greatly expanded, with choices spanning different climate variables, learning-based encodings, earth embedding models, or raw transformed geographic coordinates. In this work, we introduce a standardized evaluation framework and quantify the effectiveness of different location encodings in the context of species range estimation, both at the local and global scale.

</details>


### [225] [Equal Steps, Unequal Curvature: Where Muon's Update Goes Wrong](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24644)
*Biswa Sengupta,Jinhua Wang,Léo Maxime Brunswic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，穆昂优化器按谱范数极陡方向分配更新，并不一定符合损失曲率；按实测曲率调整更新后，分离处理注意力中的查询、键和值矩阵，以及在训练冷却阶段进行轻度谱倾斜，均能降低损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 穆昂优化器沿动量矩阵的极分解方向更新权重，这在谱范数意义下是最陡下降方向，却不考虑损失曲率。因此，它会给动量的各个奇异方向相同步长，并依据神经元的杠杆分数分配非方阵中的步长。关键问题是这些分配是否适合实际曲率，而仅凭最陡下降论证无法回答。

Method: 研究者使用留出的梯度与海森向量积，将穆昂更新按奇异值区间和神经元群组分解，并计算各组在二阶近似下的最优更新倍率；同时形式化证明了何种条件下，这类诊断足以支持在保持穆昂原有更新范数时进行干预。随后通过逐步更新范数匹配、调优基线和配对随机种子，比较多种干预方案。

Result: 在一亿二千四百万参数的解码器实验中，最优倍率从高奇异值方向到谱尾单调上升，说明穆昂对谱顶端的高曲率方向更新过大。曲率诊断预测并经留出损失验证：训练中期及冷却初期，将步长从谱顶转移出去有效，但冷却末期无效。将查询、键和值矩阵分别正交化，相比合并处理降低损失零点零二五零纳特，并解释了分块方案的全部收益。在此基础上，冷却阶段采用仅需矩阵乘法、具有旋转等变性的奇异值负四分之一次幂倾斜，进一步降低损失零点零一二零纳特；与另一种方法结合时共降低零点零一五八纳特，基线约需多百分之四点三的训练词元才能达到相同效果。在三亿五千五百万参数规模下，该倾斜降低损失零点零一八八纳特；按能量匹配的迹范数权重衰减又额外降低零点零零五二纳特。相反，精确极分解、基于输入白化的最陡下降以及偏重谱顶的衰减均未带来收益。

Conclusion: 优化器的几何合理性并不保证其更新分配与损失曲率相匹配。按方向和群组测量二阶曲率，能够识别更新失准，并指导有条件、范数受控的干预。实验表明，针对实测失准的结构化调整可稳定改善训练效果，而没有作用于该失准的其他几何改造则未必有效。

Abstract: Muon updates each hidden weight matrix along the polar factor of its momentum, the steepest-descent direction under the spectral norm. That choice fixes two allocations without consulting the loss: every singular direction of the momentum receives the same step, and in a non-square matrix each neuron's step is set by its leverage score. Whether they are good is a question about curvature, which steepest-descent arguments do not answer; we measure it. Using held-out gradients and Hessian-vector products, we decompose Muon's step by singular-value band and by neuron group and compute each group's second-order optimal multiplier. In a 124M-parameter decoder trained on FineWeb-Edu, the multipliers rise monotonically from the top singular directions to the tail: Muon over-steps the high-curvature top of its spectrum. We prove in Lean when such a diagnostic licenses an intervention at Muon's own update norm. On the measured curvature, the criterion predicts, and held-out losses confirm, that moving step length off the top helps mid-training and early in the cooldown, but not at its end. We then test interventions under per-step norm matching, tuned baselines and paired seeds. Orthogonalizing Q, K and V separately rather than as one fused matrix lowers the loss by 0.0250 nats and accounts for all of the gain of block-wise variants. A matmul-only, rotation-equivariant spectral tilt $σ^{-1/4}$ applied in the cooldown lowers the loss of this stronger baseline by a further 0.0120 nats, additively with NorMuon (0.0158 nats together, three seeds; the baseline needs about 4.3% more tokens to match), and by 0.0188 nats at 355M parameters (three seeds). Energy-matched Schatten-1 weight decay adds a further 0.0052 nats. Geometrically principled changes that do not act on the measured miscalibration, namely exact polar factors, steepest-descent input whitening and top-heavy decay, do not help.

</details>


### [226] [Zonotopic Analysis of Quantization Error in Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24648)
*Robert Debbas,Éric Goubault,Sylvie Putot*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于 zonotope 的量化神经网络验证方法，联合处理舍入与截断，并通过受约束 zonotope 提高误差界精度；在部分测试条件下，精度与速度均优于已有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化神经网络通过降低数值精度提升计算效率，但离散舍入和截断操作使量化误差难以可靠界定，进而增加网络验证的难度。论文旨在为量化的 ReLU 前馈网络提供既可靠又高效的误差分析框架。

Method: 论文构建基于 zonotope 的抽象解释框架，并提出统一的舍入—截断抽象变换，同时处理这两类操作。进一步引入带线性约束的受约束 zonotope，以收紧误差界，并推导可靠的量化误差表示。作者还证明了该抽象变换的可靠性及其仿射条带最优性，并证明完整的受约束形式具有凸包最优性。

Result: 在航空碰撞避免基准测试中，针对两种较小扰动半径，受约束 zonotope 方法相较于符号化离散舍入抽象传播，在平均误差界宽度上减少约百分之七十至八十五，运行时间减少约百分之六十五至八十。普通 zonotope 方法也能给出可靠且有竞争力的界，平均速度约快近三百倍。不过，性能优势并非适用于所有位宽；在较大扰动半径下，符号化离散舍入抽象传播得到的界更紧。

Conclusion: 该研究为量化神经网络验证提供了具有理论保证的舍入与截断联合抽象方法。受约束 zonotope 在部分扰动条件下显著改善精度与效率，普通 zonotope 则展现出很高的计算速度；但方法优劣会随扰动半径和位宽变化，实际应用中需结合验证精度与运行时间选择。

Abstract: Quantized neural networks achieve computational efficiency by reducing precision, but bounding their quantization error remains a challenge due to discrete rounding and clamping operations. We present a zonotope-based abstract interpretation framework for quantized ReLU feed-forward network verification, introducing a unified round-clamp abstract transformer that jointly handles both operations. We extend this framework to constrained zonotopes, which add linear constraints to tighten bounds, and derive sound quantization error representations. We establish soundness and affine-strip optimality of the round-clamp abstract transformer, as well as convex-hull optimality of its complete constrained formulation. On the ACAS Xu benchmarks, our constrained zonotope method reduces average error-bound widths by approximately 70-85% and runtimes by 65-80% compared to QEBVerif's symbolic DRA propagation at the two smallest tested perturbation radii (r=3 and r=6). These improvements are not uniform across bitwidths, and symbolic DRA achieves tighter bounds at larger radii. Our plain zonotope method delivers competitive sound bounds nearly 300 times faster on average.

</details>


### [227] [Stealing Exact Training Data from Other Clients during Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24689)
*Omkar Shrikanth,Soham Dambalkar,Parag Jyoti Pal,Advik Raj Basani,Arnab K. Paul*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种迭代式梯度反演攻击，逐步分离联邦学习聚合更新中的句子贡献，实现精确文本恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中的恶意参与者可能利用模型更新反推出客户端的私有数据。虽然已有针对文本的梯度反演研究，但联邦平均会混合多个客户端的梯度，使单个句子的信号难以辨认，因此此前恶意客户端尚无法精确恢复文本。

Method: 攻击流程通过迭代逐步解除梯度混合：每轮先从聚合更新中精确恢复部分句子，再减去这些句子对应的梯度贡献，从而显露尚未恢复的句子，并在后续迭代中继续恢复。

Result: 该方法首次实现了精确句子恢复，已恢复句子的精确率为百分之百。对于聚合文本量最高达到模型隐层维度四倍的情况，最多可精确恢复百分之八十点二的客户端句子；在这一规模下，现有攻击无法精确恢复任何句子。

Conclusion: 研究表明，即使联邦平均将多个客户端的更新混合，攻击者仍可能通过逐步剥离已识别句子的梯度贡献，恢复大量私有文本。这凸显了联邦学习模型更新所带来的隐私风险。

Abstract: Malicious adversaries can employ gradient inversion attacks to reverse-engineer private data using the model updates of a round in Federated Learning. While there have been developments in gradient inversion for text, exact text recovery by a malicious client has so far remained infeasible, as FedAvg entangles the gradient contributions of many clients and obscures the signal of any individual sentence. We introduce a novel attack pipeline that unwinds this entanglement progressively through an iterative loop, in which the gradient contributions of sentences recovered exactly in one round are subtracted from the aggregate, exposing the remaining sentences for recovery in the next iteration. We show, for the first time, exact sentence recovery, with 100% precision, and on aggregates containing up to $4\times$ more tokens than the model's hidden dimension, we recover up to 80.2% of client sentences exactly, a regime where existing attacks recover none exactly.

</details>


### [228] [Meta-LSQ: A Closed-Form Sobolev Inner Solver for Few-Shot Meta-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24718)
*Antonin Sulc*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 元学习最小二乘法用固定随机傅里叶基和一次正则化最小二乘求解适配新任务，只需学习三个标量；它在部分平滑回归与束流调校任务中效率和准确性突出，但对非平滑目标及基函数覆盖范围之外的目标存在局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于梯度的元学习方法需要在任务内反复更新参数并对更新过程求导，训练成本较高，且通常要元学习整个网络。本文希望通过更简单、快速且可利用导数观测的任务适配方式，降低元学习的计算开销。

Method: 为所有任务共享固定的随机傅里叶特征基，并针对每个任务通过一次带正则项的最小二乘求解拟合模型。跨任务只学习三个标量：特征带宽、岭正则强度和导数观测权重。由于正弦基函数求导后仍属于同一函数族，方法可纳入导数数据，并能解析计算任意阶导数。研究还比较了高斯过程、基于梯度的元学习及其他模型，并考察了广义交叉验证选择正则强度的表现。

Result: 在十样本正弦回归中，训练耗时为14秒，最终误差比一阶模型无关元学习低5.5倍，而后者训练时间长36倍；高斯过程对照实验表明，共享任务间核函数是主要收益来源之一。在德国电子同步加速器中心阿瑞斯设施的模拟束流调校中，利用模型导数时，该方法略优于阿尔帕卡式模型，明显优于梯度下降。覆盖完整目标束流范围的模拟和仅用测量数据的实验中，其最佳设置的中位误差为7.3微米；已发表的强化学习最终束流中位误差为14.4微米，但在300次小目标试验中，强化学习的中位误差更低。真实传感器校准数据上，带学习先验均值的方法在四个目标中的两个优于模型无关元学习，但精确高斯过程和阿尔帕卡式模型在多数目标上更准确；对非平滑目标则表现失效。此外，对基函数张成范围之外的无噪声目标，广义交叉验证等样本内准则会选出过小的正则强度。

Conclusion: 该方法以极低的元学习复杂度实现了快速任务适配，尤其适合平滑、可由共享傅里叶基描述且拥有导数观测的任务；其表现也说明，共享核结构本身可能是性能提升的重要来源。不过，它并非普遍优于高斯过程或其他方法：非平滑目标、基函数表达能力不足以及正则强度选择不当，都会限制其可靠性。

Abstract: Gradient-based meta-learners such as model-agnostic meta-learning (MAML) adapt to a new task with an inner loop of gradient descent that has to be differentiated through, and they meta-learn every network weight. Meta-LSQ instead fits each task with one regularised least-squares solve over a fixed random Fourier basis and meta-learns only three scalars, a bandwidth, a ridge and a derivative weight. Since the derivative of a sinusoid is again a sinusoid, the same solve can also take derivative observations, and the fitted model has exact closed-form derivatives of any order. On 10-shot sinusoid regression Meta-LSQ trains in 14 s and ends with a 5.5 times lower error than a first-order MAML baseline that trains 36 times longer, and a Gaussian-process control suggests that most of this gain comes from sharing one kernel across tasks. In simulated beam tuning at DESY's ARES facility with model derivatives it is slightly more accurate than an ALPaCA-style model and well ahead of gradient descent. Over DESY's full range of target beams, again in simulation and from measurements alone, its best setting has a median error of 7.3 $μ$m, against 14.4 for the published final beams of their reinforcement-learning agent, which has the lower median on their 300 trials of small targets. On real sensor-calibration data a learned prior mean puts it ahead of MAML on two of four targets, but an exact Gaussian process with the same change and an ALPaCA-style model stay more accurate on most targets, and it breaks down on non-smooth targets. On noiseless targets outside the span of the basis, in-sample criteria such as generalised cross-validation choose too small a ridge.

</details>


### [229] [Approximate Dynamic Programming with Basis Function Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24737)
*Mohammadreza Fathi,Chi-Guhn Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出从模型中迭代学习价值函数基函数的方法，并证明其近似改进与收敛性质；实验显示，该方法在库存和排队控制问题上优于已有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近似线性规划依赖基函数来近似马尔可夫决策过程的价值函数。既有基函数通常预先指定、随机生成或受限于特定结构，因而可能限制近似精度，也缺少持续改进现有近似的机制。大规模问题还面临约束数量过多、计算成本高的困难。

Method: 提出贝尔曼算子基函数学习方法：反复对当前价值估计应用贝尔曼算子，并据此生成新的基函数。其收缩版本限制基函数数量，以控制每轮计算量；针对约束规模过大的问题，还提出结合约束抽样与基函数学习的混合版本。论文为这些方法给出近似改进、收敛或有限终止保证，并明确说明混合方法保证成立的条件。

Result: 理论上，每个新学习到的基函数都能带来可量化的近似改进；基本方法可收敛至最优价值函数，收缩版本保留相应保证。混合版本在明确条件下也保留改进与有限终止保证。数值实验涵盖连续易腐品库存控制和排队控制问题，结果表明这些方法显著优于已有方法。

Conclusion: 该研究将贝尔曼算子用于自动构造和迭代改进基函数，为近似线性规划提供了一种兼顾理论保证与计算效率的价值函数近似方案。通过限制基函数数量或抽样约束，该方法还可适应大规模问题。

Abstract: This work presents a novel approach for approximating the value function in Markov decision processes (MDPs). Our approach is grounded in the approximate linear programming (ALP) framework, wherein basis functions (BFs) play an important role. Traditionally, BFs have been pre-defined, randomly generated, or restricted to specific structures, which both limits the accuracy that can be achieved and leaves no mechanism for improving a given approximation. We instead learn BFs from the model itself, by repeatedly applying the Bellman operator to the current value estimate. We introduce the Bellman operator basis function (BOBF) learning method, and prove that each learned BF improves the approximation by a quantifiable amount and that the method converges to the optimal value function. Its shrunken counterpart (SBOBF) retains this guarantee while capping the number of BFs, and therefore the computational effort per iteration. For large-scale problems, where the number of constraints is prohibitive, we develop hybrid variants (HBOBF and SHBOBF) that combine constraint sampling with BF learning, and we show that the improvement and finite-termination guarantees are preserved under explicitly stated conditions. Numerical experiments on (continuous) perishable inventory and queueing control problems demonstrate that our BF learning techniques significantly outperform existing methods in the literature.

</details>


### [230] [Uneven gains in protein-RNA affinity and mutation-effect prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24739)
*Shangkun Sima,Tongfei Shen,Tianchi Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究显示，提升总体亲和力预测能改善平均突变效应，但不必然提升突变排序或局部变化预测；评估时应同时考察这三类能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确预测突变前后的配对亲和力，是衡量突变效应的基础。然而，总体亲和力误差降低，未必能转化为平均突变效应、突变排序和局部定量变化的同步改善，因此需要分别评估这些指标，并分析性能变化来自何处。

Method: 研究比较了十一类方法，并针对各方法采用相应的匹配样本群体。通过受控亲和力预测实验，分析二百二十组突变配对（来自二十个家族）；另以独立外部测试集验证，测试集包含三个来源匹配家族的二十四组定量配对。研究还通过误差分解、特征追踪、固定权重的输入控制及匹配监督干预，探查突变响应受限的原因。

Result: 受控方法将绝对亲和力的平均绝对误差从每单位亲和力对数值一点二八九八降至一点一四三一。在二百二十组突变配对中，该方法产生了更强的突变响应，并提高平均效应预测准确度；排序方面的收益不确定，平均中心化局部误差则未降低。外部测试中，三个家族的绝对亲和力误差和常规突变效应误差均下降；平均中心化局部误差从零点四一九八降至零点三七六一，其中两个家族改善，一个家族误差上升。误差分解显示，收益主要来自突变景观均值的改善。

Conclusion: 整体亲和力预测的改进可以提升平均突变效应预测，但不能据此推断排序能力或局部定量变化也会改善。研究强调，评估突变预测方法时，应联合考察平均效应、排序和局部变化，并进一步识别限制模型突变响应的因素。

Abstract: Accurate matched affinities determine mutation effects, but gains in aggregate affinity prediction may transfer unevenly to average effects, ordering and local accuracy. We evaluated eleven method families using method-specific matched populations. Controlled CoPRA reduced absolute-affinity mean absolute error from 1.2898 to 1.1431 pKd. Across 220 mutation pairs from 20 families, it generated stronger responses and improved mean-effect accuracy, with uncertain ranking gains and no decrease in mean centered local error. In an independent external test (24 quantitative pairs from three source-matched families), absolute and ordinary mutation-effect errors fell in all three families. Mean centered local error fell from 0.4198 to 0.3761 pKd, with improvements in two families and higher error in one. Error decomposition localized gains in landscape means. PRA-Pred feature tracing, fixed-weight M2-PRNet input controls and matched supervision interventions identified specific restrictions on mutation response. These results establish the need to evaluate average effects, ordering and quantitative local variation jointly.

</details>


### [231] [Reconstruction vs. Prediction Pre-Training of Discrete Video Representations for Continuous Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24781)
*Ron Kosova,Francesco Croce*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究在匹配模型容量的条件下比较重建式与预测式视频自监督目标，发现二者形成不同的动力学表征：前者更忠实于物理状态，后者更适合长期自回归预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频编码器常通过像素重建或潜在特征预测进行自监督训练，但不同训练目标如何影响下游动力学表征仍不明确。为隔离目标本身的作用，研究在相同编码器结构和容量下进行对比。

Method: 在连续控制环境中，研究训练两个结构相同、共享有限标量量化瓶颈的视频编码器，分别采用自动编码重建目标和潜在特征预测目标。研究还设计了非对称离散化方案，使预测模型也能使用相同瓶颈，并通过状态信息、单步潜在预测、自回归滚动及结构探针进行评估。

Result: 自动编码器保留了显著更多的物理状态信息，单步潜在预测表现也略好；但在自回归滚动中，预测式编码器累积的误差更少。结构探针显示，预测式编码器对低层视觉扰动更稳健，并且更依赖时间上下文，这可能解释其长期预测优势。

Conclusion: 重建式与预测式目标会诱导出不同的动力学表征：重建式目标偏向状态保真度，预测式目标则更有利于长期滚动预测。两者体现了状态信息保留与长时域预测能力之间的权衡。

Abstract: Self-supervised video encoders are commonly trained to either reconstruct pixels or predict latent features, yet it remains unclear how these objectives shape representations for downstream dynamics. We present a capacity-matched study isolating this choice: two identical video encoders with a shared FSQ bottleneck are trained under autoencoding and V-JEPA objectives on a continuous control environment. We introduce a simple asymmetric discretization scheme to apply the same bottleneck to the predictive model. The resulting representations exhibit a clear trade-off: the autoencoder preserves substantially more physical state information and achieves slightly better one-step latent prediction, while the predictive encoder accumulates less error under autoregressive rollout. Structural probes suggest that this advantage is associated with greater robustness to low-level visual perturbations and stronger dependence on temporal context. These results indicate that predictive and reconstructive objectives induce distinct representations of dynamics, trading state fidelity for properties that may benefit long-horizon prediction.

</details>


### [232] [Prognosis Without Prediction: The Intrinsic Geometry of Survival](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24799)
*Atahan Karagoz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出自组织生存流形框架，通过无监督学习构建多组学数据的潜在几何结构，并据此预测肾透明细胞癌患者的生存风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维生物数据中的结局往往稀疏、含噪或受删失影响，依赖结局标签进行监督回归的模型因此受到限制。论文希望减少对明确生存标签和监督式特征筛选的依赖，将疾病进展视为潜在空间中的几何演化过程。

Method: 框架将异质多组学数据映射至统一潜在空间，并利用按伪时间加权、以曲率最小化为目标的图拉普拉斯结构组织患者轨迹；随后依据潜在流形上的几何关系刻画生存进程与风险。研究在一组包含一百八十四名患者的肾透明细胞癌多组学队列中，采用确定性的五折交叉验证评估模型，并分析高低风险组的生存差异及局部流形耗散。

Result: 模型的平均一致性指数为零点七六八，标准差为零点零六八；交叉验证折外风险分层显示显著的生存差异，显著性检验小于千分之一。高风险患者的局部流形耗散增加百分之十四点七。作者还报告，编码器权重所反映的特征与已知的代谢及肿瘤微环境相关疾病进展因素相符。

Conclusion: 研究表明，基于无监督潜在几何结构的建模方法在该肾癌队列中具有预后分层潜力，并可能补充依赖结局回归的传统方法。不过，摘要仅报告单一癌种、有限规模队列的结果，尚不足以证明其可推广至其他生物、工业或金融系统；仍需独立外部队列验证，并进一步检验流形耗散等指标的稳健性与生物学解释。

Abstract: Modeling systemic failure in high-dimensional environments is fundamentally limited by supervised algorithms that attempt to predict sparse, noisy or censored endpoints. To break this reliance on explicit outcome regression, we engineered the Self-Organizing Survival Manifolds (SOSM) framework, a deep learning architecture that decouples unsupervised representation learning from survival labels and treats time-to-event progression as an emergent geometric property. The system projects heterogeneous multi-omics arrays into a unified latent space governed by a pseudotime-weighted, curvature-minimizing graph Laplacian, organizing patient trajectories along low-dissipation geodesic flows. To validate the framework, we applied this architecture to a 184-patient clear cell renal cell carcinoma multi-omics cohort. Across a deterministic 5-fold cross-validation pipeline, SOSM achieved a mean Concordance Index of $0.768 \pm 0.068$, while out-of-fold risk stratification yielded significant survival divergence (log-rank $p < 0.001$) accompanied by a 14.7% increase in local manifold dissipation among high-risk patients. Furthermore, interrogating the network's encoder weights confirmed that the model autonomously recovered canonical metabolic and microenvironmental drivers of disease progression without supervised feature selection. By demonstrating that systemic failure can be mapped as a geometric phase transition, SOSM establishes a generalizable computational engine for prognostic modeling across biological, industrial and financial complex systems.

</details>


### [233] [Synthesizing hyperspectral Data using generative Models to train spectral Unmixing Methods for low-cost Crop Residue Cover Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24827)
*Ege Artan,Shuo Chen,Andrew Peng,Sammuel Aldrich Karya,Kyaw Thiha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究提出将光谱数据插值生成与丰度估计分离的解混框架，利用条件生成模型扩充稀疏地面数据；实验表明，若未严格融入光谱先验，生成数据可能失真，进而损害解混效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用于作物残茬高光谱制图的卫星光谱范围带来较大的解混难度，缺少可靠的标准光谱指数，因而需要复杂的数据驱动模型。然而，这类模型通常依赖密集且分布充分的数据，而真实地面标注数据稀少、获取成本高。研究旨在更充分地利用现有数据，并将光谱物理先验纳入解混流程。

Method: 作者提出一种将数据插值生成与丰度映射估计解耦的框架：先通过条件生成模型扩展光谱数据，再由独立解混模型估计残茬覆盖丰度。生成模型包括带有构形层的一维条件编码器—解码器，以及双路径变换器条件变分自编码器；解混模型包括多层感知机和傅里叶神经算子。生成数据旨在近似总体风险所对应的数据分布。

Result: 实验显示，解耦框架的效果取决于对光谱先验的严格整合。若先验不足，生成模型可能出现域坍缩、模式坍缩或产生虚构光谱，导致生成的数据流形偏离真实分布，最终使解混性能不理想，并难以适应分布外数据。

Conclusion: 条件生成扩充数据为缓解地面样本稀缺提供了思路，但生成数据并不会自动提升解混效果。该框架需要将可靠的光谱先验明确融入生成过程，并严格验证生成分布的真实性，才能避免错误的数据流形损害后续丰度估计。

Abstract: The spectral range of the Field Imaging Nanosatellite for Crop residue Hyperspectral Mapping (FINCH), developed by the University of Toronto Aerospace Team's Space System Division, poses significant challenges for hyperspectral unmixing to determine crop residue cover fractional abundances. The severe lack of standard indices necessitates the use of complex, data-driven unmixing models. Complex unmixing models require dense, well-distributed manifolds, whereas ground-truth datasets are sparse and expensive. To better leverage the information content in existing ground-truth datasets, this study presents a framework that decouples interpolation and abundance-mapping estimation via an intermediary conditional data generator, contrasting with traditional single-model unmixing pipelines. This decoupling enables the inclusion of physical prior knowledge about spectra, which is not natively accessible to unmixing models, thereby artificially expanding the dataset to serve as a Monte Carlo approximation of the population risk. To test this hypothesis, we have proposed two generator models: a 1D Conditional U-Net with Conformer Layers (GD-Streamline) and a Dual-Path Transformer Conditional Variational AutoEncoder (TCVAE), and two unmixing models: Multi-Layered Perceptrons (MLP) and Fourier Neural Operators (FNO). The results of the study indicate a need for rigorous integration and introduction of spectral priors within the scope of the proposed decoupling framework to prevent domain and mode collapse and hallucination by the generator models; otherwise, this leads to falsely approximated data manifolds, ultimately resulting in unsatisfactory and out-of-distribution unmixing performance.

</details>


### [234] [Overlooked and Underutilized: Prototypes are the Missing Piece in Tabular XAI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24831)
*Jacek Karolczak,Jerzy Stefanowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文主张将原型及其局部组成部分纳入表格数据的可解释人工智能，以降低理解负担，并补充特征归因方法在数据流形有效性方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险表格任务中的可解释方法过度依赖特征归因，要求非专业用户逐项理解大量孤立特征，认知负担较重。原型作为直观的相似性参照尚未得到充分利用；尤其是由相关特征构成的原型部分，可能比单个特征更贴近人类理解概念的方式。

Method: 本文从认知与几何两个角度论证原型的价值：认知层面，将原型部分与罗施的范畴化理论、卡尼曼的系统一思维联系起来，说明其如何借助连贯语义单元降低理解成本；几何层面，提出“几何有效性三元框架”，用于阐释原型如何验证数据流形。文章进一步倡议构建复合解释流程，将基于相似性的直观推理与严格的特征级审计结合。

Result: 本文提出了将完整样本示例及原型部分作为表格数据解释补充的概念框架，并指出原型部分可通过有界特征子空间表达可理解的概念。文章属于理论论证与研究议程梳理，摘要未报告实证实验或定量结果。

Conclusion: 要提升表格数据可解释人工智能的可理解性，不应只依赖孤立特征归因，而应结合原型所提供的整体相似性解释与特征级审计。未来研究还需解决基于原型方法面临的开放挑战，并探索相应方案。

Abstract: Explainable AI (XAI) is frequently applied to high-stakes tabular data, yet the field relies heavily on feature attribution methods. While these approaches offer technical transparency, evaluating dozens of isolated features demands excessive cognitive effort from non-expert users. In this paper, we argue that prototypes remain overlooked and underutilized, and should be integrated as a complementary, intuitive gateway to auditing complex machine learning models. Although entire exemplars function as an intuitive baseline for prototype-based explanations, we highlight prototypical parts as particularly valuable for tabular data. By grouping related features into bounded subspaces, prototypical parts technically embody human-understandable concepts, replacing isolated features with cohesive semantic units. We support this integration through two primary lenses. Cognitively, these parts align with Rosch's categorization theory and Kahneman's System 1 thinking to naturally reduce cognitive load for humans. Then, we introduce a Geometric Validity Triad to conceptualize why prototypes validate the data manifold - a critical dimension often neglected by existing methods. We contend that making tabular XAI genuinely comprehensible requires shifting toward composite explanation pipelines that pair intuitive similarity-based reasoning with rigorous feature-level auditing. The paper concludes by outlining open challenges of prototype-based methods and discussing potential solutions to guide future research.

</details>


### [235] [What Survival Benchmarks Don't Tell You: Impact of Model Selection and Dataset Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24837)
*Sandra Dening,Emre Kavak,Christian Wachinger,George H. Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究在四十九个多样化数据集上比较十种生存分析方法，发现评估指标、模型选择方式和数据集特征会显著影响方法排名与结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生存分析基准往往过度依赖一致性指数进行评估和模型选择，且对深度学习方法及困难数据情形覆盖不足，可能导致比较结论片面。研究旨在更全面、稳健地评估不同方法，并厘清评估设计对结论的影响。

Method: 在四十九个数据集上开展大规模、中立的基准比较，涵盖十种经典方法、树模型和深度学习方法。将模型选择依据作为独立实验变量，分别按一致性指数、积分布里尔分数或针对删失数据调整的平均绝对误差进行优化，并采用贝叶斯混合效应模型分析方法表现及数据集特征的影响。评估指标还包括概率准确性、校准表现和扩展校准误差。

Result: 评估设计会显著左右比较结论。对复杂的深度学习和树模型而言，依据积分布里尔分数选择超参数，可明显提升概率预测准确性与校准表现，同时几乎不损害排序判别能力。方法表现还受数据集特征根本性制约：深度学习方法在样本量较大时扩展性突出，但在特征维度较高时表现明显下降。此外，较重的删失会人为抬高绝对校准类指标，使检验结果显得过于保守。

Conclusion: 单一汇总分数不足以全面概括生存分析方法的表现，且可能掩盖指标取舍、数据集差异及删失机制带来的影响。研究建议透明报告评估设置，并结合多种指标和数据特征解读结果。

Abstract: Despite the proliferation of survival analysis methods, current benchmarks often operate under narrow conditions: they heavily rely on the concordance index (C-index) for evaluation and model selection, and frequently either exclude deep learning (DL) methods or underrepresent challenging dataset regimes. To address these limitations, we conduct a large-scale, neutral benchmark comparing 10 classical, tree-based, and DL methods across 49 diverse datasets. Crucially, we treat the model selection criterion (optimizing for C-index, Integrated Brier Score (IBS), or Mean Absolute Error (MAE-PO)) as an independent experimental variable. Using Bayesian mixed-effects modeling, we demonstrate that evaluation design heavily dictates conclusions. For complex DL and tree-based architectures, selecting hyperparameters to optimize for IBS instead of C-index yields substantial improvements in probabilistic accuracy and calibration at a negligible cost to discriminative ranking. Furthermore, method performance (C-index, IBS, MAE-PO, and X-CAL) is fundamentally governed by dataset characteristics. While DL models scale exceptionally well with larger sample sizes, they suffer notable performance drops in high-dimensional feature spaces. Additionally, we expose a structural vulnerability where heavy censoring artificially inflates absolute calibration metrics, creating overly conservative tests. Ultimately, our findings challenge the utility of global single-number performance summaries and advocate for survival analysis results that transparently report the evaluation setup. Our code is available at: https://github.com/sdening/surv-bench-study

</details>


### [236] [Measuring and Improving Transfer of Neural Surrogates for Vehicle Aerodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24843)
*Favour Nerrise,Julian Busch,Gurpreet Singh Hora,Martin Sipka,Danielle C. Maddix,Georgia Olympia Brikis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文系统评估预训练车辆气动神经代理模型的迁移能力，并提出几何与梯度对齐的源数据重放方法，在维持目标域精度的同时显著降低源域精度损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有车辆外流场神经代理模型难以直接泛化到新的车辆类别，通常需要为每种新设计耗费大量数值模拟数据重新训练。预训练模型理应能够凭少量目标域模拟实现适配，并保留在原训练域上的准确性，但此前缺少对这种迁移能力的系统评估。

Method: 研究对五种基于车辆气动数据预训练的代理模型进行大规模、受控基准测试，使用四个独立目标数据集；这些数据集在几何形状、数值求解器、湍流模型和流动条件上均与源域不同。研究进一步提出几何与梯度对齐的源数据重放方法：在训练中保留与目标域几何相似的源域模拟数据，并仅在其梯度更新方向与目标域一致时应用更新。作者还给出风险界限，以区分几何覆盖差异与模拟物理差异，并证明足够小的重放更新步长会降低目标训练目标。

Result: 微调在不到一半的比较中优于从头训练，而且会降低模型在源域上的准确性。所提方法在八十种设置中的七十七种降低了微调造成的源域误差，同时保持与微调相当的目标域精度。

Conclusion: 预训练并不必然带来可靠的跨车辆类别迁移：单纯微调可能既无目标域优势，又损害源域性能。几何与梯度对齐的源数据重放能更好地兼顾目标域适配和源域保留，为车辆气动代理模型的迁移训练提供了有理论依据的方法。

Abstract: Neural surrogates for external vehicle aerodynamics are fast alternatives to physics-based computational fluid dynamics (CFD) simulations. Their foundational limitation is a lack of zero-shot generalization across vehicle classes, which results in an expensive, custom training campaign for each new vehicle design. Similar to pretrained models in other fields, a pretrained surrogate should adapt to a new vehicle class using only a few simulations and stay accurate on its pretraining domain. No prior study has systematically tested this transfer behavior. We conduct a large-scale, controlled benchmarking study on five DrivAerML-pretrained surrogates across four independent target datasets. These datasets differ from the source in geometry, CFD solver, turbulence model, and flow conditions. We find that fine-tuning these surrogates outperforms training from scratch in fewer than half of the comparisons and lowers the accuracy of the surrogate on its source dataset. We introduce GeoReplay, a geometry- and gradient-aligned source replay that keeps source simulations resembling the target in training and applies their updates only when they agree with the target gradient. A risk bound separates geometric coverage from differences in the simulated physics, and we prove that every sufficiently small GeoReplay step decreases the target training objective. GeoReplay lowers the source error of fine-tuning in 77 of 80 settings and matches its target accuracy.

</details>


### [237] [Atomic MathReasoner: Instance-Level Composition of Atomic Reasoning Skills for Mathematical LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24846)
*Jiashu Li,Tianyu Gao,Qinglong Liu,Jinping Wang,Ye Wei,Zhiqiang Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出按题目动态组合数学原子能力的方法，提升大语言模型的数学与逻辑推理表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法往往把数学推理视为单一整体能力，但不同题目需要不同技能。尽管已有研究尝试拆分并专门训练原子能力，如何针对具体题目有效组合这些能力仍未充分解决。

Method: 先训练一组针对不同能力的低秩适配专家，再在验证集子集上进行无梯度优化，发现多种低损失能力组合原型。研究通过参考解的逐词负对数似然评估原型偏好，并利用置信度间隔过滤不明确的分配结果，将伪标签蒸馏至轻量级路由器。推理时，路由器为每道题选择合适的组合原型，避免在线优化开销。

Result: 在原子数学基准的通用及数学专用大语言模型上，方法均带来稳定提升。以七十亿参数的数学指令模型为例，一级平均领域准确率由百分之六十四点四升至百分之六十八点一，二级由百分之五十九点三升至百分之六十三点七；平均逻辑推理表现由百分之三十九点四升至百分之四十六点八。

Conclusion: 针对题目动态组合专门化的原子推理能力，能够有效增强大语言模型的数学推理能力；通过预先学习组合原型并使用轻量路由器选择，也可避免推理阶段进行昂贵的在线优化。

Abstract: Most existing approaches enhance mathematical reasoning in large language models (LLMs) as a monolithic capability, despite the fact that different mathematical problems require different reasoning skills. Recent work has explored a more fine-grained perspective by decomposing mathematical reasoning into atomic skills and developing specialized modules for individual capabilities. However, how to effectively utilize these atomic abilities remains underexplored. To address this challenge, we propose Atomic MathReasoner (AMR), an instance-level framework for composing atomic reasoning skills. AMR first trains a library of capability-specific LoRA experts and performs gradient-free optimization over held-out validation subsets to discover a diverse set of low-loss composition prototypes. It evaluates the preference of each prototype using the token-level negative log-likelihood of reference solutions, filters ambiguous assignments through a confidence margin, and distills the resulting pseudo-labels into a lightweight router. During inference, the router selects an appropriate composition prototype for each problem, enabling problem-specific utilization of atomic skills without costly online optimization. Experiments on the AtomicMath benchmark across general and mathematics-specialized LLMs demonstrate consistent improvements. On Qwen2.5-Math-Instruct-7B, AMR increases average field accuracy from 64.4% to 68.1% at Level 1 and from 59.3% to 63.7% at Level 2, while improving average logical reasoning performance from 39.4% to 46.8%. These results demonstrate that effectively composing specialized atomic skills provides a promising approach to strengthening mathematical reasoning in LLMs.

</details>


### [238] [TangentPDE: Learning Local Operator Responses with Sparse Finite-Difference Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24873)
*Xinjian Xie,Ruxuan Chen,Jiashu Li,Tianyu Gao,Ye Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该方法利用稀疏成对输入上的有限差分响应，在拟合状态预测的同时学习算子对局部扰动的变化；在多种方程系统和模型设置中，多数情况下改善了状态误差与滚动误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经偏微分方程算子通常通过重建状态进行优化，虽能拟合观测输入处的算子输出，却未必能准确描述输入受到局部扰动时的响应。要更真实地近似算子行为，需要同时学习状态值及其沿局部扰动方向的变化。

Method: 提出一种即插即用的训练框架：从参考求解器在稀疏成对输入上的状态输出构造有限步响应，以有限差分近似方向导数，并联合约束状态保真度、响应方向一致性和响应幅值校准。该方法不需要显式导数标签、线性化或伴随方程求解，也不需要完整雅可比矩阵；还引入选择性执行的适应机制，以加强有限差分监督下的局部算子表征。

Result: 在四类偏微分方程系统和二十种任务—骨干模型设置中，该方法在十八种设置下降低了三随机种子平均状态误差，并在十八种设置下降低了相应的二十步滚动误差。有限步响应本身的提升在扩散反应任务上最明显，在其他任务中则取决于任务和骨干模型。预算匹配及对应关系扰动对照实验用于区分关系监督带来的收益与额外状态观测或数据量增加带来的收益。

Conclusion: 仅依赖成对状态输出构造的有限差分监督，可以在无需导数接口的情况下，使神经算子同时学习状态预测和局部变化特征。整体结果表明，该框架能在多种模型设置中改善预测误差，但响应能力的提升并非普遍一致，仍受具体任务与模型结构影响。

Abstract: Neural PDE operators are often optimized through state reconstruction, which largely constrains operator values at observed inputs but does not fully characterize their sensitivity to local perturbations. A more faithful approximation of operator behavior requires not only accurate state predictions, but also correct variations along local perturbation directions. We present TangentPDE, which uses finite differences to approximate directional derivatives and constructs task-relevant finite-step responses from the state outputs of a reference solver at sparse paired inputs. Its objective jointly constrains state fidelity, response-direction alignment, and response-magnitude calibration, thereby learning both operator values and their local variations. Because the response labels are constructed solely from state outputs at paired inputs, the method requires neither explicit derivative labels, linearized or adjoint PDE solves, nor full Jacobians, and remains applicable to nonlinear solvers without derivative interfaces. Building on this, we additionally propose a selectively executed adaptation mechanism to strengthen local operator representations under finite-difference supervision. Budget-matched and correspondence-perturbed controls isolate the effect of relational supervision from improvements attributable to additional state observations or increased data volume. Designed as a plug-and-play training framework, TangentPDE integrates with diverse neural-operator backbones. Across four PDE systems and 20 task-backbone settings, TangentPDE lowers the three-seed mean state error in 18 settings and the corresponding Rollout@20 error in 18, while intrinsic finite-step response gains are strongest on DiffReact and remain task- and backbone-dependent elsewhere.

</details>


### [239] [Monoculture Robust Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24881)
*Lee Cohen,Jon Kleinberg,Omer Reingold*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出“单一数据集多模型稳健性”，研究如何仅用一份训练数据生成多个准确模型，并降低它们同时犯错的风险；可重复性可带来指数级改善，但最坏情况下仍存在对数级样本量代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算法单一化可能使共享训练数据或其他组件的模型在相同输入上同时失败。论文关注：能否只使用一份数据，生成多个各自准确的假设，并使它们同时出错的概率接近使用多份独立数据训练的情形，从而降低共同失效风险。

Method: 论文定义单一数据集多模型稳健性，以多个假设在同一点上全部误分类的联合错误概率作为衡量指标。其核心方法是将可重复性与该稳健性联系起来：若基础学习器具有参数为ρ的可重复性，就在同一数据样本上独立运行该学习器多次，利用各次运行的随机性降低联合错误。

Result: 论文证明，对于具有ρ可重复性的基础学习器，在同一份样本上独立运行k次，可使个体意义下的联合错误随k呈指数级下降；下降幅度由基础学习器在该点的单次错误率和ρ共同决定。另一方面，最坏情况下，若要将联合错误降至γ，样本量可能需要乘以至少与log(1/γ)同阶的因子。

Conclusion: 可重复性不仅有助于保证学习结果稳定，也能用来缓解多个模型的共同失效。重复利用同一数据并独立运行可有效提升多模型稳健性，但这一提升存在最坏情形下的样本复杂度下界。

Abstract: We introduce a notion of monoculture robustness in Probably Approximately Correct (PAC) learning, as a way to mitigate the problem of algorithmic monoculture (Kleinberg and Raghavan, 2021): the tendency of models sharing the same components (e.g., training data) to fail on the same inputs. Specifically, we ask whether a learner with access to a single dataset can output $k$ individually accurate hypotheses whose joint failure probability is comparable to training on $k$ independent datasets. We say that such a learning procedure achieves monoculture robustness, and we quantify this by bounding the conjunctive error that all $k$ hypotheses misclassify points in an individual sense. We design methods for achieving this type of guarantee by drawing a surprising connection between replicability (Impagliazzo et al., 2022) and monoculture robustness. Specifically, we show that if the base learner is $ρ$-replicable, then running it independently $k$ times on the same sample yields individual monoculture error that shrinks exponentially with $k$, controlled by the single-run error of the base learner at the point and $ρ$. We complement these positive results with a lower bound: for individual monoculture robustness, we prove a worst-case lower bound showing that, for learners, driving the individual conjunctive error down to $γ$ can require a sample-size multiplier of $t = Ω(\log(1/γ))$.

</details>


### [240] [Cap-and-Track: Optimizing LLM Inference Request Dispatch Across Shifting Capacity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24891)
*Benjamin Novotny,Doug Szeto,Chang Wang,Shicheng Chen,George Zhihua Yuan,Bob Kamma,Michael Ge,John Xin Zhou,Andrea Olgiati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出“容量上限与跟踪”调度方案，利用后端拒绝信号动态适配推理容量，在降低算力需求的同时维持接近最优的吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定调度速率会随着服务集群规模、请求长度及共享算力池容量变化而失准。生成内容长度在请求接入时未知，过量派发会造成排队和资源争用；但保守限速又可能浪费算力，因此需要一种无需预测输出长度或持续重调全局速率的动态调度机制。
