Method: 方案由“容量上限”和“跟踪调度”组成：在饱和资源处进行快速准入检查，超过当前容量的请求立即被拒绝，避免长时间等待；超额及被拒绝的请求放入持久化优先级队列，之后可重试。调度器仅依据拒绝信号逐步提高派发量，并在触及容量上限时回退，从而跟踪当前可用容量。系统采用有状态调度，并让推理引擎内部队列仅保留足以形成批次的工作。

Result: 在同流量并行生产对照实验中，该方案为分类器节省了百分之四十三的高端图形处理器，相比原有排队式固定速率方案；代价是高峰期低优先级任务等待更久。在稳定容量的真实引擎回放中，其有效吞吐量与高于实测容量的固定速率方案相差不到百分之一，但拒绝请求占比约为后者的四分之一。该流量整形系统每日处理超过三亿次在线请求及数百太字节媒体数据；一次回填期间，最繁忙的二十四小时处理了十一亿次请求。

Conclusion: 仅凭容量拒绝信号即可实现有效的动态派发，无需预测生成长度或维护容易过时的全局速率。该设计能显著减少算力需求并保持高吞吐，但会将部分等待转移至低优先级队列。因此，部署时需结合低优先级任务可接受的等待时间配置资源，并合理安排引擎内外的排队位置。

Abstract: A fixed dispatch rate for LLM inference becomes stale as serving fleets resize and request sizes change. Generative output length is unknown at admission, and shared provider pools can lose capacity. We present Cap-and-Track, a design for asynchronous inference across provider capacity and internal GPUs. Meta's traffic shaper implements Cap-and-Track for post and live-video understanding. Refusals signal the backend's current admission ceiling. Durable priority queues hold excess work outside execution, including refused requests for retry. Cap is a fail-fast admission check at the saturated resource that refuses work before a long wait. Using only those refusals, the Track dispatcher probes upward and backs off. It follows the ceiling without output-length prediction or a fleet-level rate to retune. In a concurrent same-traffic production A/B, the shaper ran per-replica Cap plus Track. It served a classifier on 43% fewer H100s than its queued, fixed-rate precursor, though lower-priority work waited longer at backfill peaks. In steady-capacity real-engine replay, Track ran within 1% of the goodput of a fixed rate tuned above measured capacity, with about a quarter of that rate's refusal share. The shaper carries more than 300 million online requests and hundreds of terabytes of media a day. During a backfill, it handled 1.1 billion requests in its busiest 24 hours. Lessons include keeping queues inside inference engines just long enough to fill batches, steering dispatch by capacity refusals alone, keeping dispatch stateful, and sizing fleets by how long low-priority work can wait.

</details>


### [241] [On Evaluating User Simulators for Interactive Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24892)
*Hengguang Zhou,I-Hung Hsu,Li Chen,Priyanshi Shrivastava,Anastasios Angelopoulos,Wei-Lin Chiang,Ion Stoica,Cho-Jui Hsieh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出，现有用户模拟器评估指标可能漏判重要行为错误，甚至偏好有缺陷的模拟用户；作者提出结合效用与多层次真实度的新评估套件，并验证其能识别全部十种人为注入的错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户模拟器可扩展地评估交互式智能体，但模拟器评估框架是否能可靠发现模拟行为中的实质性失败，仍不清楚。基准得分因而难以解释，也无法充分说明模拟器的实际质量。

Method: 作者通过元评估检验指标本身：对模拟交互记录进行人为扰动，注入目标改变、约束遗漏、过早停止、接受有缺陷成果等十种错误，并测试既有指标能否惩罚这些错误。在此基础上，提出结合模拟器评估智能体的效用与真实用户行为保真度的评估套件；保真度分别从群体层面和单条交互轨迹层面衡量。

Result: 三项既有研究中的评估方法仅能检测十种扰动中的五至六种，合并评分甚至会偏好被指示接受有缺陷成果的模拟用户。新套件按相同检测标准识别了全部十种扰动。作者还用六种模拟器评估了三百五十一段真实多轮会话，涉及二十三种语言、三百四十八名用户，以及应用、游戏、设计素材和自动化工作流等成果。结果显示，群体层面的指标可能漏掉模拟用户追错目标、忽略约束或偏离用户个性等问题。

Conclusion: 评估用户模拟器不能只依赖总体得分或群体行为统计，还应同时考察其对真实用户目标、约束和个性的逐条保留情况。该研究提出的多层次评估套件能更全面地揭示模拟行为缺陷，为解释基准表现和比较模拟器质量提供了更可靠的依据。

Abstract: User simulators offer a scalable way to evaluate interactive agents, but how to assess their quality remains underexplored. In particular, it is unclear whether existing user simulator evaluation frameworks reliably detect meaningful failures in simulated user behavior. This uncertainty makes it difficult to interpret simulator evaluation. To understand what a high benchmark score tells us about simulator quality, we (meta-)evaluate the metrics themselves. To instantiate this meta-evaluation, we deliberately perturb simulated replays to induce failures such as altered goals, omitted constraints, premature stopping, and acceptance of broken work, then test whether the metrics penalize these failures. Evaluation methods from three prior studies detect only 5-6 of the 10 perturbations; their combined scores even favor populations instructed to accept broken work over unmodified controls. Motivated by these gaps, we propose a new evaluation suite that combines utility of user simulators for evaluating agents and two measures of fidelity to real users: population-level fidelity assesses collective interaction patterns, while trace-level fidelity assesses the preservation of individual users' goals, constraints, and persona. Under the same detection criterion, our evaluation suite detects all 10 perturbations. Evaluating six user simulators on 351 real multi-turn sessions in which 348 distinct users across 23 languages and agents work together to create and refine artifacts such as full-stack web apps, games, design assets, and automated workflows, we find that population-level measures can miss when a simulated user pursues the wrong goal, ignores a constraint, or behaves out of character, highlighting the need to assess fidelity at comprehensive granularities.

</details>


### [242] [Equivariant Modeling with Polynomial Features via Minimal Homogeneous Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24896)
*Michael Liaofan Liu,Joe Kileel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向有限维半单实群表示之间等变函数的通用学习框架，并通过自动计算特征与拟合模型的软件工具，支持科学建模中的端到端预测和网络层构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同应用领域已发展出多种等变函数的构造方法，但缺少统一的建模与学习框架。论文旨在整合这些方法，同时利用对称性约束提升模型效率，减少可训练参数，并适用于科学建模任务。

Method: 模型将等变多项式特征作线性组合，组合系数则是关于不变量多项式特征的可学习函数。特征选取为不变量代数和协变量模的最小齐次生成元，并限制在指定次数以内。特征计算算法依赖输入、输出表示的显式等型分解，以及不可约表示张量积的显式克莱布施–戈登分解；作者还提供了自动计算特征并依据训练数据拟合模型的软件包。

Result: 科学建模数值实验展示了从特征计算到模型训练的完整流程。实验得到准确的预测结果，且所需可训练参数较少；该模型既可用于端到端预测，也可嵌入更大架构的网络层中。摘要还指出，其特征计算算法相较其他计算代数方法具有速度优势。

Conclusion: 该框架为多类应用中的等变函数学习提供了统一方案，并将代数特征构造与数据驱动拟合结合起来。实验表明，它能够以较少参数实现准确预测，具有作为独立模型或大型架构组成部分的应用潜力。

Abstract: We present a general framework for modeling and learning equivariant functions between finite dimensional semisimple real group representations, unifying constructions developed across many application areas. Our model is a linear combination of equivariant polynomial features whose coefficients are arbitrary learnable functions of invariant polynomial features. We take as features minimal homogeneous generators for the invariant algebra and the covariant module over the invariant algebra, up to prescribed degrees. We provide a Python package that automatically computes these features and fits the resulting models to training data. The algorithm for feature computation requires explicit isotypic decompositions of the input and output representations, along with explicit Clebsch-Gordan decompositions of the tensor product of irreducible representations, and achieves speedups over alternative methods in computational algebra. Numerical experiments in scientific modeling demonstrate the full learning pipeline and yield accurate predictors with few trainable parameters, using the model both for end-to-end prediction and within layers of larger architectures.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [243] [PGPO: Prototype-Guided Progressive Obfuscation for Privacy-Preserving LLM-Enhanced Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23236)
*Shengxiang Lin,Jiajie Su,Pengyang Zhou,Xiang Chen,Xiaolin Zheng,Feng Tian,Chaochao Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种兼顾隐私与推荐效用的语义嵌入保护方法：通过有引导的渐进式混淆削弱文本属性反演，同时尽量保留物品之间的语义关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型增强的推荐系统会将物品侧信息编码为语义嵌入，但攻击者可能通过反演嵌入恢复敏感文本属性。现有防御依赖可信数据收集或对输入词元进行局部扰动，可能破坏物品间的语义结构，进而损害推荐效果。因此，论文将核心目标设定为：改变嵌入以保护隐私，同时保留物品之间的相对关系。

Method: 论文提出原型引导的渐进式混淆方法。该方法为影响力较大的原型词学习感知语义拓扑的混淆锚点，再沿静态语义图传播混淆，以维护物品间的关系结构。训练时采用组相对策略优化，并结合个体差异性、结构一致性和语义约束三类奖励，引导隐私保护与推荐效用之间的平衡。

Result: 在两个基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法能够有效抵御语义嵌入反演攻击，同时使推荐性能接近其上限水平。摘要未提供具体指标或与各基线方法的数值对比。

Conclusion: 研究表明，保护语义嵌入不必以破坏物品关系结构为代价。以关系保真为目标的渐进式混淆，有望同时增强文本属性隐私并维持推荐效用。

Abstract: LLM-enhanced recommender systems encode item side information into semantic embeddings, but these embeddings may leak sensitive textual attributes through inversion attacks. Existing input-side defenses rely on trusted collection or local token perturbation, often disrupting the inter-item semantic topology needed for recommendation. To address it, we introduce relational fidelity as the key objective, requiring semantic shift for privacy while preserving relative item relations for utility. Based on this principle, we propose prototype-guided progressive obfuscation (PGPO), which learns topology-aware obfuscation anchors for high-influence prototype words and propagates them through a static semantic graph. PGPO is optimized with GRPO using individual dissimilarity, structural consistency, and semantic constraint rewards. Experiments on two benchmarks show strong inversion defense with near-upper-bound recommendation performance. The code is available at https://github.com/Shengxiang-Lin/PGPO.

</details>


### [244] [From Docket Minutes to Case Trajectories: Building Analysis-Ready Criminal Court Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23537)
*Erfan Hosseini Sereshgi,Kory Rosen,Jaya Dhariwal,Nicholas Mattei,Aron Culotta*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍一套面向新奥尔良刑事法院数据的整合与可视化系统，帮助专业人士和社区成员分析案件趋势、政策影响及程序异常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刑事法院记录往往零散、嘈杂且分散在不同来源，导致法律专家和社区成员难以获取并分析数据，也不利于公众监督。研究旨在提升地方司法数据的可访问性与可理解性。

Method: 系统收集并整合不同来源的法院数据，构建案件发展轨迹，并提供直观的仪表板可视化，支持分析人口特征、时间趋势和具体案件事件。设计过程吸收社区反馈，并通过新奥尔良近期短期趋势与政策影响的探索性分析进行展示。

Result: 系统展示了分析司法趋势、政策变化、异常现象和程序不规范情况的潜力。小规模形成性研究中的社区参与者反馈表明，该系统可能有助于提升公众获取和解读本地法院数据的能力。

Conclusion: 研究提出一种结合数据整合、案件轨迹构建和社区导向可视化的司法数据分析方案，为地方司法监督提供了可行工具。现有证据初步支持其效用，但结论仍需通过更大规模、更多样化的用户研究进一步验证。

Abstract: Criminal court records are often fragmented, noisy, and siloed, making it challenging for both legal experts and community stakeholders to access and analyze court data to identify trends and provide public oversight. In this paper, we introduce a system for data ingestion, harmonization, and dashboard visualization designed for New Orleans criminal court proceedings data, aimed at both expert and lay audiences. Our system collects and harmonizes disparate data, generates case trajectories, and provides intuitive visualization techniques based on community feedback to analyze demographics, temporal trends, and specific case events. We demonstrate the system through an exploratory analysis of recent short-term trends and policy impacts within New Orleans, illustrating how it can support the investigation of anomalies, policy changes, and procedural irregularities. We also present feedback from a small formative study of community stakeholders who have helped co-design and use the system, suggesting that it may improve access to and interpretation of local court data.

</details>


### [245] [CONCORD-Rec: Catalog-Conditioned Modality Utility for Semantic-ID Diffusion Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23710)
*Haopeng Jin,Bingkang Shi,Tao Yu,Yifei Ma,Hao Liu,Hongzhu Yi,Jinwen Luo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种可靠性条件化的离散扩散推荐方法，根据各模态对缩小候选商品范围的实际贡献来分配其影响；在多种受控数据条件下提升了推荐质量，并可显著降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态生成推荐依赖离散语义标识符。现有方法往往将历史模态对待选词元预测的影响，误当作其确实能减少整个商品目录歧义的证据。当模态缺失、质量下降或语义冲突时，统一融合及不受约束的词元门控可能在逐步去噪过程中放大误导信息。论文因此希望衡量模态对候选商品判别的真实效用，并可靠地控制其影响。

Method: 提出协和推荐模型：在固定分词器及部分掩码的标识符状态下，利用交叉折叠教师模型组合，在精确的可行商品候选集合上计算预测，并在每个未解析位置、目标编码分区内聚合商品后验概率。模型不直接蒸馏原始词元概率，而以反事实损失差作为监督，训练受模态可用性约束的逐词元门控。门控围绕行为去噪器分配有界的模态残差，同时保留精确的纯行为回退通道。论文还给出固定候选集合下、门控扰动对排序稳定性的理论界限。

Result: 在视听及图像—文本推荐基准上，研究考察了模态缺失、信号退化和语义冲突等情形。在二十组来源兼容的协议—数据集实验中，归一化折损累计增益提升百分之四；在冲突率匹配为百分之三十时，该指标提升百分之十四点六三。完整方法与均匀融合、步数为十六的方案对比显示，质量提升百分之二点零零至百分之三点五八，同时百分之九十五位延迟最多降低百分之八十六点八九。

Conclusion: 多模态信息是否值得参与推荐，应依据其对候选商品区分能力的反事实贡献，而非仅依据其对词元预测的影响。通过效用监督、受约束门控和行为回退机制，协和推荐模型能够在模态不可靠或相互冲突时更稳健地融合信息，并兼顾推荐质量与推理效率。

Abstract: Multimodal generative recommendation with discrete semantic identifiers (SIDs) requires distinguishing historical modalities that merely alter token predictions from those that genuinely reduce catalog-level ambiguity. When audiovisual, visual, or textual streams are missing, degraded, or semantically conflicting, uniform fusion and unconstrained token gates can propagate misleading content across iterative denoising steps. We propose CONCORD-Rec, a reliability-conditioned discrete diffusion recommender supervised by counterfactual catalog-partition utility. Given a fixed tokenizer and a partially masked SID state, CONCORD-Rec evaluates out-of-fold teacher coalitions over the exact feasible catalog support and aggregates item posteriors across the target code partition at each unresolved position. Rather than distilling raw token probabilities directly, the resulting counterfactual loss differences supervise an availability-masked tokenwise gate that allocates bounded modality residuals around a behavioral denoiser while retaining an exact behavioral fallback slot. We establish theoretical fixed-support ranking stability bounds under gate perturbations and evaluate position-level and code-conditional allocation across audiovisual and image-text recommendation benchmarks under controlled missingness, signal degradation, and semantic conflict. Across 20 source-compatible protocol-dataset rows, CONCORD-Rec improves NDCG by 4%; under matched 30% conflict it improves NDCG by 14.63%. The full-vs-uniform-T=16 sweep improves quality by 2.00-3.58% while reducing p95 latency by up to 86.89%.

</details>


### [246] [MARS Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24006)
*Tong Liu,Chaoqun Hou,Longxiang Xiong,Junhong Lou,Jiaqi Zheng,Qixin Li,Zixin Zhao,Yong Duan,Xiannian Hu,Fengze Jiang,Yu Zhu,Cheng Guo,Xinyuan Qi,Zi Zhuang,Ping Liu,Lv Shao,Zongwei Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出多阶段智能体推荐系统，将个性化从单个模型扩展到整条推荐链路的配置与调度。五个淘宝场景的两周线上测试显示，核心指标均有提升，最大提升为浏览到购买转化率百分之五点二二、商品交易总额百分之七点三四，平均服务延迟还降低了十二毫秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐系统通常依次经过召回、预排序、排序和重排序，将海量候选缩减为个性化列表。虽然各阶段的模型日益复杂，但渠道启用、候选配额、模型选择和分数融合等链路级决策，仍主要依赖工程师针对场景进行调参，且缺少对单个用户的适配。论文旨在将个性化从阶段级预测扩展到整条推荐链路的编排。

Method: 系统使用基于微调小型语言模型的近线智能体，为每位用户生成受约束的七维配置，涉及链路模式、召回配额、模型选择、融合权重和重排序设置。配置经过校验并缓存后异步供推荐链路读取，因此线上级联无需等待语言模型推理；同时通过格式校验、回退机制和紧急关闭开关保障服务安全。策略训练分三步进行：先以置信度感知的监督微调学习生成符合格式的配置；再通过带动作掩码和后验加权的偏好优化学习配置偏好；最后在沙盒回放中使用冻结的奖励模型进行组相对策略优化，避免将探索性动作暴露给线上流量。

Result: 在五个淘宝场景开展的为期两周的对照测试覆盖每日超过一千万用户，所有关键指标均取得正向提升。效果最佳的场景中，浏览到购买转化率提升百分之五点二二，商品交易总额提升百分之七点三四；两个点击入口的点击率也均有提升。约百分之二十的请求采用轻量模式，部署后的平均服务延迟比现网方案低十二毫秒。

Conclusion: 研究表明，利用智能体按用户动态配置推荐链路，有望在改善业务指标的同时降低服务延迟。该方案通过受约束的动作空间、异步配置和沙盒训练兼顾个性化与线上安全。不过，摘要未提供各场景的详细统计检验、消融实验或长期效果，因此其收益的稳定性及对其他业务场景的泛化能力仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Modern industrial recommender systems use multi-stage cascades--retrieval, pre-ranking, ranking, and re-ranking--to narrow millions of candidates to a personalized list. While each stage deploys sophisticated models, pipeline-level decisions on channel activation, quotas, model selection, and score fusion remain engineer-tuned and user-agnostic within each scenario.
  This report presents MARS (Multi-stage Agentic Recommendation System), extending personalization from individual-stage predictions to full pipeline orchestration. A near-line agent built on fine-tuned Qwen3-0.6B generates per-user configurations--a seven-dimensional action covering pipeline mode, retrieval quotas, model selection, fusion weights, and reranker settings--from a constrained action space. Configurations are validated, cached, and consumed asynchronously; the cascade never waits for LLM inference. Schema checks, fallbacks, and a kill switch ensure serving safety.
  The policy is trained in three stages: Confidence-aware SFT distills schema-valid generation from a teacher model; Format-Anchored Preference Optimization (FAPO) learns action preferences via ActionMask with posterior weighting; Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) refines the policy through Sandbox replay with a frozen reward model, without exposing exploratory actions to live traffic.
  Two-week A/B tests across five Taobao scenarios with over ten million daily users show positive gains on all key metrics. The largest reach +5.22% V2P and +7.34% GMV, with positive CTR lifts on both click surfaces. About 20% of requests use a lightweight Minimal mode, and deployment shows 12 ms lower mean serving latency versus production.

</details>


### [247] [X-split Argus: Learning Reusable History Tokens for Efficient CTR Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24283)
*Alexander Ploshkin,Nikita Stepanov,Alexey Pismenny,Viacheslav Ovchinnikov,Nikolay Savushkin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向广告排序的历史压缩框架，在控制线上计算与时延开销的同时提升点击率预测效果，并带来显著的广告业务收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确预测点击率需要同时刻画用户长期偏好和近期意图，但对每次排序请求都处理庞大的行为历史，会增加线上计算成本和响应时延。因此，研究旨在压缩历史信息，同时尽量保留其对候选广告排序的价值。

Method: 框架端到端训练历史压缩器与线上排序器。压缩器将历史交互编码为一组紧凑标记，在离线计算后供多个候选广告复用；排序器通过交叉注意力结合这些标记与近期行为，为广告打分。训练时使用点击反馈的自回归损失，并通过蒸馏，使模型拟合一个基于未压缩历史、针对不同候选广告生成点击分数的教师模型。压缩标记数量和近期历史长度均可调整，以适应算力与时延预算。

Result: 离线评估中，相较生产基线，归一化熵相对降低百分之零点八一。大规模实时竞价系统部署后，线上推理成本比基线增加百分之十五。线上对照实验显示，反映广告主转化货币价值的业务指标相对提升百分之四点六五，点击量相对提升百分之二点九六。

Conclusion: 该方法通过离线复用的紧凑历史表示与近期行为建模，在有限的线上开销增幅下有效改善广告排序效果。结果表明，历史压缩与教师模型蒸馏能够兼顾推理效率和业务表现，且压缩规模可按部署预算灵活调整。

Abstract: Accurate click-through rate (CTR) prediction depends on user representations that capture both persistent preferences and recent intent, yet processing extensive behavioral histories for every ranking request is costly under production compute and latency constraints. We present X-split ARGUS, a framework for ad ranking whose history compressor and online ranker are trained end to end. The compressor encodes past interactions into a compact set of tokens that are computed offline and reused across candidates, while the online ranker scores ads through cross-attention to these tokens and representations of recent events. Training combines an autoregressive loss for click feedback with distillation of candidate-specific click logits from a larger teacher that scores each candidate using the uncompressed history. The number of compressed history tokens and the length of recent history can be adjusted to fit serving compute and latency budgets. Offline evaluation shows a 0.81% relative reduction in normalized entropy (NE) compared with the production baseline. Deployment in a large-scale real-time bidding system increases online inference cost by 15% relative to the production baseline. An online A/B test against this baseline yields relative gains of 4.65% in a business metric reflecting the monetary value of conversions generated for advertisers and 2.96% in clicks.

</details>


### [248] [Can Programs Replace LLM Judges? Executable Program Judges for Scalable and Efficient Relevance Labeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24849)
*Eugene Agichtein,Charles L. A. Clarke,Kaustubh Dhole,William Stern*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该方法让大语言模型按主题生成可读、可执行的相关性判定程序，以低成本、可复查的方式替代逐文档调用大模型；实验显示其判断与专业评审员较为一致，并能较准确地评估检索系统排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型评审需要对每篇文档分别调用昂贵模型，成本高、判断过程难以解释且不易复现。论文探讨能否让大语言模型根据评审指南为每个主题编写程序，从而规模化生成相关性判断。

Method: 执行式评审方法使用前沿大语言模型将评审指南转化为针对各主题的可执行程序。程序仅依赖词汇匹配、常见自然语言处理工具（如句法分析器）及候选文档池统计信息。扩展版本还可利用少量人工标注作为示例。程序生成后即可批量判定文档，无须逐篇调用大语言模型。

Result: 在七个文本检索会议数据集上的实验中，基础方法在二〇一九至二〇二一年的深度学习评测集上，与美国国家标准与技术研究院评审员的一致程度达到某前沿大语言模型评审的水平。加入少量主题级人工标注后，扩展方法在七个数据集上均优于使用相同标注量的主题分类器；与使用相同标注量的大语言模型评审相比，其一致性更高，或仅低约零点零五的克里彭多夫阿尔法。生成的标注对官方系统排名的肯德尔秩相关系数为零点八七至零点九四。每个主题编写程序的一次性成本约为零点一七至零点四九美元，之后无需额外判定费用。

Conclusion: 按主题生成程序能够将相关性评审从逐文档模型调用转变为一次生成、反复执行的流程。该方案兼具可解释、可复现、快速和成本较低等优点，可作为深度结果池标注及检索系统评估或排序改进的替代方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) judges have become a common way to create large-scale relevance judgments for retrieval evaluation. However, each judgment requires a potentially expensive call to a frontier model, and the judgments are hard to interpret and may not be reproducible. In this paper, we ask whether an LLM can write a readable program for each topic that replaces its per-document judgments. To address these issues, we propose ExecJudges, a method for generating fast, inspectable and scalable judges for automatic relevance labeling. Specifically, ExecJudges uses a frontier LLM to turn assessment guidelines into executable programs, which rely only on lexical matching, standard NLP libraries like parsers, and pool statistics. An ExecJudges-L variant also uses examples of human labels if available. We evaluate ExecJudges on seven TREC collections by comparing the produced labels with trained NIST assessors, frontier LLM judges, and recently proposed per-topic classifiers. Our results show that ExecJudges agrees with NIST assessors at the level of the Claude Sonnet 5.5 LLM judges on TREC DL 2019-2021. With a small number of human labels per topic, the ExecJudges-L variant agrees with NIST more than per-topic classifiers with the same labels on all seven collections, and either exceeds the agreement of LLM judges provided the same labels or is within about 0.05 of it in Krippendorff's alpha. The labels rank the official TREC DL runs with Kendall's tau of 0.87 to 0.94, and writing the programs incurs a one-time cost of $0.17 to $0.49 per topic, with no additional costs for grading. Our results suggest that ExecJudges offers a reproducible, inspectable, efficient and cost-effective alternative to LLM judges for labeling deep result pools for system evaluation or ranking improvements.

</details>
